# M2 Media Implementation Plan

> For agentic workers: executed subagent-driven. Each task runs in a fresh session. Two critical
> reviewers and an arbiter review each task.

**Goal (exit criterion, design §1):** `rawreel-cli info` and `dump` give byte-identical output through the CPU path and the GPU path; GPU unpack is bit-exact for the committed 37-format catalogue.

**Design (binding):** the approved M2 design: scope, decisions, APIs, threading handshake, tests,
task list. Its decisions log comes with it.

## Global Constraints
- **Platform scope (decision U-WIN):** v1.0 supports Windows only; M2 tests only on Windows. The code
  stays portable, because Linux support must follow soon: Linux code paths (POSIX sources,
  `#if defined(__linux__)` test cases, Linux presets and CMake branches) are written, reviewed and kept
  compiling, but not run. No WSL test runs, no lavapipe, no Linux ASan/TSan/UBSan, no xvfb.
- **Toolchains:** C++23. MSVC v145 (`vs2026`, `vs2026-asan`). Clang 19 with libstdc++ 14 in WSL
  Ubuntu 24.04: clang-format, and `ninja-clang` only for the checkpoint build (clang-tidy's build tree
  and the Linux sanity build); the `ninja-clang-asan` and `ninja-clang-tsan` presets stay defined but
  are not run. Warnings are errors for our code. Third-party sources compiled into a dvv target never
  fail the build on warnings. Third-party headers are always SYSTEM.
- **Per-task checks (U-PROC, binding).** Every task runs them once, on its final commit, in each
  repository it changed (`<repo>`). Nothing else runs per task: no `vs2026` Release, no
  `vs2026-asan`, no clang-tidy, no WSL build, no Linux sanity build. A failure is fixed in a new
  commit and the check repeated. `<GPU env>` is the line of "GPU tests" below.
  1. Windows Debug build and all tests, GPU tests on NVIDIA (`run_in_background: true` when long):
     `<GPU env>; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --preset vs2026 | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`.
     Expected: `100% tests passed`, no GPU test skipped, 0 validation messages.
  2. Concurrency repeats, only in a risky task that names them (in M2: Tasks 7a and 7b, ×20):
     `<GPU env>; Push-Location <repo>; ctest --preset vs2026 -R '<regex>' --repeat until-fail:<n> --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 5; Pop-Location`.
  3. clang-format on the files the task changed (WSL, no build). `<base>` is the repository's head
     before the task's first commit (record `git -C <repo> rev-parse HEAD` when the task starts):
     `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd <repo> && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <base> -- "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r clang-format --dry-run --Werror && echo FORMAT-OK'`.
     `git diff <base>` compares the working tree (run it after `git add -A`), so it works before and
     after the commit. In a repository the task creates, `git ls-files -z "*.h" "*.cpp"` replaces it.
     Expected `FORMAT-OK`; a reported file is fixed with `clang-format -i` in WSL.
  4. Privacy lint, commits mode:
     `cmake -DREPO=<repo> -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`.
     Expected `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.
- **Checkpoints (U-PROC):** Task 3, Task 5, Task 7b, Task 9, Task 13, Task 15 (final).
- **Checkpoint checks (U-PROC, U-PROC-tidy, binding).** A checkpoint task runs them after its own
  steps (its Per-task checks included), on its final commits, in every repository changed since the
  previous checkpoint (`<repo>`). `<cp-base>` is the repository's head at the previous checkpoint (the
  `m2` branch start for the first one): the controller records the heads in its ledger at each
  checkpoint and names them in the brief; a repository created since then uses `git ls-files` in
  place of the `git diff`. The two Windows runs go in one background call per repository (never two
  package tests at once); the WSL build may run in parallel.
  1. Windows Release:
     `<GPU env>; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --build --preset vs2026-release; ctest --preset vs2026-release --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`.
  2. Windows ASan, then the concurrency repeats of each risky task since the previous checkpoint
     under ASan:
     `<GPU env>; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|AddressSanitizer'; Pop-Location` and
     `<GPU env>; Push-Location <repo>; ctest --preset vs2026-asan -R '<regex>' --repeat until-fail:<n> --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 5; Pop-Location`.
     Expected: all passed, no AddressSanitizer report.
  3. clang-tidy on the C++ sources changed since `<cp-base>`, after one WSL `ninja-clang` configure +
     build with no tests (`run_in_background: true`). This build is also the Linux sanity build:
     `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc 'set -o pipefail && . _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd <repo> && cmake --preset ninja-clang >/dev/null && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 30 && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <cp-base> HEAD -- ":(glob)src/**/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/*.cpp" ":(glob)tools/*.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_win32.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_msw.cpp" | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 1 clang-tidy -p ../_build/<repo>/ninja-clang --quiet && echo TIDY-OK'`.
     Expected `TIDY-OK`. A build error in our code is a portability bug and is fixed; a toolchain or
     third-party failure is recorded in the plan's `## Changes during execution` section and
     reported, not fixed.
  A checkpoint failure is fixed (a `fix:` commit, then the Per-task checks and the failed checkpoint
  check again) before the next task starts. The final task's checkpoint covers everything: every
  repository of the milestone, clang-tidy on all sources (`git ls-files -z` with the same pathspecs
  replaces the `git diff`), and the ASan repeats of every risky task of the milestone.
- **Risk accepted (U-WIN):** Windows has no TSan and MSVC has no UBSan, so data races and undefined
  shifts are found only by review and Windows ASan until Linux returns.
- **Git and repositories:**
  - Branch `m2` in every existing repository (created from the `m1b` heads; M2 starts after M1b).
  - New repository dvv-media is created with `git init -b m2`, local only.
  - No remotes, no push. Commits use the noreply identity from bootstrap, with the trailer
    `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Privacy:** no machine paths, user names, host names or personal e-mails in committed files or
  messages. Run the privacy lint in commits mode before finishing a task.
- **Shells:**
  - Windows: the PowerShell tool only; git only from PowerShell, except read-only `git ls-files` and
    `git diff --name-only` inside the WSL format and tidy commands (decisions: M1b-plan, U-PROC).
  - Linux (clang-format and the checkpoint build only): `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && …'`
    from the workspace root. WSL has no passwordless sudo.
- **GPU tests:**
  - Exit 77 means skip; `DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1` turns a skip into a failure.
  - Every GPU run sets the variables **in the same tool call**:
    `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'`.
  - Zero validation messages.
- **Licence gate:** M1b allows `libpng-2.0`, `IJG`, `X11` and `Unicode-DFS-2016` for static
  linking (decisions D4 and D6), in addition to the M1a lists.
- **Long runs:** a tool call stops after 10 minutes, so long runs use `run_in_background`.
  The Windows runs and the WSL checkpoint build may run in parallel. The Per-task checks run once,
  on a task's final commit; the workspace workflow runs once per task group, at a checkpoint.
  Groups: G1 = Tasks 0–9 (Task 9's run), G2 = Tasks 10–13 (Task 13's run); G3 = Tasks 14–15 (Task
  15's run).
- **Spec §9.4** applies to every managed type.

## Conventions for every task

Conventions for every task below:
- Run commands from the workspace root, the folder that contains `rawreel-workspace`.
- Use the PowerShell tool on Windows. WSL runs only clang-format and the checkpoint build, as
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && <command>'`, also from the
  workspace root. No test runs in WSL (decision U-WIN).
- Run git from PowerShell only, except the read-only `git ls-files` and `git diff --name-only` of the
  format and tidy commands.
- Every commit ends with the trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Nothing is pushed.
- A tool call stops after 10 minutes. Bootstraps, workflow presets and the checkpoint build run with
  `run_in_background`. The Windows runs and the WSL checkpoint build may go in parallel.
- Inner loop (Windows, foreground): `<GPU env>; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --build --preset vs2026;
  ctest --preset vs2026 -E "\.package$" --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`, where `<GPU env>` is the
  line of Global Constraints.
- **Per-task checks** for `<repo>` (on the task's final commit; Global Constraints has the commands):
  vs2026 Debug build + all tests (package test included) with `<GPU env>`, the Debug concurrency
  repeats in a risky task that names them, clang-format on the changed files (`FORMAT-OK`), and the
  privacy lint. No Release, no ASan, no clang-tidy, no WSL build (decision U-PROC).
- **Checkpoint checks** (only in the tasks of the Checkpoints list; Global Constraints has the
  commands): vs2026 Release, the vs2026-asan workflow with the ASan repeats of the risky tasks since
  the last checkpoint, and clang-tidy on the files changed since the last checkpoint after one WSL
  `ninja-clang` configure + build with no tests (`TIDY-OK`; also the Linux sanity build). A compile
  error in that build that comes from our code is a portability bug and is fixed; a toolchain or
  third-party failure is recorded in the plan's `## Changes during execution` section and reported
  to the controller, not fixed.
- Privacy lint after every commit: `cmake -DREPO=<repo> -DMODE=commits
  "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`.
  Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.

---

### Task 0: Branches and device check

**Goal:** Start M2 on branch `m2` in every existing repository, taken from the finished `m1b` heads, and
confirm on the local NVIDIA device that the Vulkan features the M2 design needs
exist (push descriptors, storage images without a declared format on R8_UINT/R16_UINT). The result decides
the design's fallbacks R1 and R2.

**Files:** only the M2 plan file `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m2-media.md`
(Step 2b). The results go into its `## Changes during execution` section.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M1b complete. Its final task (13b) is committed. The six repositories `rawreel-workspace`,
  `dvv-base`, `dvv-registry`, `dvv-platform`, `dvv-gpu` and `rawreel` sit side by side in the workspace
  folder, each on `m1b` with a clean tree. The M1b build tree `_build/rawreel-workspace/vs2026` exists.
- Produces: branch `m2` in those six repositories, created from their `m1b` heads. Every later M2 commit
  goes onto `m2`; the new repository `dvv-media` (Task 1) starts with `git init -b m2`. Also produces the
  recorded device facts and the R1/R2 decision ("not needed" or "needed").
- Constraint for the whole plan: nothing is pushed, no GitHub repository is created, and no remote is
  added. Git runs only from PowerShell, except read-only `git ls-files` inside the WSL lint command
  (decisions: M1b-plan). All commands run from the workspace root (the
  folder that contains `rawreel-workspace`).

**Behaviour:**
- If a repository is not on `m1b`, has uncommitted changes, or does not exist, stop and report it to the
  controller. Do not create `m2` from anything else.
- R1 (design §11) is needed when any of these is missing on the NVIDIA device: `shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats`,
  `shaderStorageImageWriteWithoutFormat`, or (in `optimalTilingFeatures`) `FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_IMAGE_BIT`
  and `FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT_BIT` for both `FORMAT_R8_UINT` and `FORMAT_R16_UINT`.
  R2 is needed when push descriptors are missing (`pushDescriptor` false or absent on a 1.4 device, or
  `maxPushDescriptors` < 32). If R1 or R2 is needed, stop and report it; the controller decides before
  Task 5 starts.
- Note (decision U-WIN): the lavapipe (WSL) device check is not run in M2; it returns with Linux support
  (v1.x). Task 5 enables `pushDescriptor` as a required feature, and its GPU tests on NVIDIA are the final
  proof.
- Facts seen while this plan was written (2026-10-23), for comparison: an NVIDIA desktop GPU,
  `apiVersion = 1.4.x`, `maxPushDescriptors = 32`, `pushDescriptor = true`, both storage-image
  features true, R8_UINT and R16_UINT with STORAGE_IMAGE and STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT. So R1
  and R2 are expected to be "not needed". Record what you actually see, not these lines.

**Code** (the format check is easy to get wrong because `vulkaninfo --show-formats` groups formats with
identical properties; this parser was run on the NVIDIA device while the plan was written):

```powershell
# Prints "<formats in the group> optimal: <bit>" for the groups that contain R8_UINT or R16_UINT.
function Show-StorageFormatBits([string[]] $lines) {
    $want = 'FORMAT_R8_UINT', 'FORMAT_R16_UINT'
    $group = @(); $inFormats = $false; $inOptimal = $false; $hit = $null
    foreach ($l in $lines) {
        if ($l -match '^Formats: count') { $group = @(); $inFormats = $true; $inOptimal = $false; continue }
        if ($inFormats -and $l -match '^\t(FORMAT_\w+)$') { $group += $Matches[1]; continue }
        if ($l -match '^Properties:') { $inFormats = $false; $hit = $group | Where-Object { $want -contains $_ }; continue }
        if ($l -match '^\toptimalTilingFeatures') { $inOptimal = [bool]$hit; continue }
        if ($inOptimal -and $l -match '^\t\t(FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_(IMAGE|WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT)_BIT)$') {
            "$($hit -join ',') optimal: $($Matches[1])"; continue
        }
        if ($l -match '^\t\w') { $inOptimal = $false }
    }
}
```

**Tests first:** none (no code). The checks below are the test.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Check that M1b is finished and every repository is clean**

```powershell
foreach ($r in 'rawreel-workspace','dvv-base','dvv-registry','dvv-platform','dvv-gpu','rawreel') { "== $r"; git -C $r status --short --branch }
Get-Content rawreel-workspace/repos.txt
```

Expected: each repository prints exactly `## m1b` (no tracking info for the local-only repositories,
`## m1b` with nothing else for the others) and no file lines. `repos.txt` lists `dvv-base`,
`dvv-registry`, `dvv-platform`, `dvv-gpu`, `rawreel` (comment lines aside).

- [ ] **Step 2: Create the branches**

```powershell
foreach ($r in 'rawreel-workspace','dvv-base','dvv-registry','dvv-platform','dvv-gpu','rawreel') { git -C $r switch -c m2 }
foreach ($r in 'rawreel-workspace','dvv-base','dvv-registry','dvv-platform','dvv-gpu','rawreel') { "$r " + (git -C $r branch --show-current) + ' ' + (git -C $r config user.email) }
```

Expected: `Switched to a new branch 'm2'` six times; then every line reads `<repo> m2 <handle>@users.noreply.github.com`.

- [ ] **Step 2b: Put the M2 plan into rawreel-workspace**

Copy the approved `m2-plan.md` to `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m2-media.md`,
append an empty `## Changes during execution` section, and commit it on `m2`:

```powershell
git -C rawreel-workspace add docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m2-media.md
@'
docs: add the M2 plan

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C rawreel-workspace commit -F -
cmake -DREPO=rawreel-workspace -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake
```

Expected: one commit on `m2`; `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Device check on Windows (NVIDIA)**

```powershell
vulkaninfo 2>$null | Select-String -Pattern 'deviceName|apiVersion|maxPushDescriptors|pushDescriptor |shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats|shaderStorageImageWriteWithoutFormat'
# paste the function from "Code" first, then:
Show-StorageFormatBits (vulkaninfo --show-formats 2>$null)
```

Expected: the NVIDIA device with `apiVersion` 1.4.x, `maxPushDescriptors` ≥ 32, `pushDescriptor = true`,
`shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats = true`, `shaderStorageImageWriteWithoutFormat = true`; the parser
prints four lines: STORAGE_IMAGE and STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT for R8_UINT and for R16_UINT (one line may
name both formats when they share a group). `vulkaninfo` lives in the Windows system directory (installed
with the driver). A loader warning about layer manifests in the registry is harmless.
If the parser prints nothing, check that `vulkaninfo --show-formats` prints a `Formats: count` section.
If it does not, show the format properties with `vulkaninfo --json` and record how you checked.

- [ ] **Step 4: Baseline test count and the lint tools in WSL (no action if all fine)**

```powershell
ctest --test-dir _build/rawreel-workspace/vs2026 -N -C Release | Select-String 'Total Tests'
wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cmake --version | head -1 && clang-format --version && clang-tidy --version | head -2'
```

Expected: `Total Tests: <N>`; write N down. Task 1 expects N + 2. Then `cmake version 4.3.x`,
`clang-format version 19.1.7` and a clang-tidy 19 line (clang-format and the checkpoint build
need them; no test runs in WSL).

- [ ] **Step 5: Record the results**

Append to the `## Changes during execution` section of
`rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m2-media.md` (Step 2b) a block like this, using the
lines you saw:

```text
Task 0 (device check, <date>):
- NVIDIA: <deviceName>, apiVersion <x>, maxPushDescriptors <n>, pushDescriptor <true>,
  shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats <true>, shaderStorageImageWriteWithoutFormat <true>;
  R8_UINT/R16_UINT optimal: STORAGE_IMAGE, STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT.
- R1: not needed / needed (<what is missing>). R2: not needed / needed (<why>).
- Baseline workspace test count: <N>.
```

Commit it:

```powershell
git -C rawreel-workspace add docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m2-media.md
@'
docs: record the M2 device check

Push descriptors and storage images without a declared format are available on the local NVIDIA
device, so the fallbacks R1 and R2 of the M2 design are not needed.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C rawreel-workspace commit -F -
cmake -DREPO=rawreel-workspace -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake
```

Adjust the message body if R1 or R2 is needed. Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`. The block
must not contain machine paths, user names or host names (the device names are fine).

**Expected results:** six `m2` branches; device facts recorded; R1 and R2 "not needed" (as seen while
planning). No test runs and no full matrix (no code changed).

**Commit:** the plan file (Step 2b) and its record (Step 5).

**Notes:** design §10 Task 0, §4.2 (required features and the per-format write-without-format check),
§11 R1/R2/R3. The Slang spelling of `format("unknown")` (R3) is decided in Task 5, not here.

### Task 1: dvv-media skeleton, value types and workspace wiring

**Goal:** Create the local repository `dvv-media` (package `dvv_media` 0.1.0, namespace `dvv::media`) with the
standard skeleton, a package test and the frame value types of design §4.3 (`types.h`), including the
thread-safe colour table and the UTF-8 path helper. Wire it into the workspace before `rawreel`.

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/.clang-format`, `.clang-tidy`, `.gitattributes`, `.gitignore`, `LICENSE`. These are byte
  copies of the dvv-gpu files (MIT, "The rawreel authors").
- Create `dvv-media/CMakePresets.json`: includes the workspace presets.
- Create `dvv-media/.github/workflows/ci.yml`: calls the reusable workflow with `repo: dvv-media`. It stays
  inactive until the repository is published. It defines no job of its own (decision U-WIN: new CI
  work adds no Linux test jobs); the reusable workflow's existing jobs stay as they are.
- Create `dvv-media/README.md`: a short description and build instructions.
- Create `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`: the dependencies, target `dvv_media`, install and export.
- Create `dvv-media/cmake/dvv_mediaConfig.cmake.in`: `find_dependency` of the four dvv packages.
- Create `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/types.h`: the value types, the colour table API, and
  `pathFromUtf8`/`utf8FromPath`.
- Create `dvv-media/src/types.cpp`: `FrameKeyHash`, the colour table, the UTF-8 sanitiser and the path
  helpers.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/main.cpp`, `tests/test_types.cpp`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/package/CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/package/main.cpp`,
  `tests/package/run-package-test.cmake`.
- Modify in `rawreel-workspace`: `repos.txt`, `cmake/dvv-versions.cmake`, `CMakeLists.txt`,
  `cmake/DvvPresets.json` and `README.md`, which wire dvv-media in before `rawreel`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 0 (branch `m2` everywhere). Also the M1b packages: `dvv_base` (`dvv::base`, `Result`,
  `Errc`, `DVV_CMAKE_DIR`, `dvv_set_compiler_options`, `finishWithLeakCheck`), `dvv_registry`
  (`dvv::registry`), `dvv_platform` (`dvv::platform`) and `dvv_gpu` (`dvv::gpu`,
  `dvv::gpu::DeviceId`, `dvv::gpu::DeviceGeneration` from `dvv/gpu/device_id.h`).
- Produces (CMake): package `dvv_media` 0.1.0; target `dvv_media` (STATIC, alias `dvv::media`,
  `EXPORT_NAME media`, PUBLIC `dvv::base dvv::registry dvv::platform dvv::gpu`); test executable
  `dvv_media_tests` (CTest `dvv_media.unit`) with `${dvv_media_SOURCE_DIR}/src` on its include path, so
  tests can include private headers; CTest `dvv_media.package` (not under sanitizers, `RESOURCE_LOCK
  dvv_build_tree`). Later tasks add their sources to `add_library(dvv_media …)` and their test files to
  `add_executable(dvv_media_tests …)`. Task 6 adds `dvv_media_gpu_tests`, Task 7a the compile-fail
  cases and Task 9 `dvv_media_sources`.
- Produces (C++), `include/dvv/media/types.h` in full:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Frame value types of the media layer (spec §6, M2 design §4.3).

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/gpu/device_id.h"

namespace dvv::media {

struct Rational {
    std::int64_t num = 0;
    std::int64_t den = 1;
    bool operator==(const Rational&) const = default;  // plain member equality, no normalisation
};

using FormatId = std::uint32_t;  // index into the process-wide format table (catalogue first)
using ColourId = std::uint32_t;  // index into the process-wide colour table (0 = all Unspecified)
using OriginId = std::uint64_t;  // 0 = none

struct FrameKey {
    OriginId origin = 0;
    std::int64_t index = 0;
    bool operator==(const FrameKey&) const = default;
};
struct FrameKeyHash {
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t operator()(const FrameKey& key) const noexcept;
};
struct FrameDesc {
    FormatId format = 0;
    ColourId colour = 0;
    std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    bool operator==(const FrameDesc&) const = default;
};

enum class Api : std::uint8_t { HostPlanes, VkBuffer, VkImage /*, AvFrame (M4) */ };
enum class MemKind : std::uint8_t { Host, HostVisible, DeviceLocal };
struct Domain {
    MemKind kind = MemKind::Host;
    gpu::DeviceId device{0};
    gpu::DeviceGeneration generation{};
    bool operator==(const Domain&) const = default;
};
enum class LayoutPolicy : std::uint8_t { Any, Tight, RowAlign256, VkOptimal };
struct ViewSpec {
    FormatId format = 0;
    ColourId colour = 0;
    LayoutPolicy layout = LayoutPolicy::Any;
    Api api = Api::HostPlanes;
    Domain domain{};
    std::uint8_t downscaleLog2 = 0;
    bool operator==(const ViewSpec&) const = default;
};
enum class Fidelity : std::uint8_t { Exact, Lossless, Derived };
struct SyncPoint {
    gpu::DeviceId device{0};
    std::uint8_t timeline = 0;
    std::uint64_t value = 0;
};

enum class ColourMatrix : std::uint8_t { Unspecified, Bt601, Bt709, Bt2020Ncl, Rgb };
enum class ColourRange : std::uint8_t { Unspecified, Limited, Full };
enum class TransferFn : std::uint8_t { Unspecified, Srgb, Bt1886, Pq, Hlg, Linear };
enum class Primaries : std::uint8_t { Unspecified, Bt601_525, Bt601_625, Bt709, Bt2020 };
struct ColourParams {
    ColourMatrix matrix{};
    ColourRange range{};
    TransferFn transfer{};
    Primaries primaries{};
    bool operator==(const ColourParams&) const = default;
};

// Thread-safe. Equal parameters always give the same id; ColourParams{} is id 0.
[[nodiscard]] ColourId internColour(const ColourParams& params);
// A stable reference (entries are never moved or removed). An unknown id gives the parameters of
// id 0 (all Unspecified).
[[nodiscard]] const ColourParams& colourParams(ColourId id) noexcept;

// UTF-8 text to a path through std::u8string, never through the ANSI code page on Windows (D19).
// Invalid UTF-8 is replaced first (each byte that does not start a well-formed sequence becomes
// U+FFFD), so the conversion never throws.
[[nodiscard]] std::filesystem::path pathFromUtf8(std::string_view utf8);
// The opposite direction for messages and resolved URIs: path.u8string(), never path.string().
// Returns "(path not representable as UTF-8)" if the platform conversion fails.
[[nodiscard]] std::string utf8FromPath(const std::filesystem::path& path);

}  // namespace dvv::media
```

- Note (plan choice): `ViewSpec` and `ColourParams` get default member initialisers. The design shows
  the fields without them. With the initialisers a default-constructed value is never indeterminate, and
  the structs stay aggregates.
- Note (plan choice): `utf8FromPath` is added next to `pathFromUtf8`. It implements the arbiter's note:
  messages and `resolvedUri` convert paths to UTF-8 through `u8string()`, never `path::string()`.
  Tasks 11 and 13 use it.
- Note (plan choice): invalid UTF-8 is replaced with U+FFFD on every platform, not only on Windows.
  MSVC's `path(std::u8string)` throws on invalid UTF-8, and the design's signature has no error channel.
  One rule on both platforms keeps the tests identical. The cost is that a Linux file whose name is not
  valid UTF-8 cannot be opened through a URI; the README says so.
- Note (plan choice): dvv-base is not changed in M2 Tasks 0–3b. The small arithmetic helpers
  (`ceilDiv`, `alignUp`, `alignDown`) live privately in dvv-media (`src/math_util.h`, Task 2).
- Note (plan choice): `dvv_media` links all four dvv packages from Task 1 on (design §3), so the package
  test and the config file are complete from the start.

**Behaviour:**
- `FrameKeyHash`: mixes both fields so that equal keys hash equally and nearby keys spread. For example,
  use a SplitMix64 finaliser over `origin * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15 ^ uint64(index)`. Never throws.
- Colour table: process infrastructure that is never destroyed (a function-local
  `static Table* table = new Table`, like `LifetimeRegistry`). It holds a `std::vector<std::unique_ptr<ColourParams>>`
  under a `std::shared_mutex`, pre-seeded with `ColourParams{}` as id 0. `internColour` scans under a
  shared lock; on a miss it takes the unique lock, scans again and appends. A linear scan is fine (at most
  450 combinations). `colourParams` takes the shared lock, checks the bound and returns `*entries[id]`
  (unknown id: `*entries[0]`). The reference stays valid because entries are never moved or removed.
- `pathFromUtf8(utf8)`: (1) replace invalid UTF-8. Well-formed means the Unicode Table 3-7 rules: no
  overlong forms, no surrogates (U+D800..U+DFFF), nothing above U+10FFFF, no truncated sequences.
  Scanning from the left, a byte that does not start a well-formed sequence is replaced by `EF BF BD`
  and scanning goes on at the next byte. (2) Copy the bytes into a `std::u8string` and return
  `std::filesystem::path(u8)`. An empty input gives an empty path.
- `utf8FromPath(path)`: inside `try`, `const std::u8string text = path.u8string();` then copy the bytes
  into a `std::string`; `catch (const std::system_error&)` returns
  `"(path not representable as UTF-8)"`.
- No `Errc` values in this task (nothing here fails).

**Code** (CMake: the dependency handling and the package test are easy to get wrong):

`dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`:

```cmake
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
project(dvv_media VERSION 0.1.0 DESCRIPTION "dvv media layer" LANGUAGES CXX)

option(DVV_BUILD_TESTS "Build the dvv tests" ${PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL})

# The dvv dependencies: inside the workspace build cmake/dvv-versions.cmake has declared them first,
# so these declarations are ignored there (spec §3.1). Stand-alone builds take them from the sibling
# folders (FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_<NAME> in the workspace presets), from installed packages or from
# Git.
include(FetchContent)
FetchContent_Declare(dvv_base
    GIT_REPOSITORY "https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dvv-base.git"
    GIT_TAG main
    FIND_PACKAGE_ARGS 0.1)
FetchContent_Declare(dvv_registry
    GIT_REPOSITORY "https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dvv-registry.git"
    GIT_TAG main
    FIND_PACKAGE_ARGS 0.1)
FetchContent_Declare(dvv_platform
    GIT_REPOSITORY "https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dvv-platform.git"
    GIT_TAG main
    FIND_PACKAGE_ARGS 0.1)
FetchContent_Declare(dvv_gpu
    GIT_REPOSITORY "https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dvv-gpu.git"
    GIT_TAG main
    FIND_PACKAGE_ARGS 0.1)
# A stand-alone build tests only this repository (the normal variable hides the cache entry from
# the dependencies' option()); as a subproject the parent's value is kept, and the workspace
# build tests every repository.
if(PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL)
    set(DVV_BUILD_TESTS OFF)
endif()
FetchContent_MakeAvailable(dvv_base dvv_registry dvv_platform dvv_gpu)
if(PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL)
    unset(DVV_BUILD_TESTS)
endif()
if(NOT DVV_CMAKE_DIR)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_base did not define DVV_CMAKE_DIR")
endif()

include(GNUInstallDirs)
include(CMakePackageConfigHelpers)

add_library(dvv_media STATIC
    src/types.cpp)
add_library(dvv::media ALIAS dvv_media)

    $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include>
    $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR}>)
target_link_libraries(dvv_media PUBLIC dvv::base dvv::registry dvv::platform dvv::gpu)
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_media)

install(DIRECTORY include/ DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR})
set(config_dir ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/cmake/dvv_media)
install(EXPORT dvv_mediaTargets NAMESPACE dvv:: DESTINATION ${config_dir})

    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_mediaConfig.cmake INSTALL_DESTINATION ${config_dir})
write_basic_package_version_file(${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_mediaConfigVersion.cmake
    COMPATIBILITY SameMinorVersion)
install(FILES
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_mediaConfig.cmake
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_mediaConfigVersion.cmake
    DESTINATION ${config_dir})

if(DVV_BUILD_TESTS)
    enable_testing()
    add_subdirectory(tests)
endif()
```

`dvv-media/README.md` (keep it short; Task 15 completes it): a title, then a paragraph: "Media layer of
the dvv stack used by rawreel: pixel formats, frames, the frame store and sources. C++23; depends on
dvv-base, dvv-registry, dvv-platform and dvv-gpu." Then the same "Build it through rawreel-workspace"
paragraph and `cmake --workflow` block as `dvv-gpu/README.md`. Then a section "Paths": URI and
command-line paths are UTF-8; invalid UTF-8 becomes U+FFFD, so a file whose name is not valid UTF-8
cannot be opened by name.

**Tests first** (`tests/test_types.cpp`, doctest; the first failure is a compile error: `dvv/media/types.h`
does not exist):
1. `frame keys compare and hash by value`: `FrameKey{1, 2} == FrameKey{1, 2}`, `!= FrameKey{1, 3}`,
   `!= FrameKey{2, 2}`; equal keys give equal `FrameKeyHash` values. An
   `std::unordered_set<FrameKey, FrameKeyHash>` filled with origins 1..10 × indices 0..99 has `size() ==
   1000` and `contains` every inserted key.
2. `value types compare by every field`: default `FrameDesc`, `ViewSpec`, `Domain` and `Rational`
   compare equal to their defaults. Changing any single field makes `ViewSpec` differ: format, colour,
   layout, api, domain kind, domain device (`gpu::DeviceId{1}`), domain generation, `downscaleLog2`.
   `Rational{50, 2} != Rational{25, 1}` (no normalisation).
3. `colour interning`: `internColour(ColourParams{}) == 0`; `colourParams(0) == ColourParams{}`.
   `{Bt709, Limited, Bt1886, Bt709}` interned twice gives the same id, which is not 0;
   `colourParams(that id)` returns those values. A params value differing in one field gets a different id.
   `colourParams(0xFFFFFFFF) == ColourParams{}`.
4. `colour interning is thread-safe and references stay valid`. Before the threads, take
   `const ColourParams& first = colourParams(internColour(p0))` for some `p0`. Then 8 `std::thread`s each
   intern all 450 combinations (5 matrices × 3 ranges × 6 transfers × 5 primaries), each in a different
   rotation of the list, and store the ids in a per-thread vector (no doctest assertion inside threads:
   doctest assertions are not thread-safe). After `join`: all eight vectors agree combination by combination,
   the 450 ids are distinct, `colourParams(id)` returns the combination for each, `&first ==
   &colourParams(internColour(p0))` and `first == p0`.
5. `pathFromUtf8 keeps non-ASCII file names`. In a fresh directory under
   `std::filesystem::temp_directory_path()` (unique name, removed at the end), write 3 bytes to
   `dir / pathFromUtf8("Grüße-日本-🎞.yuv")` with `std::ofstream`. A `directory_iterator` over `dir` finds
   exactly one entry, `utf8FromPath(entry.path().filename()) == "Grüße-日本-🎞.yuv"`, and its
   `file_size == 3`. (The sources are UTF-8; MSVC gets `/utf-8` from `dvv_set_compiler_options`.)
6. `pathFromUtf8 replaces invalid UTF-8`: `utf8FromPath(pathFromUtf8(x))` gives, for these `x`:
   `"a\xFF" "b"` → `"a\xEF\xBF\xBD" "b"`; `"\xE6\x97"` (truncated) → two U+FFFD; `"\xC0\xAF"` (overlong) →
   two U+FFFD; `"\xED\xA0\x80"` (surrogate) → three U+FFFD; `"\xF0\x9F\x8E\x9E"` (🎞) unchanged;
   `""` → `""`.

process infrastructure), §9 (wiring), D19 and the arbiter's minor note (`u8string`). Risk: the in-tree
package consumer rebuilds dvv-gpu (with vk-bootstrap) from source, so the package test takes a few
minutes; that is expected. Publishing order (later, user go-ahead only): dvv-media must exist on GitHub

---

### Task 2: Pixel-format descriptor, catalogue and geometry

**Goal:** Add the per-plane "pixel groups with sample slots" descriptor (D2) with its validation, the
process-wide format table holding the 37-format catalogue (D3) in a fixed order, frame geometry,
component sizes and the derived unpacked formats (D5).

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/pixel_format.h`: the public descriptor API (below).
- Create `dvv-media/src/pixel_format.cpp`: format table, `validate`, `catalogue`, `descriptor`,
  `findFormat`, `unpackedFormat`, `isUnpacked`, `geometry`, `componentSize`.
- Create `dvv-media/src/catalogue.cpp`: the 37 built-in descriptors (`detail::builtInFormats()`).
- Create `dvv-media/src/format_table.h`: private declarations shared by `pixel_format.cpp`,
  `catalogue.cpp` and (Task 3b) `format_json.cpp`.
- Create `dvv-media/src/math_util.h`: private `constexpr` helpers `ceilDiv`, `alignUp`, `alignDown`
  over `std::uint64_t` (`alignUp(v, a) = ceilDiv(v, a) * a`, `a > 0`, not necessarily a power of two).
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_pixel_format.cpp`.
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`: add `src/catalogue.cpp` and `src/pixel_format.cpp` to `dvv_media`.
- Modify `dvv-media/tests/CMakeLists.txt`: add `test_pixel_format.cpp` to `dvv_media_tests`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`types.h`: `FormatId`; target layout; `Result`/`Errc` from dvv-base).
- Produces: `include/dvv/media/pixel_format.h` in full:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Pixel-format descriptors (spec §7.3, M2 design D2): a plane is a row of groups; a group spans
// groupPixels full-resolution pixels in groupBytes bytes made of words of wordBytes with one byte
// order; each sample of a group is a slot {component, index in the component's own grid, word,
// shift}, value = (word >> shift) & ((1 << bits) - 1).

#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/media/types.h"

namespace dvv::media {

enum class ColourModel : std::uint8_t { Gray, Yuv, Rgb };
enum class ComponentKind : std::uint8_t { Y, U, V, R, G, B, A };
enum class ByteOrder : std::uint8_t { Little, Big };

inline constexpr std::size_t MAX_COMPONENTS = 4, MAX_PLANES = 4, MAX_SLOTS = 12;
// GPU unpack limit (design §5.4): samples of one component per group.
inline constexpr std::size_t MAX_COMPONENT_SAMPLES = 6;

struct ComponentDesc {
    ComponentKind kind = ComponentKind::Y;
    std::uint8_t bits = 0;      // significant bits, 1..16
    std::uint8_t log2SubX = 0;  // horizontal subsampling (1 = half width, rounded up)
    std::uint8_t log2SubY = 0;
    bool operator==(const ComponentDesc&) const = default;
};
struct SampleSlot {
    std::uint8_t component = 0;  // index into components
    std::uint8_t index = 0;      // sample index inside the group, in the component's own grid
    std::uint8_t word = 0;       // word index inside the group
    std::uint8_t shift = 0;      // bit position of the sample's LSB inside the word
    bool operator==(const SampleSlot&) const = default;
};
struct PlaneDesc {
    std::uint8_t groupPixels = 1;  // full-resolution pixels per group (1, 2, 6, ...)
    std::uint8_t groupBytes = 1;   // bytes per group
    std::uint8_t wordBytes = 1;    // 1, 2 or 4; groupBytes % wordBytes == 0
    ByteOrder order = ByteOrder::Little;
    std::uint16_t rowAlign = 1;    // row stride alignment in bytes (v210: 128)
    std::uint8_t log2SubY = 0;     // rows = ceil(height / 2^log2SubY); equals its components'
    std::uint8_t slotCount = 0;
    std::array<SampleSlot, MAX_SLOTS> slots{};  // every sample of one group; other bits are padding
    bool operator==(const PlaneDesc&) const = default;
};
struct PixelFormatDescriptor {
    std::string name;  // FFmpeg pix_fmt name ("v210": codec name)
    ColourModel model = ColourModel::Gray;
    std::uint8_t componentCount = 0;
    std::array<ComponentDesc, MAX_COMPONENTS> components{};  // order = unpacked plane order
    std::uint8_t planeCount = 0;
    std::array<PlaneDesc, MAX_PLANES> planes{};
    std::uint8_t widthMultiple = 1;  // the width must be a multiple of this (v210: 2)
    bool operator==(const PixelFormatDescriptor&) const = default;
};

// Checks a descriptor, including the GPU limits. Errc::Format with the reason (rules in the plan).
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> validate(const PixelFormatDescriptor& descriptor);

// Process-wide table: entries are heap-allocated and never moved or removed, so descriptor()
// returns stable references while other threads intern formats. The catalogue (D3) is interned at
// first use, in the order of D3, as ids 0..36.
[[nodiscard]] std::span<const FormatId> catalogue();
// Unknown id: a reference to a static empty descriptor (name "", no components, no planes).
[[nodiscard]] const PixelFormatDescriptor& descriptor(FormatId format) noexcept;
// Exact, case-sensitive name match over every interned format (catalogue, unpacked, custom).
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<FormatId> findFormat(std::string_view name) noexcept;
// The lossless planar form (D5), named "<name>@planes"; interned on first use; thread-safe;
// idempotent for unpacked formats; an unknown id is returned unchanged.
[[nodiscard]] FormatId unpackedFormat(FormatId format);
[[nodiscard]] bool isUnpacked(FormatId format) noexcept;

struct PlaneGeometry {
    std::uint64_t offset = 0;  // from the frame start
    std::uint32_t stride = 0;  // rowBytes rounded up to the plane's rowAlign
    std::uint32_t rows = 0;
    std::uint32_t rowBytes = 0;  // ceil(width / groupPixels) * groupBytes
    bool operator==(const PlaneGeometry&) const = default;
};
struct FrameGeometry {
    std::array<PlaneGeometry, MAX_PLANES> planes{};
    std::uint8_t planeCount = 0;
    std::uint64_t bytes = 0;  // tight layout, planes back to back
    bool operator==(const FrameGeometry&) const = default;
};
// Errc::Format for a 0 width or height, a width that is not a multiple of widthMultiple ("v210 needs
// a width that is a multiple of 2"), or a plane whose stride or stride x rows exceeds 0xFFFFFFFF.
// Errc::Internal for an unknown id.
[[nodiscard]] Result<FrameGeometry> geometry(FormatId format, std::uint32_t width,
                                             std::uint32_t height);
// ceil(width / 2^log2SubX) x ceil(height / 2^log2SubY); {0, 0} for an unknown id or component.
[[nodiscard]] std::pair<std::uint32_t, std::uint32_t> componentSize(FormatId format,
                                                                    std::size_t component,
                                                                    std::uint32_t width,
                                                                    std::uint32_t height);

}  // namespace dvv::media
```

- Produces (private), `src/format_table.h`:

```cpp
namespace dvv::media::detail {
// The 37 built-in descriptors in the order of D3 (catalogue.cpp). Each one passes validate().
[[nodiscard]] std::span<const PixelFormatDescriptor> builtInFormats();
}
```

  Task 3b adds `internCustomFormat` here.
- Note (plan choice): the descriptor structs get defaulted `operator==` and default member initialisers
  (the design shows neither). Value-initialised arrays make unused slots and planes zero, so equality is
  well defined. Task 3b's JSON round trip and its idempotent interning need it.
- Note (plan choice): `validate()` always includes the GPU limits (at most 6 samples of a component per
  group, §5.4). There is no separate GPU check. Every catalogue entry passes.
- Note (plan choice): `validate()` requires the canonical component order of D5 (Gray: Y[, A]; Yuv:
  Y, U, V[, A]; Rgb: R, G, B[, A]), so the unpacked plane order is fixed by the model.
- Note (plan choice): an unknown id is `Internal` in `geometry()` (a programming error, not bad input).
  `descriptor()` returns a static empty descriptor, so the `noexcept` functions never fail.
- Note (plan choice): unpacked descriptors have `widthMultiple = 1`. Their planes have groups of 1 or
  2^log2SubX pixels, so a multiple of 2 would fail `validate()` for v210's unpacked Y plane. The source's
  geometry is always checked first anyway.

**Behaviour:**

*Format table* (in `pixel_format.cpp`; process infrastructure, never destroyed:
`static Table* table = new Table` built on first use). It holds a
`std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Entry>>` under a `std::shared_mutex`. `Entry` = `{PixelFormatDescriptor
descriptor; bool unpacked; std::optional<FormatId> unpackedId}`, where `unpackedId` is guarded by the
table mutex. The constructor interns `detail::builtInFormats()` in order, so the catalogue ids are 0..36.
`catalogue()` returns a span over a static `std::array<FormatId, 37>` = {0..36}. `findFormat` scans under
a shared lock. `unpackedFormat(f)`: an unknown id is returned unchanged; an unpacked `f` returns `f`; if
`unpackedId` is set, return it; otherwise build the descriptor (below), take the unique lock, re-check
`unpackedId`, append the entry with `unpacked = true`, store the new id and return it. All lookups are
thread-safe. Returned references stay valid forever.

*Unpacked descriptor* of `f` (D5): name = `f.name + "@planes"`; same `model`, `componentCount` and
`components`; `widthMultiple = 1`; `planeCount = componentCount`. Plane `i` belongs to component `i`:
`groupPixels = 1 << components[i].log2SubX`, `groupBytes = wordBytes = (bits <= 8 ? 1 : 2)`, `order =
Little`, `rowAlign = 1`, `log2SubY = components[i].log2SubY`, `slotCount = 1`, `slots[0] = {i, 0, 0,
0}`. It passes `validate()`.

*validate()* returns `Errc::Format` with the message `"pixel format '<name>': <reason>"`. It checks in this
order and stops at the first failure. Reasons (tests match on the quoted substrings):
1. name: 1..64 bytes of `[A-Za-z0-9_.-]`, optionally followed by exactly the suffix `@planes` → else
   `"invalid name"`.
2. `componentCount` 1..4 → `"needs 1 to 4 components"`; `planeCount` 1..4 → `"needs 1 to 4 planes"`.
3. component kinds equal the canonical list of the model: Gray `Y` or `Y A`; Yuv `Y U V` or `Y U V A`; Rgb
   `R G B` or `R G B A` → else `"components do not match the colour model"`.
4. per component `c`: `bits` 1..16 → `"component <c>: bits must be 1 to 16"`; Y, R, G, B and A have
   `log2SubX == log2SubY == 0`; U and V have both ≤ 2, and U equals V → else
   `"component <c>: invalid subsampling"`.
5. `widthMultiple >= 1` → `"widthMultiple must be at least 1"`.
6. per plane `p`: `wordBytes` ∈ {1, 2, 4} → `"plane <p>: wordBytes must be 1, 2 or 4"`; `groupBytes > 0`
   and `groupBytes % wordBytes == 0` → `"plane <p>: groupBytes must be a non-zero multiple of
   wordBytes"`; `groupPixels > 0` and `groupPixels % widthMultiple == 0` → `"plane <p>: groupPixels
   must be a non-zero multiple of widthMultiple"`; `rowAlign` a power of two in 1..4096 → `"plane <p>:
   rowAlign must be a power of two up to 4096"`; `slotCount` 1..12 → `"plane <p>: needs 1 to 12
   slots"`.
7. per slot `s` of plane `p`: `component < componentCount` → `"plane <p> slot <s>: component out of
   range"`; `word < groupBytes / wordBytes` and `shift + bits(component) <= 8 * wordBytes` →
   `"plane <p> slot <s>: outside its word"`.
8. no two slots of a plane share a bit: same `word` and intersecting `[shift, shift + bits)` →
   `"plane <p>: slots <s> and <t> overlap"`.
9. for `c` = 0, 1, …: the planes holding at least one slot of `c` — more than one →
   `"component <c> is in more than one plane"`; none → `"component <c> is in no plane"`.
10. per plane `p` and each component `c` in it: `log2SubY(p) == log2SubY(c)` → `"plane <p>: log2SubY
    differs from component <c>"`; `groupPixels % (1 << log2SubX(c)) == 0`, the slot count of `c` equals
    `n = groupPixels >> log2SubX(c)` and the indices are exactly `0..n-1` once each → `"plane <p>:
    samples of component <c> do not match the group"`; `n <= 6` → `"plane <p>: more than 6 samples of
    component <c> per group (GPU limit)"`.

*geometry(f, w, h)*: unknown id → `Internal` `"unknown format id <f>"`. `w == 0 || h == 0` → `Format`
`"<name>: frame size <w>x<h> is empty"`. `w % widthMultiple != 0` → `Format` `"<name> needs a width that is
a multiple of <m>"` (v210: exactly `"v210 needs a width that is a multiple of 2"`). Each plane is computed
in `std::uint64_t`: `rowBytes = ceilDiv(w, groupPixels) * groupBytes`, `stride = alignUp(rowBytes,
rowAlign)`, `rows = ceilDiv(h, 1 << log2SubY)`. If `stride > 0xFFFFFFFF` or `stride * rows >
0xFFFFFFFF` → `Format` `"<name> at <w>x<h>: plane <p> exceeds 4 GiB"`. `offset` = the sum of the earlier
planes' `stride * rows`; `bytes` = the sum of all of them.

*componentSize(f, c, w, h)*: `{ceilDiv(w, 1 << log2SubX), ceilDiv(h, 1 << log2SubY)}`; `{0, 0}` for an
unknown id or `c >= componentCount`.

*The catalogue* (`catalogue.cpp`, D3 order = ids 0..36). Notation: a component is `Kbits` or
`Kbits(sx,sy)` (log2SubX, log2SubY; omitted = 0,0). A plane is `gp/gb/wb` = groupPixels/groupBytes/wordBytes,
then `BE` when big-endian, `a128` for rowAlign 128, `sy1` for log2SubY 1, then its slots as
`K.index@word<<shift` in the order listed (`<<0` omitted). Slot order matters for descriptor equality and
the JSON form. Use exactly this order.

| id | name | model | components | planes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | gray | Gray | Y8 | 1/1/1: Y.0@0 |
| 1 | gray10le | Gray | Y10 | 1/2/2: Y.0@0 |
| 2 | gray12le | Gray | Y12 | 1/2/2: Y.0@0 |
| 3 | gray16le | Gray | Y16 | 1/2/2: Y.0@0 |
| 4 | gray16be | Gray | Y16 | 1/2/2 BE: Y.0@0 |
| 5 | yuv420p | Yuv | Y8, U8(1,1), V8(1,1) | 1/1/1: Y.0@0 · 2/1/1 sy1: U.0@0 · 2/1/1 sy1: V.0@0 |
| 6 | yuv422p | Yuv | Y8, U8(1,0), V8(1,0) | 1/1/1: Y.0@0 · 2/1/1: U.0@0 · 2/1/1: V.0@0 |
| 7 | yuv444p | Yuv | Y8, U8, V8 | 1/1/1: Y.0@0 · 1/1/1: U.0@0 · 1/1/1: V.0@0 |
| 8 | yuv420p10le | Yuv | Y10, U10(1,1), V10(1,1) | 1/2/2: Y.0@0 · 2/2/2 sy1: U.0@0 · 2/2/2 sy1: V.0@0 |
| 9 | yuv422p10le | Yuv | Y10, U10(1,0), V10(1,0) | 1/2/2: Y.0@0 · 2/2/2: U.0@0 · 2/2/2: V.0@0 |
| 10 | yuv444p10le | Yuv | Y10, U10, V10 | 1/2/2: Y.0@0 · 1/2/2: U.0@0 · 1/2/2: V.0@0 |
| 11 | yuv420p12le | Yuv | Y12, U12(1,1), V12(1,1) | as id 8 |
| 12 | yuv422p12le | Yuv | Y12, U12(1,0), V12(1,0) | as id 9 |
| 13 | yuv444p12le | Yuv | Y12, U12, V12 | as id 10 |
| 14 | yuv420p16le | Yuv | Y16, U16(1,1), V16(1,1) | as id 8 |
| 15 | yuv444p16le | Yuv | Y16, U16, V16 | as id 10 |
| 16 | yuv420p10be | Yuv | Y10, U10(1,1), V10(1,1) | as id 8, every plane BE |
| 17 | nv12 | Yuv | Y8, U8(1,1), V8(1,1) | 1/1/1: Y.0@0 · 2/2/1 sy1: U.0@0, V.0@1 |
| 18 | nv21 | Yuv | Y8, U8(1,1), V8(1,1) | 1/1/1: Y.0@0 · 2/2/1 sy1: V.0@0, U.0@1 |
| 19 | p010le | Yuv | Y10, U10(1,1), V10(1,1) | 1/2/2: Y.0@0<<6 · 2/4/2 sy1: U.0@0<<6, V.0@1<<6 |
| 20 | p016le | Yuv | Y16, U16(1,1), V16(1,1) | 1/2/2: Y.0@0 · 2/4/2 sy1: U.0@0, V.0@1 |
| 21 | yuyv422 | Yuv | Y8, U8(1,0), V8(1,0) | 2/4/1: Y.0@0, U.0@1, Y.1@2, V.0@3 |
| 22 | uyvy422 | Yuv | Y8, U8(1,0), V8(1,0) | 2/4/1: U.0@0, Y.0@1, V.0@2, Y.1@3 |
| 23 | y210le | Yuv | Y10, U10(1,0), V10(1,0) | 2/8/2: Y.0@0<<6, U.0@1<<6, Y.1@2<<6, V.0@3<<6 |
| 24 | v210 | Yuv | Y10, U10(1,0), V10(1,0); widthMultiple 2 | 6/16/4 a128: U.0@0, Y.0@0<<10, V.0@0<<20, Y.1@1, U.1@1<<10, Y.2@1<<20, V.1@2, Y.3@2<<10, U.2@2<<20, Y.4@3, V.2@3<<10, Y.5@3<<20 |
| 25 | rgb24 | Rgb | R8, G8, B8 | 1/3/1: R.0@0, G.0@1, B.0@2 |
| 26 | bgr24 | Rgb | R8, G8, B8 | 1/3/1: B.0@0, G.0@1, R.0@2 |
| 27 | rgba | Rgb | R8, G8, B8, A8 | 1/4/1: R.0@0, G.0@1, B.0@2, A.0@3 |
| 28 | bgra | Rgb | R8, G8, B8, A8 | 1/4/1: B.0@0, G.0@1, R.0@2, A.0@3 |
| 29 | argb | Rgb | R8, G8, B8, A8 | 1/4/1: A.0@0, R.0@1, G.0@2, B.0@3 |
| 30 | rgb48le | Rgb | R16, G16, B16 | 1/6/2: R.0@0, G.0@1, B.0@2 |
| 31 | rgb48be | Rgb | R16, G16, B16 | 1/6/2 BE: R.0@0, G.0@1, B.0@2 |
| 32 | rgba64le | Rgb | R16, G16, B16, A16 | 1/8/2: R.0@0, G.0@1, B.0@2, A.0@3 |
| 33 | x2rgb10le | Rgb | R10, G10, B10 | 1/4/4: R.0@0<<20, G.0@0<<10, B.0@0 |
| 34 | gbrp | Rgb | R8, G8, B8 | 1/1/1: G.0@0 · 1/1/1: B.0@0 · 1/1/1: R.0@0 |
| 35 | gbrp10le | Rgb | R10, G10, B10 | 1/2/2: G.0@0 · 1/2/2: B.0@0 · 1/2/2: R.0@0 |
| 36 | gbrp16le | Rgb | R16, G16, B16 | 1/2/2: G.0@0 · 1/2/2: B.0@0 · 1/2/2: R.0@0 |

`K` maps to the component index in the model's canonical order (Y=0, U=1, V=2, A=3; R=0, G=1, B=2,
A=3). Planes are listed in memory order (plane 0 first). Every plane without `BE` is little-endian, and every
`rowAlign` without `a128` is 1. Write the table with small local helper functions, e.g.
`planarPlane(component, wordBytes, subX, subY)`, as long as the resulting values are exactly these.

**Code:** none dictated beyond the header; the rules above are complete.

**Tests first** (`tests/test_pixel_format.cpp`; the first failure is a compile error: `dvv/media/pixel_format.h` is
missing):
1. `the catalogue lists 37 formats in D3 order`: `catalogue().size() == 37`, `catalogue()[i] == i`, and
   `descriptor(i).name` equals the table's name column for every `i`.
2. `every catalogue entry validates`: `validate(descriptor(id))` succeeds for all 37 (the message is
   printed on failure).
3. `findFormat`: `findFormat("v210") == 24`, `findFormat("gbrp16le") == 36`, `findFormat("V210")`,
   `findFormat("")` and `findFormat("nope")` are `nullopt`.
4. `representative descriptor fields`: p010le has `planeCount 2`; `planes[1]` = groupPixels 2,
   groupBytes 4, wordBytes 2, Little, log2SubY 1, slots `{1,0,0,6}`, `{2,0,1,6}`. v210 has
   `widthMultiple 2`, `planes[0].rowAlign 128`, `slotCount 12`, and `slots[11] == {0,5,3,20}`. gbrp
   `planes[0].slots[0].component == 1` (G). rgb48be `planes[0].order == Big`.
5. `geometry matches hand-computed sizes`: for every format, at 1×1, 33×17 and 49×3 (v210: 2×2, 34×17,
   50×3), compare `stride` and `rows` per plane, `rowBytes == stride` (except v210, where `rowBytes =
   ceil(w/6)·16`: 16, 96, 144), `offset` = the sum of the earlier planes, and `bytes`, against this table.
   The table was derived from FFmpeg's `av_image_fill_linesizes` with alignment 1 and the v210 decoder
   stride, not from the descriptor model. Entries read `stride×rows` per plane `= bytes`:

| format | 1×1 | 33×17 | 49×3 |
|---|---|---|---|
| gray | 1×1 = 1 | 33×17 = 561 | 49×3 = 147 |
| gray10le, gray12le, gray16le, gray16be | 2×1 = 2 | 66×17 = 1122 | 98×3 = 294 |
| yuv420p | 1×1 1×1 1×1 = 3 | 33×17 17×9 17×9 = 867 | 49×3 25×2 25×2 = 247 |
| yuv422p | 1×1 1×1 1×1 = 3 | 33×17 17×17 17×17 = 1139 | 49×3 25×3 25×3 = 297 |
| yuv444p | 1×1 1×1 1×1 = 3 | 33×17 ×3 planes = 1683 | 49×3 ×3 planes = 441 |
| yuv420p10le, yuv420p12le, yuv420p16le, yuv420p10be | 2×1 2×1 2×1 = 6 | 66×17 34×9 34×9 = 1734 | 98×3 50×2 50×2 = 494 |
| yuv422p10le, yuv422p12le | 2×1 2×1 2×1 = 6 | 66×17 34×17 34×17 = 2278 | 98×3 50×3 50×3 = 594 |
| yuv444p10le, yuv444p12le, yuv444p16le | 2×1 ×3 = 6 | 66×17 ×3 = 3366 | 98×3 ×3 = 882 |
| nv12, nv21 | 1×1 2×1 = 3 | 33×17 34×9 = 867 | 49×3 50×2 = 247 |
| p010le, p016le | 2×1 4×1 = 6 | 66×17 68×9 = 1734 | 98×3 100×2 = 494 |
| yuyv422, uyvy422 | 4×1 = 4 | 68×17 = 1156 | 100×3 = 300 |
| y210le | 8×1 = 8 | 136×17 = 2312 | 200×3 = 600 |
| rgb24, bgr24 | 3×1 = 3 | 99×17 = 1683 | 147×3 = 441 |
| rgba, bgra, argb, x2rgb10le | 4×1 = 4 | 132×17 = 2244 | 196×3 = 588 |
| rgb48le, rgb48be | 6×1 = 6 | 198×17 = 3366 | 294×3 = 882 |
| rgba64le | 8×1 = 8 | 264×17 = 4488 | 392×3 = 1176 |
| gbrp | 1×1 ×3 = 3 | 33×17 ×3 = 1683 | 49×3 ×3 = 441 |
| gbrp10le, gbrp16le | 2×1 ×3 = 6 | 66×17 ×3 = 3366 | 98×3 ×3 = 882 |

   v210: 2×2 → 128×2 = 256; 34×17 → 128×17 = 2176; 50×3 → 256×3 = 768.
6. `v210 stride and width rules`: widths 2, 48, 50, 96, 98 (height 1) give strides 128, 128, 256, 256,
   384. `geometry(v210, 33, 17)` and `geometry(v210, 1, 1)` fail with `Errc::Format` and the message
   `"v210 needs a width that is a multiple of 2"`.
7. `geometry rejects bad sizes`: width 0 or height 0 → `Format` ("is empty"); `gray16le` at 65536×65536 →
   `Format` ("exceeds 4 GiB"); `rgba64le` at 0xFFFFFFFF×1 → `Format` ("exceeds 4 GiB"); id 100000 →
   `Internal`.
8. `componentSize`: yuv420p 33×17: component 0 `{33, 17}`, 1 `{17, 9}`; v210 34×17 component 2 `{17, 17}`;
   nv12 1×1 component 2 `{1, 1}`; yuv420p component 3 → `{0, 0}`; id 100000 → `{0, 0}`.
9. `unpacked formats`: for every catalogue id `f` with `u = unpackedFormat(f)`: `u != f`, `isUnpacked(u)`,
   `!isUnpacked(f)`, `unpackedFormat(u) == u`, `unpackedFormat(f) == u` again,
   `descriptor(u).name == descriptor(f).name + "@planes"`, `validate(descriptor(u))` succeeds,
   `planeCount == componentCount`, plane `i` has exactly one slot `{i,0,0,0}`, and `wordBytes` is 1 for
   ≤ 8 bits and 2 otherwise. Also `findFormat("v210@planes") == unpackedFormat(24)`.
   `geometry(unpackedFormat(v210), 34, 17)` equals `geometry(yuv422p10le, 34, 17)` field by field.
   `descriptor(unpackedFormat(gbrp)).planes[0].slots[0].component == 0` (R first).
10. `unpackedFormat is thread-safe`: 8 `std::thread`s each call `unpackedFormat` for all 37 ids (rotated
    start) and store the results. After `join` all agree, and each equals `unpackedFormat(id)` called
    afterwards. (Assertions only after `join`.)
11. `unknown ids`: `descriptor(100000).name.empty()` and `planeCount == 0`; `isUnpacked(100000)` false;
    `unpackedFormat(100000) == 100000`.
12. `validate rejects broken descriptors` (one SUBCASE each). Copy a catalogue descriptor, change it,
    expect `Errc::Format` and a message containing the quoted text:
    - `gray` with name `""`, `"a b"`, `"a@b"` → "invalid name"; with name `"gray@planes"` → succeeds.
    - `gray` with `componentCount = 0` → "needs 1 to 4 components".
    - `yuv420p` with `components[1].kind = Y` → "components do not match the colour model".
    - `gray10le` with bits 17, and with bits 0 → "bits must be 1 to 16".
    - `yuv420p` with `components[0].log2SubX = 1`; with U and V `log2SubX = 3`; with only V's
      `log2SubY = 0` → "invalid subsampling".
    - `gray` with `widthMultiple = 0` → "widthMultiple must be at least 1".
    - `gray10le` plane 0 `wordBytes = 3` → "wordBytes must be 1, 2 or 4".
    - `rgb48le` plane 0 `groupBytes = 5` → "groupBytes must be a non-zero multiple of wordBytes".
    - `v210` with `widthMultiple = 4` → "groupPixels must be a non-zero multiple of widthMultiple".
    - `gray` plane 0 `rowAlign = 3`, and `rowAlign = 8192` → "rowAlign must be a power of two up to
      4096".
    - `gray` plane 0 `slotCount = 0` → "needs 1 to 12 slots".
    - `gray` slot 0 `component = 5` → "component out of range".
    - `p010le` plane 0 slot 0 `shift = 7`; `yuyv422` slot 0 `word = 4` → "outside its word".
    - `x2rgb10le` slot 1 (G) `shift = 15` → "slots 0 and 1 overlap".
    - `gbrp` plane 1 slot 0 `component = 1` → "component 1 is in more than one plane".
    - `yuv420p` with `planeCount = 2` → "component 2 is in no plane".
    - `yuv420p` plane 1 `log2SubY = 0` → "log2SubY differs from component 1".
    - `yuyv422` slot 2 `index = 0` (Y index 0 twice) → "samples of component 0 do not match the group".
    - a Gray descriptor named `wide`: Y8, one plane 7/7/1 with slots `Y.k@k` for k = 0..6 →
      "more than 6 samples of component 0 per group (GPU limit)".

### Task 3: CPU reference pack/unpack and layout goldens

**Goal:** Add the scalar CPU reference (spec §7.3): `pack`, `unpack` and `unpackToPlanes` for every
descriptor. Add the shared code-value generators (`noise`, `gradient`, D16) and a hand-derived layout
golden for each of the 37 formats, plus the v210 partial-group goldens 2×1 and 4×1 (D4).

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/reference.h`: the public reference API (below).
- Create `dvv-media/src/reference.cpp`: `pack`, `unpack`, `unpackToPlanes`.
- Create `dvv-media/src/patterns.h`: private generator API (below), used by Task 6 (GPU tests) and Task 10
  (synthetic source).
- Create `dvv-media/src/patterns.cpp`: `gradientValue`, `noiseValue`, `generate`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_reference.cpp`: round trips, padding, unpacked planes, errors, generators.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_layout_goldens.cpp`: the 39 literal goldens.
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`: add `src/patterns.cpp` and `src/reference.cpp`.
- Modify `dvv-media/tests/CMakeLists.txt`: add the two test files.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (`pixel_format.h`: `descriptor`, `geometry`, `componentSize`, `unpackedFormat`,
  `catalogue`, `findFormat`, `MAX_COMPONENTS`; `src/math_util.h`).
- Produces: `include/dvv/media/reference.h` in full:

```cpp
#pragma once

// The scalar CPU reference (slow, obviously correct), spec §7.3: the ground truth for tests and the
// CPU fallback (CpuUnpack, Task 8).

#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <span>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/media/pixel_format.h"
#include "dvv/media/types.h"

namespace dvv::media {

// One plane of code values per component, row-major, tight, value range [0, 2^bits).
struct CodeValues {
    FormatId format = 0;
    std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    std::array<std::vector<std::uint16_t>, MAX_COMPONENTS> components{};
    bool operator==(const CodeValues&) const = default;
};

// Packs code values into the format's tight layout (geometry()); padding bits, row padding and
// samples beyond the width are 0. Errc::Internal for a value out of range or sizes that do not
// match; geometry() errors are passed through.
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::vector<std::byte>> pack(const CodeValues& values);
// Unpacks original bytes (at least geometry().bytes, tight layout) into code values.
[[nodiscard]] Result<CodeValues> unpack(FormatId format, std::uint32_t width, std::uint32_t height,
                                        std::span<const std::byte> original);
// The host form of the unpacked view (D5): planes in component order, LE 8/16-bit, tight, i.e. the
// layout of unpackedFormat(format). `planes.size()` must equal geometry(unpackedFormat(format)).bytes.
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> unpackToPlanes(FormatId format, std::uint32_t width,
                                          std::uint32_t height,
                                          std::span<const std::byte> original,
                                          std::span<std::byte> planes);

}  // namespace dvv::media
```

- Produces (private), `src/patterns.h`:

```cpp
namespace dvv::media::detail {

enum class Pattern : std::uint8_t { Gradient, Noise };

// SplitMix64 finaliser step: z += 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15; z = (z ^ (z >> 30)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9;
// z = (z ^ (z >> 27)) * 0x94D049BB133111EB; return z ^ (z >> 31). (All arithmetic mod 2^64.)
[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint64_t splitMix64(std::uint64_t z) noexcept;  // defined inline

// D16 gradient: maxv = 2^bits - 1; ramp = (x + y) * maxv / max(grid_width + grid_height - 2, 1);
// value = (ramp + uint64(index) + component * ((maxv + 1) / 4)) mod (maxv + 1).
[[nodiscard]] std::uint16_t gradientValue(std::uint8_t bits, std::size_t component,
                                          std::uint32_t x, std::uint32_t y,
                                          std::uint32_t grid_width, std::uint32_t grid_height,
                                          std::int64_t index) noexcept;
// D16 noise: h = splitMix64(seed); h = splitMix64(h ^ uint64(index)); h = splitMix64(h ^ component);
// h = splitMix64(h ^ y); h = splitMix64(h ^ x); value = h & maxv.
[[nodiscard]] std::uint16_t noiseValue(std::uint8_t bits, std::uint64_t seed, std::int64_t index,
                                       std::size_t component, std::uint32_t x,
                                       std::uint32_t y) noexcept;
// Code values of a whole frame (each component on its componentSize() grid). The geometry() checks
// apply (Format for an odd v210 width, Internal for an unknown id). `seed` is ignored for Gradient.
[[nodiscard]] Result<CodeValues> generate(Pattern pattern, FormatId format, std::uint32_t width,
                                          std::uint32_t height, std::uint64_t seed,
                                          std::int64_t index);

}  // namespace dvv::media::detail
```

- Note (plan choice): `src/patterns.cpp` is added (the design names only `src/patterns.h`). `CodeValues`
  gets a defaulted `operator==` for the tests and the GPU comparisons of Task 6. `unpackToPlanes` needs
  the exact size: a larger buffer would hide size bugs, and callers can pass a subspan.

**Behaviour:**
- `pack(v)`:
  1. `geometry(v.format, v.width, v.height)`; its error is returned unchanged.
  2. For `c < componentCount`: `v.components[c].size() == cw * ch` (`componentSize`); for `c >=
     componentCount` the vector is empty → else `Internal` `"component <c> has <n> values, expected
     <m>"`.
  3. Every value `< 2^bits` → else `Internal` `"component <c> value <v> at (<x>, <y>) does not fit in
     <bits> bits"`.
  4. Output: `std::vector<std::byte>(geometry.bytes)` zero-filled. For each plane `p`, row `r` in
     `[0, rows)`, group `g` in `[0, ceilDiv(width, groupPixels))`: start with all words of the group at
     0. For each slot `{c, i, word, shift}`: `xc = g * (groupPixels >> log2SubX(c)) + i`; when `xc < cw`,
     OR `value(c, xc, r) << shift` into the word (in `std::uint32_t`). Then write each word at
     `planeOffset + r * stride + g * groupBytes + word * wordBytes`, `wordBytes` bytes, in the plane's
     byte order. The plane's rows equal the component's rows, because `validate` ties the plane's
     `log2SubY` to its components.
- `unpack(f, w, h, original)`: `geometry` errors pass through; `original.size() < bytes` → `Internal`
  `"original has <n> bytes, expected at least <m>"`. Reads every word in the plane's byte order and
  extracts `value = (word >> shift) & ((1 << bits) - 1)` for in-width samples (`xc < cw`). Everything
  else (padding bits, row padding, samples beyond the width) is ignored. Returns `CodeValues{f, w, h,
  …}` with `componentCount` filled vectors.
- `unpackToPlanes(f, w, h, original, planes)`: `u = unpackedFormat(f)`; `geometry(u, w, h)` (errors pass
  through, but the source format's `geometry(f, w, h)` is checked first, so an odd v210 width is
  `Format`); `planes.size() != geometry(u).bytes` → `Internal` `"planes need exactly <m> bytes, got
  <n>"`. Result: `unpack` then `pack` with `format = u`, copied into `planes`. A direct loop is allowed if it
  gives identical bytes.
- Generators as in `patterns.h`. `generate` fills components `0..componentCount-1` on their grids,
  row-major. `gradientValue` and `noiseValue` compute in `std::uint64_t` and never fail.
- None of these functions throws, except `std::bad_alloc`.

**FFmpeg layouts for the goldens** (the source for the hand derivation, D4/R8). Derive every golden from
this table and from FFmpeg's semantics, never from the descriptor table of Task 2 and never by calling
`pack`. FFmpeg's `AVComponentDescriptor` is `{plane, step, offset, shift, depth}`. Component sample `x` (in
the component's own grid) of row `y` belongs to the `step` bytes that start at byte `x * step` of row `y` of
`plane`. For little-endian and 8-bit formats it occupies bits `[8 * offset + shift, 8 * offset + shift +
depth)` of the little-endian integer formed by those bytes. For the big-endian formats (all 16-bit
containers) it occupies bits `[shift, shift + depth)` of the big-endian 16-bit word at byte `x * step +
offset`. Order in pixdesc is Y,U,V or R,G,B(,A). "chroma w,h" are FFmpeg's `log2_chroma_w` and
`log2_chroma_h`.

| format | components {plane, step, offset, shift, depth} | chroma w,h | flags |
|---|---|---|---|
| gray | Y{0,1,0,0,8} | – | |
| gray10le / gray12le / gray16le / gray16be | Y{0,2,0,0,10/12/16/16} | – | gray16be: BE |
| yuv420p / yuv422p / yuv444p | Y{0,1,0,0,8} U{1,1,0,0,8} V{2,1,0,0,8} | 1,1 / 1,0 / 0,0 | |
| yuv4xxp10le / 12le / 16le, yuv420p10be | Y{0,2,0,0,d} U{1,2,0,0,d} V{2,2,0,0,d} | per name | 10be: BE |
| nv12 | Y{0,1,0,0,8} U{1,2,0,0,8} V{1,2,1,0,8} | 1,1 | |
| nv21 | Y{0,1,0,0,8} U{1,2,1,0,8} V{1,2,0,0,8} | 1,1 | |
| p010le | Y{0,2,0,6,10} U{1,4,0,6,10} V{1,4,2,6,10} | 1,1 | |
| p016le | Y{0,2,0,0,16} U{1,4,0,0,16} V{1,4,2,0,16} | 1,1 | |
| yuyv422 | Y{0,2,0,0,8} U{0,4,1,0,8} V{0,4,3,0,8} | 1,0 | |
| uyvy422 | Y{0,2,1,0,8} U{0,4,0,0,8} V{0,4,2,0,8} | 1,0 | |
| y210le | Y{0,4,0,6,10} U{0,8,2,6,10} V{0,8,6,6,10} | 1,0 | |
| rgb24 / bgr24 | R{0,3,0/2,0,8} G{0,3,1,0,8} B{0,3,2/0,0,8} | – | |
| rgba / bgra / argb | R{0,4,0/2/1} G{0,4,1/1/2} B{0,4,2/0/3} A{0,4,3/3/0}, depth 8 | – | |
| rgb48le / rgb48be | R{0,6,0,0,16} G{0,6,2,0,16} B{0,6,4,0,16} | – | 48be: BE |
| rgba64le | R{0,8,0} G{0,8,2} B{0,8,4} A{0,8,6}, depth 16 | – | |
| x2rgb10le | R{0,4,2,4,10} G{0,4,1,2,10} B{0,4,0,0,10} (bits 20, 10, 0 of the LE 32-bit pixel; top 2 bits X = 0) | – | |
| gbrp / gbrp10le / gbrp16le | R{2,s,0,0,d} G{0,s,0,0,d} B{1,s,0,0,d}, s = 1/2/2, d = 8/10/16 | – | |
| v210 | (`v210enc.c`, `v210_template.c`) per 6 pixels four LE 32-bit words, each `a | b << 10 | c << 20` with (a, b, c) = (Cb0, Y0, Cr0), (Y1, Cb1, Y2), (Cr1, Y3, Cb2), (Y4, Cr2, Y5); row stride `ceil(w / 48) * 128` (`v210dec.c`); for a last group of 2 pixels the encoder writes word 0 and word 1 = Y1 only; for 4 pixels words 0, 1 and word 2 = Cr1 | Y3 << 10; every other byte of the row is 0 | 1,0 | |

**Golden values** (in `test_layout_goldens.cpp`): component `c`, sample `n` (row-major in the component's
own grid), `bits` of that component:

```cpp
// Distinct code values: the top 4 bits name the component (c + 1), the low 4 bits the sample
// (n + 1), and the bits between alternate 1010... so byte-order and shift mistakes show.
std::uint16_t goldenValue(std::size_t c, std::size_t n, unsigned bits) {
    const unsigned pattern = 0xAAAAU & ((1U << (bits - 8U)) - 1U);  // 0 for 8 bits
    return static_cast<std::uint16_t>(((c + 1U) << (bits - 4U)) | (pattern << 4U) | (n + 1U));
}
```

So 8-bit values are `0x11, 0x12, …` (Y/R), `0x21…` (U/G), `0x31…` (V/B), `0x41…` (A); 10-bit `0x061…`,
`0x0A1…`, `0x0E1…`; 12-bit `0x1A1…`; 16-bit `0x1AA1…`, `0x2AA1…`, `0x3AA1…`, `0x4AA1…`.

Frames: 2×2 for every format except v210, which has 6×1 plus the partial-group goldens 2×1 and 4×1. That
makes 39 goldens. A golden is
`struct Golden { std::string_view format; std::uint32_t width, height; std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes; }`.
`bytes` is the whole frame, except for v210, where it lists a prefix (16, 8 or 12 bytes for 6×1, 2×1,
4×1); tests 2 and 3 zero-extend it to `geometry().bytes` (128).

Worked goldens. These were computed while the plan was written; write them exactly, and derive the other
27 the same way by hand:
- gray12le 2×2: `A1 01 A2 01 A3 01 A4 01`
- gray16be 2×2: `1A A1 1A A2 1A A3 1A A4`
- yuv420p10le 2×2: `61 00 62 00 63 00 64 00 | A1 00 | E1 00`
- nv21 2×2: `11 12 13 14 | 31 21`
- p010le 2×2: `40 18 80 18 C0 18 00 19 | 40 28 40 38`
- yuyv422 2×2: `11 21 12 31 13 22 14 32` (U and V grids are 1×2: row 1 uses U n=1, V n=1)
- rgb48be 2×2: `1A A1 2A A1 3A A1 1A A2 2A A2 3A A2 1A A3 2A A3 3A A3 1A A4 2A A4 3A A4`
- x2rgb10le 2×2: `E1 84 12 06 E2 88 22 06 E3 8C 32 06 E4 90 42 06`
- gbrp 2×2: `21 22 23 24 | 31 32 33 34 | 11 12 13 14`
- v210 6×1: `A1 84 11 0E 62 88 32 06 E2 90 31 0A 65 8C 63 06`, then 112 zero bytes
- v210 2×1: `A1 84 11 0E 62 00 00 00`, then zeros to 128
- v210 4×1: `A1 84 11 0E 62 88 32 06 E2 90 01 00`, then zeros to 128

(`|` marks plane boundaries for readability; the arrays are contiguous.)

**Tests first** (the first failure is a compile error: `dvv/media/reference.h` and `patterns.h` are missing):

`tests/test_layout_goldens.cpp`:
1. `every format has a layout golden`: the golden list has 39 entries; the set of their format names
   equals the 37 catalogue names; v210 appears with 6×1, 2×1 and 4×1.
2. `pack produces the golden bytes`: for each golden, build `CodeValues` with `goldenValue` on each
   component grid; `pack(values)` equals the golden bytes (v210 zero-extended to 128).
   On a mismatch, print format, offset, expected and actual byte.
3. `unpack reads the golden values`: `unpack(format, w, h, goldenBytes) == values` for each golden.

`tests/test_reference.cpp`:
4. `pattern generators`:
   - `splitMix64(0) == 0xE220A8397B1DCDAF`, `splitMix64(1) == 0x910A2DEC89025CC1`.
   - `noiseValue(10, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0) == 0x0E5`, `noiseValue(16, 1, 0, 0, 1, 0) == 0xC8F9`.
   - `gradientValue(8, 0, 0, 0, 33, 17, 0) == 0`, `gradientValue(8, 0, 32, 16, 33, 17, 0) == 255`,
     `gradientValue(8, 1, 0, 0, 33, 17, 0) == 64`, `gradientValue(8, 0, 0, 0, 33, 17, 1) == 1`,
     `gradientValue(10, 3, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0) == 768`.
   - `generate(Noise, yuv420p10le, 33, 17, 1, 0)` twice is equal; with seed 2 it differs; every value
     is < 1024.
   - `generate(Noise, gray16le, 64, 64, 1, 0)` has a value ≥ 0x8000.
   - `generate(Gradient, v210, 33, 17, 1, 0)` is `Format`.
5. `round trip for every format`: for each catalogue id, sizes 1×1, 2×2, 33×17, 49×3, 64×64 (v210: 2×2,
   34×17, 50×3, 64×64), seeds 1 and 2: `x = generate(Noise, id, w, h, seed, 0)`, `b = pack(x)`, `b.size()
   == geometry(id, w, h)->bytes`, `unpack(id, w, h, b) == x`.
6. `pack of unpack clears exactly the padding`: for each catalogue id at 33×17 (v210 34×17): `b` = random
   bytes of `geometry().bytes`, filled from a `splitMix64` stream seeded 7. Let `mask = pack(all values =
   2^bits − 1)`; then `pack(*unpack(id, w, h, b)) == (b & mask)` byte by byte. Also, for v210 at 34×17,
   `mask` is 0 in bytes 92..127 of every 128-byte row (the unused fourth word of the partial last group
   and the row padding), and byte 3 of row 0 is `0x3F` (bits 30–31 of every word are padding).
7. `unpacked planes equal packing in the unpacked format`: for each catalogue id at 33×17 (v210 34×17)
   with noise `x`: `unpackToPlanes(id, w, h, pack(x), planes)` succeeds into a buffer of
   `geometry(unpackedFormat(id), w, h)->bytes`, and `planes == pack(CodeValues{unpackedFormat(id), w, h,
   x.components})`.
8. `v210 planes are a yuv422p10le frame`: `x` = noise for v210 at 34×17;
   `unpackToPlanes(v210, …, pack(x), planes)` equals `pack(CodeValues{yuv422p10le, 34, 17,
   x.components})`.
9. `errors`:
   - `pack` with a 10-bit value 1024 → `Internal`, message contains `"does not fit in 10 bits"`.
   - a component with one value too few → `Internal` ("expected").
   - `components[3]` non-empty for yuv420p → `Internal`.
   - `pack` for v210 33×1 → `Format`.
   - `unpack` with one byte too few → `Internal` ("expected at least").
   - `unpackToPlanes` with a buffer one byte too large → `Internal` ("exactly").
   - `unpack` for id 100000 → `Internal`.
