### Task 3b: Custom formats as JSON

**Goal:** Serialise any interned descriptor to JSON, and parse, validate and intern a custom descriptor
from JSON (spec §7.3, D3). The schema is documented in the dvv-media README. The CLI (`fmt=@<file>`, Task
11) and `info --json` (Task 13) build on it.

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/format_json.h`: the public API (below).
- Create `dvv-media/src/format_json.cpp`: conversion both ways and the strict parser.
- Modify `dvv-media/src/format_table.h` and `src/pixel_format.cpp`: add `detail::internCustomFormat`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_format_json.cpp`.
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt` (add `src/format_json.cpp`), `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (add the test).
- Modify `dvv-media/README.md`: new section "Custom pixel formats (JSON)".

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (`PixelFormatDescriptor`, `validate`, the format table, `findFormat`, `descriptor`,
  `catalogue`), Task 3 (`pack`/`unpack` for the custom-format test), dvv-base `dvv::json` (`Value`,
  `parse`, `dump`, `get<T>`).
- Produces: `include/dvv/media/format_json.h` in full:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Custom pixel formats as JSON (spec §7.3, M2 design D3). The schema is in the dvv-media README.

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/json.h"
#include "dvv/media/types.h"

namespace dvv::media {

// Every key written explicitly, defaults included. An unknown id gives a JSON null.
[[nodiscard]] json::Value formatToJson(FormatId format);
// Parses strictly, validates (validate(), incl. the GPU limits) and interns. Idempotent: an equal
// descriptor already interned under that name returns its id. Errc::Format for a malformed value,
// an invalid descriptor, a catalogue name, a name ending in "@planes", or a different descriptor
// already interned under that name.
[[nodiscard]] Result<FormatId> formatFromJson(const json::Value& value);

}  // namespace dvv::media
```

- Produces (private), added to `src/format_table.h`:

```cpp
// Interns a validated custom descriptor. Under the table's unique lock: a catalogue name → Format
// "'<name>' is a built-in format"; an interned non-catalogue entry with the same name → its id when
// the descriptors are equal, else Format "a different format named '<name>' is already loaded";
// otherwise appended (unpacked = false).
[[nodiscard]] Result<FormatId> internCustomFormat(const PixelFormatDescriptor& descriptor);
```

- Note (plan choice): the parser is strict. Unknown keys are `Format`, so a typo such as `"bitz"` is
  reported instead of silently taking a default. The design does not mention a schema version key, so
  none is added.
- Note (plan choice): idempotent interning (the arbiter's minor note). Loading the same JSON twice, for
  example from two URIs, gives one id.
- Note (plan choice): names ending in `@planes` are rejected, because that suffix is reserved for
  unpacked formats. Every other name rule comes from `validate()`.

**Behaviour:**

*Schema* (all keys camelCase, as in the C++ structs):

```json
{
  "name": "p010le",
  "model": "yuv",
  "widthMultiple": 1,
  "components": [
    {"kind": "Y", "bits": 10, "log2SubX": 0, "log2SubY": 0},
    {"kind": "U", "bits": 10, "log2SubX": 1, "log2SubY": 1},
    {"kind": "V", "bits": 10, "log2SubX": 1, "log2SubY": 1}
  ],
  "planes": [
    {"groupPixels": 1, "groupBytes": 2, "wordBytes": 2, "byteOrder": "little", "rowAlign": 1,
     "log2SubY": 0, "slots": [{"component": 0, "index": 0, "word": 0, "shift": 6}]},
    {"groupPixels": 2, "groupBytes": 4, "wordBytes": 2, "byteOrder": "little", "rowAlign": 1,
     "log2SubY": 1, "slots": [{"component": 1, "index": 0, "word": 0, "shift": 6},
                              {"component": 2, "index": 0, "word": 1, "shift": 6}]}
  ]
}
```

- Required: `name` (string), `model` (`"gray"` | `"yuv"` | `"rgb"`), `components` (array, 1..4),
  `planes` (array, 1..4); per component `kind` (`"Y" "U" "V" "R" "G" "B" "A"`) and `bits`; per plane
  `groupPixels`, `groupBytes`, `wordBytes` and `slots` (array, 1..12); per slot all four keys.
- Optional with defaults: `widthMultiple` 1; `log2SubX`/`log2SubY` 0; `byteOrder` `"little"` (or `"big"`);
  `rowAlign` 1; plane `log2SubY` 0.
- Integers are read with `json::get<std::uint8_t>` (`rowAlign`: `std::uint16_t`), which rejects floats,
  booleans and out-of-range values.
- `componentCount`, `planeCount` and `slotCount` come from the array sizes. Unused array entries stay
  value-initialised.

*formatToJson(id)*: writes the schema with every key, defaults included, in the descriptor's order of
components, planes and slots. An unknown id returns `json::Value()` (null). It works for catalogue,
unpacked and custom formats.

*formatFromJson(value)*, in order:
1. Structural parse. On error: `Format` `"custom pixel format: <path>: <reason>"`. `<path>` is like `planes[1].slots[0].shift`
   (or `name`). `<reason>` is the `json::get` message, or `"not an object"`, `"unknown key '<k>'"`,
   `"expected 1 to 4 entries"`, `"expected 1 to 12 entries"`, or `"unknown value '<v>'"` (for model, kind
   and byteOrder).
2. A name ending in `@planes` → `Format` `"custom pixel format '<name>': the suffix @planes is reserved"`.
3. `validate(descriptor)` → its `Format` error unchanged.
4. `detail::internCustomFormat(descriptor)` → its id or its `Format` error.

*README section* "Custom pixel formats (JSON)": the model in two sentences (groups, words, slots), the
schema above as the example, a key table (key, type, range, default), the rules (canonical component order
per model; one plane per component; at most 6 samples of a component per group; names `[A-Za-z0-9_.-]`, 1–64
bytes; built-in names and `@planes` reserved; loading the same JSON twice is fine), and how URIs use it
(`file:clip.yuv?fmt=@my_format.json&size=1920x1080`, Task 11). The full README comes in Task 15.

**Tests first** (`tests/test_format_json.cpp`; the first failure is a compile error: `format_json.h` is
missing):
1. `formatToJson writes every key`: `formatToJson(findFormat("p010le").value())` equals
   `json::parse(<the schema example above>)`. The comparison is of `json::Value`s, so key order does not
   matter.
2. `every catalogue entry round-trips`: for each catalogue id, take `j = formatToJson(id)` and set
   `j["name"] = name + "_copy"`. Then `formatFromJson(j)` succeeds, and `descriptor(new id)` equals
   `descriptor(id)` except for the name (compare with the name patched back). `formatToJson(new id)`
   equals `j`.
3. `defaults`: the p010le JSON with the optional keys removed (`widthMultiple`, `log2SubX`/`log2SubY` of
   Y, `byteOrder`, `rowAlign`, the plane `log2SubY` of plane 0), under the name `p010_min`, parses to a
   descriptor equal to p010le's, apart from the name.
4. `interning is idempotent`: the same JSON (name `my_y210`, otherwise y210le's) twice gives the same id;
   `findFormat("my_y210")` is that id.
5. `a different descriptor under a loaded name is rejected`: after test 4's format, `my_y210` with one
   slot shift changed → `Format`, message contains `"already loaded"`.
6. `reserved names`: name `"nv12"` → `Format` ("built-in"); `"nv12@planes"` → `Format` ("reserved");
   `"a b"` → `Format` ("invalid name", from `validate`).
7. `malformed JSON values` → `Format`, each with the given substring:
   - `json::Value(42)` → "not an object".
   - missing `name` → "name".
   - extra key `"bitz": 8` in a component → "unknown key 'bitz'".
   - model `"xyz"` → "unknown value 'xyz'".
   - kind `"Q"` → "unknown value 'Q'".
   - `bits: 17` → "bits must be 1 to 16" (from `validate`).
   - 5 components → "expected 1 to 4 entries".
   - 13 slots → "expected 1 to 12 entries".
   - `shift: 300` → "planes[0].slots[0].shift".
   - `shift: -1` → "planes[0].slots[0].shift".
   - `shift: 1.5` → "planes[0].slots[0].shift".
   - `byteOrder: "middle"` → "unknown value 'middle'".
   - a gray-like format with 7 Y samples per group → "GPU limit".
8. `a custom format works with geometry and the reference`: `my_y210` from test 4 has the same
   `geometry` as y210le at 33×17. `pack` of noise in `my_y210` equals `pack` of the same code values in
   y210le, and `unpack` round-trips.
9. `concurrent interning`: 8 `std::thread`s each intern 20 custom formats `t<i>_<k>` (copies of gray10le
   with that name) while a ninth thread loops `descriptor(id).name` over the catalogue ids 1000 times.
   Results are stored and asserted after `join`: all 160 calls succeed with distinct ids, `findFormat`
   finds each, and the catalogue names read by the ninth thread were always right.

### Task 4: `FileReader` (dvv-platform)

**Goal:** Give dvv-platform a move-only file handle with blocking positional reads that may run on several threads at once, plus aligned host memory. Reads are unbuffered when the file system allows it, and buffered otherwise (M2 design D8, §4.1).

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-platform/include/dvv/platform/file_reader.h`: the public API (`FileReadMode`, `FileReader`, `AlignedBuffer`, `allocateAligned`).
- Create `dvv-platform/src/file_reader_detail.h`: the private platform primitives and the test seam `openWith(path, mode, opener)`.
- Create `dvv-platform/src/file_reader.cpp`: shared logic (open with probe and fallback, `readAt` checks, moves, `allocateAligned`).
- Create `dvv-platform/src/file_reader_win32.cpp`: `CreateFileW` with `FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED`, per-call event, `GetOverlappedResult(…, TRUE)`.
- Create `dvv-platform/src/file_reader_posix.cpp`: `open(O_DIRECT)`, `pread`, `statx(STATX_DIOALIGN)`.
- Modify `dvv-platform/CMakeLists.txt`: add `src/file_reader.cpp` to `dvv_platform`, plus `src/file_reader_win32.cpp` under `if(WIN32)` and `src/file_reader_posix.cpp` otherwise.
- Create `dvv-platform/tests/test_file_reader.cpp`. Modify `dvv-platform/tests/CMakeLists.txt` to add the source to the unit-test executable that M1b created (`dvv_platform_tests`, CTest `dvv_platform.unit`; if M1b named it differently, use M1b's name). Also add `target_include_directories(<that target> PRIVATE ${dvv_platform_SOURCE_DIR}/src)` if it is missing, because the seam header is private.
- Modify `dvv-platform/README.md`: add a "FileReader" section. It lists the open flags of the table below, the alignment rule, the buffered fallback with its probe, and the rule "run `readAt` on an I/O pool".

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: branch `m2` of dvv-platform (Task 0); the M1b skeleton of dvv-platform (target `dvv_platform`, alias `dvv::platform`, PUBLIC `dvv::base`, unit-test executable); dvv-base `Result`, `makeError`, `Errc`, `DVV_LOG_DEBUG`.
- Produces: `dvv::platform::FileReader`, `FileReadMode`, `AlignedBuffer`, `AlignedBufferDeleter` and `allocateAligned`. Task 7a uses them (`HostFrame` memory, `ProduceContext::allocateHost`), Task 9 (`SourceFactory` head read, buffered), and Tasks 11 and 12 (raw and Y4M reads).

Public header `include/dvv/platform/file_reader.h` (complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

// Positional file reads for frame data (M2 design D8, spec §10.4). A FileReader is a move-only
// handle whose blocking readAt() may run concurrently on several threads (no shared file
// position). Callers run reads on an I/O thread pool (co_await resumeOn(io)).
//
// Open flags: Windows CreateFileW(GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, OPEN_EXISTING,
// FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED [+ FILE_FLAG_NO_BUFFERING when unbuffered]); each read has its own
// OVERLAPPED and event (low bit set, no completion port) and waits in GetOverlappedResult(TRUE).
// Linux open(O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC [| O_DIRECT when unbuffered]) and pread().

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <memory>
#include <span>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"

namespace dvv::platform {

namespace detail {
struct FileReaderAccess;
}  // namespace detail

enum class FileReadMode : std::uint8_t {
    Unbuffered,  // try FILE_FLAG_NO_BUFFERING / O_DIRECT, fall back to buffered on refusal
    Buffered,    // plain reads (small header reads, tests of the fallback)
};

class FileReader {
public:
    // Errc::Io "'<path>' could not be opened: <system message>" (missing, denied, a directory).
    // The size is read once at open. Unbuffered: after the open succeeds and the file is not
    // empty, a probe reads the first alignment() bytes. Any error of the unbuffered open retries
    // buffered; only the probe read's refusal is limited to alignmentRefusedError(). A fallback
    // reopens buffered with a debug log line.
    [[nodiscard]] static Result<FileReader> open(const std::filesystem::path& path,
                                                 FileReadMode mode = FileReadMode::Unbuffered);
    FileReader(FileReader&& other) noexcept;
    FileReader& operator=(FileReader&& other) noexcept;
    FileReader(const FileReader&) = delete;
    FileReader& operator=(const FileReader&) = delete;
    ~FileReader();

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t size() const noexcept { return size_; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool unbuffered() const noexcept { return unbuffered_; }  // false after fallback
    // Required alignment of offset, length and destination address: 1 when buffered; otherwise
    // max(4096, the volume's sector size) (Windows FileStorageInfo, Linux statx STATX_DIOALIGN).
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t alignment() const noexcept { return alignment_; }
    // Blocking read of destination.size() bytes at `offset`; returns the bytes read, fewer only at
    // the end of the file (0 at or past it). Errc::Internal for a misaligned offset, size or
    // address (unbuffered only) and for a moved-from reader; Errc::Io for a system error (the
    // message names the file and the offset). Retries EINTR and partial reads; never reopens.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<std::size_t> readAt(std::uint64_t offset,
                                             std::span<std::byte> destination) const;
    [[nodiscard]] const std::filesystem::path& path() const noexcept { return path_; }

private:
    friend struct detail::FileReaderAccess;
    FileReader(std::intptr_t handle, std::filesystem::path path, std::uint64_t size,
               std::uint32_t alignment, bool unbuffered) noexcept;
    void close() noexcept;

    std::intptr_t handle_ = -1;  // HANDLE (Windows) or file descriptor (Linux); -1: none
    std::filesystem::path path_;
    std::uint64_t size_ = 0;
    std::uint32_t alignment_ = 1;
    bool unbuffered_ = false;
};

// Aligned host memory for unbuffered reads (operator new[] with std::align_val_t).
struct AlignedBufferDeleter {
    std::size_t alignment = 1;
    void operator()(std::byte* memory) const noexcept;
};
using AlignedBuffer = std::unique_ptr<std::byte[], AlignedBufferDeleter>;
// Errc::Internal for 0 bytes or an alignment that is not a power of two; Errc::OutOfMemory when
// the allocation fails.
[[nodiscard]] Result<AlignedBuffer> allocateAligned(std::size_t bytes, std::size_t alignment);

}  // namespace dvv::platform
```

Note (plan choice): the design's "move-only like `DynamicLibrary`" is kept. The native handle is an `std::intptr_t` (`INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE` and an invalid file descriptor are both -1), so the public header needs no platform `#ifdef`.

Private header `src/file_reader_detail.h` (complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <functional>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <utility>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/platform/file_reader.h"

namespace dvv::platform::detail {

inline constexpr std::intptr_t NO_HANDLE = -1;

struct NativeOpen {
    std::intptr_t handle = NO_HANDLE;
    int error = 0;  // errno or GetLastError() when handle == NO_HANDLE
};
struct NativeSize {
    std::uint64_t size = 0;
    int error = 0;  // EISDIR / ERROR_* when the handle is not a readable regular file
};
struct NativeRead {
    std::size_t bytes = 0;  // bytes read before the error or the end of the file
    int error = 0;
};

// Platform parts (file_reader_win32.cpp, file_reader_posix.cpp).
[[nodiscard]] NativeOpen openNative(const std::filesystem::path& path, bool unbuffered) noexcept;
void closeNative(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] NativeSize fileSize(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t unbufferedAlignment(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept;  // >= 4096
// Reads until `destination` is full or offset + bytes reaches `file_size`; retries EINTR and
// partial reads.
[[nodiscard]] NativeRead readNative(std::intptr_t handle, std::uint64_t offset,
                                    std::span<std::byte> destination,
                                    std::uint64_t file_size) noexcept;
// EINVAL / ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER: the file system refuses unbuffered I/O.
[[nodiscard]] int alignmentRefusedError() noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] std::string systemMessage(int error);  // UTF-8, with the number

// UTF-8 for messages (path::u8string, never path::string, which uses the ANSI code page).
[[nodiscard]] std::string displayPath(const std::filesystem::path& path);

// FileReader::open with a replaceable opener: the test seam of the buffered fallback (D8).
using Opener = std::function<NativeOpen(const std::filesystem::path&, bool unbuffered)>;
[[nodiscard]] Result<FileReader> openWith(const std::filesystem::path& path, FileReadMode mode,
                                          const Opener& opener);

struct FileReaderAccess {
    [[nodiscard]] static FileReader make(std::intptr_t handle, std::filesystem::path path,
                                         std::uint64_t size, std::uint32_t alignment,
                                         bool unbuffered) noexcept {
        return FileReader(handle, std::move(path), size, alignment, unbuffered);
    }
};

}  // namespace dvv::platform::detail
```

**Behaviour:**
- `open(path, Buffered)` opens buffered. It fails with `Io "'<path>' could not be opened: <system message>"` when the opener fails, or when `fileSize` reports an error (on Linux a directory gives `EISDIR`; on Windows `CreateFileW` refuses a directory with `ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED`). `alignment() == 1` and `unbuffered() == false`.
- `open(path, Unbuffered)` follows these rules in order:
  1. `opener(path, true)` fails with any error: debug log `"'<path>': unbuffered open refused (<message>); reading buffered"`, then the buffered open (rule above).
  2. The open succeeds: `fileSize` (an error closes the handle and gives `Io`), then `alignment = unbufferedAlignment(handle)`.
  3. If `size > 0`, the probe reads `alignment` bytes at offset 0 into `allocateAligned(alignment, alignment)`. If the probe error equals `alignmentRefusedError()`: close the handle, debug log `"'<path>': unbuffered reads refused (<message>); reading buffered"`, then the buffered open. Any other probe error: close the handle and return `Io "reading '<path>' at offset 0 failed: <message>"`.
  4. Otherwise the result is an unbuffered reader with that alignment.
- `readAt(offset, destination)`:
  - A moved-from reader (`handle_ == -1`) returns `Internal "'<path>': the reader is closed"`.
  - Unbuffered: `offset`, `destination.size()` and the address must be multiples of `alignment()`, otherwise `Internal "misaligned unbuffered read of '<path>': offset <o>, <n> bytes, address mod <a> = <r>"`.
  - An empty destination returns 0.
  - Otherwise `readNative(handle_, offset, destination, size_)`. An error gives `Io "reading '<path>' at offset <offset + bytes> failed: <message>"`; success gives the bytes read.
- Reads stop at `size_` (the size at open). This also keeps an unbuffered read from continuing at an unaligned offset after a short read at the end of the file.
- Moves transfer the handle. The moved-from reader has `handle_ == -1`, an empty path, size 0 and alignment 1. Move assignment closes the old handle first. The destructor closes.
- `allocateAligned` behaves as stated in the header.

**Code (platform parts; binding):**

`src/file_reader_win32.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <format>
#include <span>
#include <string>

#ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#endif
#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#include <windows.h>

#include "file_reader_detail.h"

namespace dvv::platform::detail {

namespace {

HANDLE toHandle(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept {
    return reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(handle);  // NOLINT(performance-no-int-to-ptr)
}

// One ReadFile moves at most this much (a DWORD count); a multiple of every alignment.
constexpr std::size_t MAX_CHUNK = std::size_t{1} << 30;

}  // namespace

NativeOpen openNative(const std::filesystem::path& path, bool unbuffered) noexcept {
    DWORD flags = FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL | FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED;
    if (unbuffered) {
        flags |= FILE_FLAG_NO_BUFFERING;
    }
    const HANDLE file = CreateFileW(path.c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
                                    nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, flags, nullptr);
    if (file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        return {.handle = NO_HANDLE, .error = static_cast<int>(GetLastError())};
    }
    return {.handle = reinterpret_cast<std::intptr_t>(file), .error = 0};
}

void closeNative(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept {
    if (handle != NO_HANDLE) {
        CloseHandle(toHandle(handle));
    }
}

NativeSize fileSize(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept {
    LARGE_INTEGER size{};
    if (GetFileSizeEx(toHandle(handle), &size) == 0) {
        return {.size = 0, .error = static_cast<int>(GetLastError())};
    }
    return {.size = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(size.QuadPart), .error = 0};
}

std::uint32_t unbufferedAlignment(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept {
    FILE_STORAGE_INFO info{};
    std::uint32_t sector = 0;
    if (GetFileInformationByHandleEx(toHandle(handle), FileStorageInfo, &info, sizeof(info)) != 0) {
        sector = std::max<std::uint32_t>(info.LogicalBytesPerSector,
                                         info.PhysicalBytesPerSectorForAtomicity);
    }
    return std::max<std::uint32_t>(4096, sector);
}

NativeRead readNative(std::intptr_t handle, std::uint64_t offset, std::span<std::byte> destination,
                      std::uint64_t file_size) noexcept {
    const HANDLE file = toHandle(handle);
    // Its own manual-reset event: concurrent reads on the handle never wait for each other.
    const HANDLE event = CreateEventW(nullptr, TRUE, FALSE, nullptr);
    if (event == nullptr) {
        return {.bytes = 0, .error = static_cast<int>(GetLastError())};
    }
    std::size_t done = 0;
    int error = 0;
    while (done < destination.size() && offset + done < file_size) {
        const auto chunk =
            static_cast<DWORD>(std::min<std::size_t>(destination.size() - done, MAX_CHUNK));
        const std::uint64_t at = offset + done;
        OVERLAPPED overlapped{};
        overlapped.Offset = static_cast<DWORD>(at & 0xFFFFFFFFU);
        overlapped.OffsetHigh = static_cast<DWORD>(at >> 32);
        // Low bit set: no completion-port notification (none is attached; D8 says so explicitly).
        overlapped.hEvent = reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(  // NOLINT(performance-no-int-to-ptr)
            reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(event) | 1U);
        ResetEvent(event);
        if (ReadFile(file, destination.data() + done, chunk, nullptr, &overlapped) == 0) {
            const DWORD failed = GetLastError();
            if (failed == ERROR_HANDLE_EOF) {
                break;
            }
            if (failed != ERROR_IO_PENDING) {
                error = static_cast<int>(failed);
                break;
            }
        }
        DWORD transferred = 0;
        if (GetOverlappedResult(file, &overlapped, &transferred, TRUE) == 0) {
            const DWORD failed = GetLastError();
            if (failed != ERROR_HANDLE_EOF) {
                error = static_cast<int>(failed);
            }
            break;
        }
        if (transferred == 0) {
            break;  // end of file
        }
        done += transferred;
    }
    CloseHandle(event);
    return {.bytes = done, .error = error};
}

int alignmentRefusedError() noexcept {
    return ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER;
}

std::string systemMessage(int error) {
    wchar_t* text = nullptr;
    const DWORD length = FormatMessageW(
        FORMAT_MESSAGE_ALLOCATE_BUFFER | FORMAT_MESSAGE_FROM_SYSTEM | FORMAT_MESSAGE_IGNORE_INSERTS,
        nullptr, static_cast<DWORD>(error), 0, reinterpret_cast<LPWSTR>(&text), 0, nullptr);
    std::string message;
    if (length > 0 && text != nullptr) {
        const int bytes = WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, text, static_cast<int>(length), nullptr,
                                              0, nullptr, nullptr);
        if (bytes > 0) {
            message.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(bytes));
            WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, text, static_cast<int>(length), message.data(), bytes,
                                nullptr, nullptr);
        }
    }
    if (text != nullptr) {
        LocalFree(text);
    }
    while (!message.empty() && (message.back() == '\n' || message.back() == '\r' ||
                                message.back() == ' ' || message.back() == '.')) {
        message.pop_back();
    }
    if (message.empty()) {
        message = "system error";
    }
    return std::format("{} (error {})", message, error);
}

}  // namespace dvv::platform::detail
```

If M1b's `DynamicLibrary` already added a private UTF-8 system-message helper to dvv-platform, call that helper from `systemMessage` instead of duplicating it.

`src/file_reader_posix.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <format>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <system_error>

#include "file_reader_detail.h"

namespace dvv::platform::detail {

namespace {
constexpr std::size_t MAX_CHUNK = std::size_t{1} << 30;  // below Linux's 0x7ffff000 per call
}  // namespace

NativeOpen openNative(const std::filesystem::path& path, bool unbuffered) noexcept {
    int flags = O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC;
    if (unbuffered) {
        flags |= O_DIRECT;
    }
    int fd = -1;
    do {
        fd = ::open(path.c_str(), flags);  // NOLINT(cppcoreguidelines-pro-type-vararg)
    } while (fd < 0 && errno == EINTR);
    if (fd < 0) {
        return {.handle = NO_HANDLE, .error = errno};
    }
    return {.handle = fd, .error = 0};
}

void closeNative(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept {
    if (handle != NO_HANDLE) {
        ::close(static_cast<int>(handle));
    }
}

NativeSize fileSize(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept {
    struct stat info {};
    if (::fstat(static_cast<int>(handle), &info) != 0) {
        return {.size = 0, .error = errno};
    }
    if (S_ISDIR(info.st_mode)) {
        return {.size = 0, .error = EISDIR};
    }
    return {.size = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(info.st_size), .error = 0};
}

std::uint32_t unbufferedAlignment(std::intptr_t handle) noexcept {
    std::uint32_t alignment = 4096;
#if defined(STATX_DIOALIGN)
    struct statx info {};
    if (::statx(static_cast<int>(handle), "", AT_EMPTY_PATH, STATX_DIOALIGN, &info) == 0 &&
        (info.stx_mask & STATX_DIOALIGN) != 0) {
        alignment = std::max({alignment, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(info.stx_dio_offset_align),
                              static_cast<std::uint32_t>(info.stx_dio_mem_align)});
    }
#endif
    return alignment;
}

NativeRead readNative(std::intptr_t handle, std::uint64_t offset, std::span<std::byte> destination,
                      std::uint64_t file_size) noexcept {
    std::size_t done = 0;
    while (done < destination.size() && offset + done < file_size) {
        const std::size_t chunk = std::min(destination.size() - done, MAX_CHUNK);
        const ssize_t read = ::pread(static_cast<int>(handle), destination.data() + done, chunk,
                                     static_cast<off_t>(offset + done));
        if (read < 0) {
            if (errno == EINTR) {
                continue;
            }
            return {.bytes = done, .error = errno};
        }
        if (read == 0) {
            break;  // end of file
        }
        done += static_cast<std::size_t>(read);
    }
    return {.bytes = done, .error = 0};
}

int alignmentRefusedError() noexcept {
    return EINVAL;
}

std::string systemMessage(int error) {
    return std::format("{} (errno {})", std::generic_category().message(error), error);
}

}  // namespace dvv::platform::detail
```

`src/file_reader.cpp` holds `displayPath` (via `path.u8string()`), `openWith` (the open rules above, with a small RAII guard that calls `closeNative` on every early return), the constructor, moves, destructor, `close()`, `readAt` (the checks above), `FileReader::open(path, mode) { return detail::openWith(path, mode, detail::openNative); }`, and:

```cpp
Result<AlignedBuffer> allocateAligned(std::size_t bytes, std::size_t alignment) {
    if (bytes == 0 || !std::has_single_bit(alignment)) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal,
                         std::format("allocateAligned: {} bytes at alignment {}: the size must not "
                                     "be 0 and the alignment must be a power of two",
                                     bytes, alignment));
    }
    void* memory = ::operator new[](bytes, std::align_val_t{alignment}, std::nothrow);
    if (memory == nullptr) {
        return makeError(Errc::OutOfMemory,
                         std::format("allocating {} bytes aligned to {} failed", bytes, alignment));
    }
    return AlignedBuffer(static_cast<std::byte*>(memory), AlignedBufferDeleter{alignment});
}

void AlignedBufferDeleter::operator()(std::byte* memory) const noexcept {
    ::operator delete[](memory, std::align_val_t{alignment});
}
```

**Tests first** (`tests/test_file_reader.cpp`, `TEST_SUITE("file_reader")`). The file has a local `TemporaryDirectory` (a unique directory under `std::filesystem::temp_directory_path()`, removed in the destructor) and `writeFile(path, bytes)` (`std::ofstream` binary). Test content is `pattern(i) = static_cast<std::byte>((i * 131 + (i >> 8) * 7) & 0xFF)`.
1. `allocateAligned returns aligned memory`: for `(bytes, alignment)` in `{(1, 1), (100, 512), (8192, 4096), (5000, 65536)}` the result has a value and `reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(p.get()) % alignment == 0`. `allocateAligned(0, 64)` and `allocateAligned(64, 3)` give `Errc::Internal`.
2. `a buffered reader reads any range`: a file of 10000 pattern bytes, opened `Buffered`. `size() == 10000`, `!unbuffered()`, `alignment() == 1`. `readAt(3, 100 bytes) == 100` with matching content. `readAt(9990, 100 bytes) == 10`. `readAt(10000, 16 bytes) == 0`. `readAt(20000, 16 bytes) == 0`.
3. `an unbuffered reader reads aligned ranges`: a file of `3 * 4096 + 123` bytes opened `Unbuffered`. On `_WIN32`: `REQUIRE(reader.unbuffered())` (NTFS temp directory). Elsewhere: `MESSAGE` the mode. When unbuffered: `alignment() >= 4096`, `alignment() % 512 == 0`. With `a = alignment()` and a buffer from `allocateAligned(alignUp(size, a), a)`: the whole read returns `size()` bytes with matching content; `readAt(a, a bytes) == a`; `readAt(alignDown(size, a), a bytes) == size % a`.
4. `a misaligned unbuffered read is Internal`: only when `unbuffered()` (else `MESSAGE` and return). `readAt(1, a bytes)`, `readAt(0, a - 1 bytes)` and `readAt(0, span(buffer.get() + 1, a))` each give `Errc::Internal` with `"misaligned"` in the message.
5. `eight threads read concurrently`: an 8 MiB pattern file, `Unbuffered`, `a = alignment()`. 8 `std::jthread`s each do 64 reads of 65536 bytes at aligned offsets from a per-thread LCG (`seed = thread + 1; seed = seed * 6364136223846793005 + 1442695040888963407; offset = ((seed >> 33) % (size / 65536)) * 65536`), each into its own `allocateAligned(65536, a)` buffer. Each thread counts its mismatches; after joining, the sum is 0. There is no timing assertion.
6. `a missing file is Io naming the file`: `open(dir / "missing.bin")` gives `Errc::Io`, and the message contains `missing.bin` and `could not be opened`.
7. `a directory is Io`: `open(dir)` in both modes gives `Errc::Io`.
8. `an empty file opens with size 0`: `Unbuffered`: has a value, `size() == 0`; a read of one aligned block returns 0.
9. `a refused unbuffered open falls back to buffered` (seam): the opener `[](auto& p, bool unbuffered) { return unbuffered ? NativeOpen{NO_HANDLE, alignmentRefusedError()} : openNative(p, false); }`. `openWith(file, Unbuffered, opener)` has a value, `!unbuffered()`, `alignment() == 1`, and `readAt(5, 7 bytes)` returns 7 matching bytes. A `dvv::MemorySink` added to `Logger::instance()` at `LogLevel::Debug` (restore the level and sinks afterwards) has a line containing `reading buffered`.
10. `an opener that fails both opens gives Io` (seam): an opener that always returns `{NO_HANDLE, alignmentRefusedError()}` gives `Errc::Io`, and the message contains the file name.
11. `a non-ASCII file name opens`: `dir / std::filesystem::path(u8"frame_ü_日本.bin")`, 100 bytes. Both modes read them back. In the missing-file variant the error message contains the UTF-8 name (`u8"ü"` as `std::string`).
12. `a moved reader keeps the file; the moved-from one is closed`: move-construct `b` from `a`. `b` reads correctly; `a.readAt(0, …)` gives `Errc::Internal` containing `closed`; `a.size() == 0`.
13. `#if defined(__linux__)` `/dev/shm works either way`: if `/dev/shm` is a directory, write a 3×4096-byte file there, open `Unbuffered`, read it back (aligned when unbuffered), `MESSAGE` the mode, and remove the file.

The first failure: after the header and an empty `file_reader.cpp` exist, the tests fail to link (`FileReader::open`, `allocateAligned` undefined). That is the expected red state.

Release, ASan (case 5 is the concurrency target), clang-tidy and the Linux compile of the POSIX path run at the Task 5 checkpoint.

**Commit:** `feat: add FileReader with unbuffered positional reads and a buffered fallback`, ending with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

**Notes:** Design §4.1, D8, D9, §7 (rows 1–4), §8.1 (dvv-platform row), R4, R5.
- `FILE_FLAG_NO_BUFFERING` requires the buffer address, offset and length to be sector multiples. `alignment()` is at least 4096, which covers 512e and 4Kn drives.
- `ERROR_HANDLE_EOF` from `ReadFile` or `GetOverlappedResult` is a short read, not an error.
- WSL drvfs (`/mnt/c`) refuses `O_DIRECT` at the open (EINVAL), so the fallback runs there. Tests never use `/mnt/c` (for when the Linux test runs return in v1.x; the temp directory is then on the Linux file system, `$TMPDIR` or `/tmp`).
- Since Linux 6.6, tmpfs accepts `O_DIRECT`. That is why the fallback is tested through the seam and `/dev/shm` is "works either way".
- Decision U-WIN: the POSIX path (`O_DIRECT`, `pread`, `statx`) is compiled and reviewed but not run in M2; running the unit tests on Linux is a v1.x item.
- Risk: a file system that accepts the flag but refuses some later reads. `readAt` returns `Io` (it never reopens); `bench` (Task 14) reports the mode.

---

### Task 5: Images in shaders through push descriptors (dvv-gpu)

**Goal:** Let compute shaders write storage images bound through Vulkan 1.4 push descriptors (set 0, `PUSH_DESCRIPTOR` layout). `GpuImage` gets an image view, pipelines declare their storage images, and `CommandRecorder::dispatch` binds images, moves them to `GENERAL` and pushes their descriptors (M2 design D6, §4.2).

**Files:**
- Modify `dvv-gpu/src/devices.h`: three required features.
- Modify `dvv-gpu/src/device_core.h`, `src/device_core.cpp`:
  - `RetiredHandle` gains `vk::ImageView` and `vk::DescriptorSetLayout`, and `destroy()` handles them.
  - New member `maxPushDescriptors()`.
- Modify `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/resources.h`, `src/resources.cpp`: `GpuImage::view()`; the constructor takes the view; the destructor retires the view before the image.
- Modify `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/compute_pipeline.h`, `src/compute_pipeline.cpp`:
  - `ComputeBindings` and `ComputePipeline::bindings()`.
  - The constructor takes the set layout and the bindings, and the destructor retires the set layout.
- Modify `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/execution_context.h`, `src/execution_context.cpp`:
  - The `createComputePipeline` overload with `ComputeBindings`, and the reflection check of descriptor bindings.
  - `createImage` creates the view and checks the format's storage support.
  - New `formatFeatures(VkFormat)`.
- Modify `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h`, `src/command_recorder.cpp`: the `dispatch` overload with storage images, and the multi-image barrier.
- Create `dvv-gpu/tests/shaders/write_image.slang` and `tests/shaders/write_two_images.slang`. Modify `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (add both to `dvv_gpu_test_shaders`, add `gpu_test_images.cpp` to `dvv_gpu_device_tests`).
- Modify `dvv-gpu/tests/test_gpu_info.cpp` (feature names) and `tests/test_shaders.cpp` (SPIR-V image format and reflection of `write_image`).
- Create `dvv-gpu/tests/gpu_test_images.cpp`.
- Modify `dvv-gpu/tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp`: case 7 passes the new constructor arguments, every one except the key.
- Modify `dvv-gpu/README.md`: a section "Images in shaders" with:
  - the Slang declaration;
  - the binding rule;
  - the layout rule (`GENERAL`);
  - the confirmed `format("unknown")` spelling;
  - the note "a storage image bound by `dispatch` is writable; M4 adds read-only bindings".
- Only if Step 3 shows the attribute does not work: `dvv-gpu/cmake/DvvShaders.cmake` gets a `SLANGC_OPTIONS` argument.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: branch `m2` of dvv-gpu (Task 0, which confirmed `pushDescriptor`, `maxPushDescriptors ≥ 32`, the two storage-image features and `STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT` for R8_UINT/R16_UINT on NVIDIA); the M1b state of dvv-gpu (depends PUBLIC on `dvv::platform`; `command_recorder.cpp` contains M1b's internal `presentBlit`, which this task leaves working).
- Produces: the API below. Task 6 uses it: `ComputeBindings{.storageImages = 1}`, the `dispatch` overload, `GpuImage::view()`, STORAGE images in R8_UINT/R16_UINT, and `formatFeatures` for the write-without-format check.

```cpp
// compute_pipeline.h (additions)
// The descriptors a compute pipeline binds (spec §5.4 as changed by M2 D6): storage images in
// set 0, bindings 0..storageImages-1, pushed per dispatch (VK_DESCRIPTOR_SET_LAYOUT_CREATE_PUSH_DESCRIPTOR_BIT).
// Pipelines without bindings have no descriptor set at all.
struct ComputeBindings {
    std::uint32_t storageImages = 0;
    bool operator==(const ComputeBindings&) const = default;
};
class ComputePipeline final : public Managed<ComputePipeline> {
public:
    ComputePipeline(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core,
                    std::string name, VkPipelineLayout layout, VkPipeline pipeline,
                    std::uint32_t push_constant_size, VkDescriptorSetLayout set_layout,
                    ComputeBindings bindings) noexcept;
    // ... existing members ...
    [[nodiscard]] const ComputeBindings& bindings() const noexcept { return bindings_; }
    // VK_NULL_HANDLE without bindings.
    [[nodiscard]] VkDescriptorSetLayout setLayout() const noexcept { return set_layout_; }
private:
    // ... existing members ..., then:
    VkDescriptorSetLayout set_layout_;
    ComputeBindings bindings_;
};

// execution_context.h (additions)
class ExecutionContext {
    // The existing overload is createComputePipeline(shader, size, ComputeBindings{}).
    // Errc::Internal when bindings.storageImages exceeds the device's maxPushDescriptors, or when
    // the shader's reflection declares descriptor bindings other than set 0, bindings
    // 0..storageImages-1 (checked when the shader has its reflection JSON).
    [[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<ComputePipeline>> createComputePipeline(
        const EmbeddedShader& shader, std::uint32_t push_constant_size, ComputeBindings bindings);
    // The device's optimal-tiling features of a format (VkFormatProperties3), e.g.
    // VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT_BIT.
    [[nodiscard]] VkFormatFeatureFlags2 formatFeatures(VkFormat format) const;
};
// createImage: VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT is accepted; the format must have
// VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_IMAGE_BIT (else Errc::Unsupported "image '<name>': device <id> has
// no storage images of <format>"). STORAGE or SAMPLED usage creates a VkImageView (2D, whole
// image, the image's format), retired with the image.

// resources.h (changes)
class GpuImage final : public Managed<GpuImage>, public std::enable_shared_from_this<GpuImage> {
public:
    GpuImage(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core,
             ImageDesc desc, VkImage image, VmaAllocation_T* allocation,
             VkImageView view) noexcept;
    // The view of the whole image; VK_NULL_HANDLE without STORAGE or SAMPLED usage.
    [[nodiscard]] VkImageView view() const noexcept { return view_; }
    // ... existing members; new private member VkImageView view_;
};

// command_recorder.h (addition)
// As dispatch() above, and binds exactly pipeline.bindings().storageImages images, binding i =
// storage_images[i]. Each image belongs to this device and generation, has STORAGE usage and
// appears once. Images not yet in GENERAL move there in the automatic barrier (tracked like every
// GpuImage layout); then the descriptors are pushed (vkCmdPushDescriptorSet, set 0) and the
// dispatch is recorded. Otherwise the recorder fails (Errc::Internal). A bound image is writable
// by the shader: do not bind an image others read (M4 adds read-only bindings).
void dispatch(const ComputePipeline& pipeline, std::span<const std::byte> push_constants,
              std::span<GpuImage* const> storage_images, std::uint32_t group_count_x,
              std::uint32_t group_count_y = 1, std::uint32_t group_count_z = 1);
```

Private additions: `DeviceCore::maxPushDescriptors() const noexcept`, and `RetiredHandle = std::variant<vk::Buffer, vk::Image, vk::ImageView, vk::Pipeline, vk::PipelineLayout, vk::DescriptorSetLayout>`.

Note (plan choice): `ExecutionContext::formatFeatures` is not in the design's §4.2 list. The design requires "the unpack pipeline setup checks `VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT_BIT`" in dvv-media, which cannot call Vulkan itself, so this one-line query is the simplest way to make that possible.

**Behaviour:**
- **Required features** (`forEachRequiredFeature`): add `visit("shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats", core.shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats)` and `visit("shaderStorageImageWriteWithoutFormat", core.shaderStorageImageWriteWithoutFormat)` right after `shaderInt64`, and `visit("pushDescriptor", v14.pushDescriptor)` after `maintenance6`. The six descriptor-indexing features stay required.
- `createComputePipeline(shader, size, bindings)`:
  - Runs the existing checks, then `bindings.storageImages > core_->maxPushDescriptors()`, which gives `Internal "shader '<name>': <n> storage images exceed the device's maxPushDescriptors (<m>)"`.
  - When the reflection JSON is present, the set of `{space, index}` of all parameters whose `binding.kind == "descriptorTableSlot"` must equal `{(0, 0) … (0, n-1)}`. Otherwise it gives `Internal "shader '<name>' declares descriptor bindings <list>; the pipeline binds <n> storage images (set 0, bindings 0..n-1)"`. An unreadable reflection gives `Format`, as for push constants.
  - With `n > 0`, a set layout with `n` bindings (`eStorageImage`, count 1, compute stage) and flag `ePushDescriptor` becomes the single set layout of the pipeline layout. With `n == 0` there is no set layout, as today.
- `createImage`:
  - STORAGE usage first checks `core_->formatFeatures(format) & VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_IMAGE_BIT`, then the existing image-format query.
  - After `vmaCreateImage` and the debug name, STORAGE or SAMPLED usage creates the view; on failure the image is destroyed and the converted Vulkan error is returned.
  - The view gets the image's debug name.
- `~GpuImage`: `retire(vk::ImageView(view_), nullptr)` (when set), **then** `retire(vk::Image(image_), allocation_)`. The retire queue frees in order (stable partition), so a view is never destroyed after its image.
- `~ComputePipeline`: retires the pipeline, the pipeline layout, then the set layout (when set).
- `dispatch` (both overloads; the old one calls the new one with an empty span):
  - Existing checks.
  - Then `storage_images.size() == pipeline.bindings().storageImages`, otherwise fail with `"dispatch: pipeline '<name>' binds <n> storage images, not <m>"`.
  - Then for each image: not null; `checkImage(…, VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT)` (device, generation, usage); `checkFormat`; not equal to an earlier image (`"dispatch: <image> appears twice"`).
  - Then one barrier with the global memory barrier and one image barrier for every image whose recorded layout is not `GENERAL`.
  - Then bind the pipeline, push descriptors (when there are images), push constants, and dispatch.

**Code (binding):**

`src/device_core.cpp`, inside `DeviceCore::create`, this replaces the `limits_` line (the device is 1.4, so the push-descriptor properties may be chained):

```cpp
    vk::StructureChain<vk::PhysicalDeviceProperties2, vk::PhysicalDevicePushDescriptorProperties>
        properties;
    core->physical_.getProperties2(&properties.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceProperties2>(),
                                   instance.dispatch());
    core->limits_ = properties.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceProperties2>().properties.limits;
    core->max_push_descriptors_ =
        properties.get<vk::PhysicalDevicePushDescriptorProperties>().maxPushDescriptors;
```

`DeviceCore::destroy` gains:

```cpp
    } else if (const auto* view = std::get_if<vk::ImageView>(&object.handle)) {
        device_.destroyImageView(*view, nullptr, dispatch_);
    } else if (const auto* set_layout = std::get_if<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>(&object.handle)) {
        device_.destroyDescriptorSetLayout(*set_layout, nullptr, dispatch_);
    }
```

`src/execution_context.cpp`: the reflection check and the layouts. These replace the layout part of today's `createComputePipeline`; the old overload becomes `return createComputePipeline(shader, push_constant_size, ComputeBindings{});`.

```cpp
// {space, index} of every descriptor binding the shader's reflection declares (parameters whose
// binding kind is descriptorTableSlot). Errc::Format when it cannot be read.
Result<std::vector<std::pair<std::uint32_t, std::uint32_t>>> reflectedDescriptorBindings(
    std::string_view reflection_json) {
    DVV_TRY(const json::Value reflection, json::parse(reflection_json));
    try {
        std::vector<std::pair<std::uint32_t, std::uint32_t>> bindings;
        const auto parameters = reflection.find("parameters");
        if (parameters != reflection.end()) {
            for (const json::Value& parameter : *parameters) {
                const auto binding = parameter.find("binding");
                if (binding != parameter.end() &&
                    binding->value("kind", std::string()) == "descriptorTableSlot") {
                    bindings.emplace_back(binding->value("space", 0U),
                                          binding->at("index").get<std::uint32_t>());
                }
            }
        }
        std::ranges::sort(bindings);
        return bindings;
    } catch (const json::Value::exception& error) {
        return makeError(Errc::Format, error.what());
    }
}
```

In `createComputePipeline(shader, push_constant_size, bindings)`, after the push-constant checks:

```cpp
    const std::uint32_t max_images = core_->maxPushDescriptors();
    if (bindings.storageImages > max_images) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal,
                         std::format("shader '{}': {} storage images exceed the device's "
                                     "maxPushDescriptors ({})",
                                     shader.name, bindings.storageImages, max_images));
    }
    if (!shader.reflectionJson.empty()) {
        const auto declared = reflectedDescriptorBindings(shader.reflectionJson);
        if (!declared) {
            return makeError(Errc::Format,
                             std::format("shader '{}': its reflection cannot be read: {}",
                                         shader.name, declared.error().message));
        }
        std::vector<std::pair<std::uint32_t, std::uint32_t>> wanted;
        for (std::uint32_t index = 0; index < bindings.storageImages; ++index) {
            wanted.emplace_back(0U, index);
        }
        if (*declared != wanted) {
            std::string list;
            for (const auto& [space, index] : *declared) {
                list += std::format("{}(set {}, binding {})", list.empty() ? "" : ", ", space, index);
            }
            return makeError(Errc::Internal,
                             std::format("shader '{}' declares descriptor bindings [{}]; the "
                                         "pipeline binds {} storage images (set 0, bindings "
                                         "0..n-1)",
                                         shader.name, list, bindings.storageImages));
        }
    }
    core_->collect();

    std::string name(shader.name);
    const vk::Device device = core_->device();
    const auto& dispatch = core_->dispatch();
    vk::DescriptorSetLayout set_layout{};
    if (bindings.storageImages > 0) {
        std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayoutBinding> slots(bindings.storageImages);
        for (std::uint32_t index = 0; index < bindings.storageImages; ++index) {
            slots[index] = {.binding = index,
                            .descriptorType = vk::DescriptorType::eStorageImage,
                            .descriptorCount = 1,
                            .stageFlags = vk::ShaderStageFlagBits::eCompute};
        }
        const auto created = device.createDescriptorSetLayout(
            {.flags = vk::DescriptorSetLayoutCreateFlagBits::ePushDescriptor,
             .bindingCount = bindings.storageImages,
             .pBindings = slots.data()},
            nullptr, dispatch);
        DVV_TRY_VOID(detail::vkCheck(created.result, "vkCreateDescriptorSetLayout"));
        set_layout = created.value;
    }
    const vk::PushConstantRange range{
        .stageFlags = vk::ShaderStageFlagBits::eCompute, .offset = 0, .size = push_constant_size};
    const auto layout = device.createPipelineLayout(
        {.setLayoutCount = set_layout ? 1U : 0U,
         .pSetLayouts = &set_layout,
         .pushConstantRangeCount = push_constant_size > 0 ? 1U : 0U,
         .pPushConstantRanges = &range},
        nullptr, dispatch);
    if (layout.result != vk::Result::eSuccess) {
        if (set_layout) {
            device.destroyDescriptorSetLayout(set_layout, nullptr, dispatch);
        }
        return detail::vkError(layout.result, "vkCreatePipelineLayout");
    }
    // ... pipeline creation as today. On its failure, and in the catch block, also destroy
    // set_layout (when set). setDebugName(set_layout, name) when set. The constructor gets
    // static_cast<VkDescriptorSetLayout>(set_layout) and bindings.
```

`formatFeatures` (no `noexcept`: M1b's cached `DeviceCore::formatFeatures` locks a mutex):

```cpp
VkFormatFeatureFlags2 ExecutionContext::formatFeatures(VkFormat format) const {
    return core_->formatFeatures(format);
}
```

`createImage`, after the image exists (before the `try` that makes the `GpuImage`):

```cpp
    VkImageView view = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    if ((desc.usage & (VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT | VK_IMAGE_USAGE_SAMPLED_BIT)) != 0) {
        const auto created = core_->device().createImageView(
            {.image = vk::Image(image),
             .viewType = vk::ImageViewType::e2D,
             .format = format,
             .subresourceRange = {.aspectMask = vk::ImageAspectFlagBits::eColor,
                                  .baseMipLevel = 0,
                                  .levelCount = 1,
                                  .baseArrayLayer = 0,
                                  .layerCount = 1}},
            nullptr, core_->dispatch());
        if (created.result != vk::Result::eSuccess) {
            vmaDestroyImage(core_->allocator(), image, allocation);
            return detail::vkError(created.result, "vkCreateImageView");
        }
        view = static_cast<VkImageView>(created.value);
        if (!desc.name.empty()) {
            core_->setDebugName(created.value, desc.name);
        }
    }
    // the catch block also destroys the view (when set) before vmaDestroyImage
```

`src/command_recorder.cpp`. The header declares a new private member `void barrier(std::span<GpuImage* const> images, VkImageLayout layout);`, and the existing single-image `barrier` becomes a wrapper around it:

```cpp
void CommandRecorder::dispatch(const ComputePipeline& pipeline,
                               std::span<const std::byte> push_constants,
                               std::uint32_t group_count_x, std::uint32_t group_count_y,
                               std::uint32_t group_count_z) {
    dispatch(pipeline, push_constants, std::span<GpuImage* const>{}, group_count_x, group_count_y,
             group_count_z);
}

void CommandRecorder::dispatch(const ComputePipeline& pipeline,
                               std::span<const std::byte> push_constants,
                               std::span<GpuImage* const> storage_images,
                               std::uint32_t group_count_x, std::uint32_t group_count_y,
                               std::uint32_t group_count_z) {
    if (!usable()) {
        return;
    }
    // ... the three existing checks (device, push-constant size, group counts), unchanged ...
    if (storage_images.size() != pipeline.bindings().storageImages) {
        static_cast<void>(check(false, "dispatch",
                                std::format("pipeline '{}' binds {} storage images, not {}",
                                            pipeline.name(), pipeline.bindings().storageImages,
                                            storage_images.size())));
        return;
    }
    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < storage_images.size(); ++index) {
        GpuImage* const image = storage_images[index];
        if (!check(image != nullptr, "dispatch", std::format("storage image {} is null", index)) ||
            !checkImage("dispatch", *image, VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT) ||
            !checkFormat("dispatch", *image) ||
            !check(std::find(storage_images.begin(), storage_images.begin() + index, image) ==
                       storage_images.begin() + index,
                   "dispatch", std::format("{} appears twice", describe(*image)))) {
            return;
        }
    }
    barrier(storage_images, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL);
    const vk::CommandBuffer commands(command_buffer_);
    commands.bindPipeline(vk::PipelineBindPoint::eCompute, vk::Pipeline(pipeline.handle()),
                          core_->dispatch());
    if (!storage_images.empty()) {
        std::vector<vk::DescriptorImageInfo> infos(storage_images.size());
        std::vector<vk::WriteDescriptorSet> writes(storage_images.size());
        for (std::size_t index = 0; index < storage_images.size(); ++index) {
            infos[index] = {.imageView = vk::ImageView(storage_images[index]->view()),
                            .imageLayout = vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral};
            writes[index] = {.dstBinding = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(index),
                             .descriptorCount = 1,
                             .descriptorType = vk::DescriptorType::eStorageImage,
                             .pImageInfo = &infos[index]};
        }
        commands.pushDescriptorSet(vk::PipelineBindPoint::eCompute,
                                   vk::PipelineLayout(pipeline.layout()), 0, writes,
                                   core_->dispatch());
    }
    if (!push_constants.empty()) {
        // ... pushConstants2 as today ...
    }
    commands.dispatch(group_count_x, group_count_y, group_count_z, core_->dispatch());
}

void CommandRecorder::barrier(GpuImage* image, VkImageLayout layout) {
    if (image == nullptr) {
        barrier(std::span<GpuImage* const>{}, layout);
    } else {
        barrier(std::span<GpuImage* const>(&image, 1), layout);
    }
}

// One global memory barrier plus a layout change for every image not yet in `layout`.
void CommandRecorder::barrier(std::span<GpuImage* const> images, VkImageLayout layout) {
    const vk::MemoryBarrier2 memory{.srcStageMask = WORK_STAGES,
                                    .srcAccessMask = vk::AccessFlagBits2::eMemoryWrite,
                                    .dstStageMask = WORK_STAGES,
                                    .dstAccessMask = WORK_ACCESS};
    std::vector<vk::ImageMemoryBarrier2> changes;
    std::vector<GpuImage*> changed;
    changes.reserve(images.size());
    changed.reserve(images.size());
    for (GpuImage* const image : images) {
        const VkImageLayout current = layoutOf(*image);
        if (current == layout) {
            continue;
        }
        changes.push_back({.srcStageMask = WORK_STAGES,
                           .srcAccessMask = vk::AccessFlagBits2::eMemoryWrite,
                           .dstStageMask = WORK_STAGES,
                           .dstAccessMask = WORK_ACCESS,
                           .oldLayout = static_cast<vk::ImageLayout>(current),
                           .newLayout = static_cast<vk::ImageLayout>(layout),
                           .srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED,
                           .dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED,
                           .image = vk::Image(image->handle()),
                           .subresourceRange = COLOR_RANGE});
        changed.push_back(image);
    }
    // Remembered before recording; reserving first makes the updates below nothrow, so a throw
    // leaves both the tracked layouts and the command buffer unchanged.
    layouts_.reserve(layouts_.size() + changed.size());
    for (GpuImage* const image : changed) {
        const auto recorded = std::ranges::find_if(
            layouts_, [image](const auto& entry) { return entry.first.get() == image; });
        if (recorded != layouts_.end()) {
            recorded->second = layout;
        } else {
            layouts_.emplace_back(image->shared_from_this(), layout);
        }
    }
    const vk::DependencyInfo dependency{
        .memoryBarrierCount = 1,
        .pMemoryBarriers = &memory,
        .imageMemoryBarrierCount = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(changes.size()),
        .pImageMemoryBarriers = changes.data()};
    vk::CommandBuffer(command_buffer_).pipelineBarrier2(dependency, core_->dispatch());
}
```

`tests/shaders/write_image.slang`:

```slang
// Writes x + y * width into an R16_UINT (or any uint) storage image bound by push descriptor.
struct PushConstants
{
    uint width;
    uint height;
};

[[vk::push_constant]] ConstantBuffer<PushConstants> pc;

[[vk::binding(0, 0)]] [format("unknown")] RWTexture2D<uint> image;

[shader("compute")]
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void main(uint3 id: SV_DispatchThreadID)
{
    if (id.x < pc.width && id.y < pc.height)
    {
        image[id.xy] = id.x + id.y * pc.width;
    }
}
```

`tests/shaders/write_two_images.slang` has the same push constants, binding 0 `first` and binding 1 `second` (both `[format("unknown")] RWTexture2D<uint>`). It writes `first[id.xy] = id.x + id.y * pc.width; second[id.xy] = id.x + id.y * pc.width + 1;`.

**Tests first:**
- `tests/test_gpu_info.cpp`, new case `the required features include images in shaders`: collect names with `forEachRequiredFeature(chain, [&](std::string_view name, vk::Bool32&) { names.emplace_back(name); })`, then CHECK that `pushDescriptor`, `shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats` and `shaderStorageImageWriteWithoutFormat` are among them. The existing cases stay unchanged and green.
- `tests/test_shaders.cpp`, new case `write_image declares one storage image of unknown format`: walk the SPIR-V of `test_shaders::writeImage()` from word 5. Each instruction is `words[at] >> 16` words long with opcode `words[at] & 0xFFFF`. For OpTypeImage (25), word `+7` is Sampled and `+8` is Image Format; for OpCapability (17), word `+1` is the capability. CHECK that exactly one OpTypeImage has Sampled 2 and Format 0 (Unknown), and that capability 56 (`StorageImageWriteWithoutFormat`) is present. The reflection's `parameters` contain one entry with `binding.kind == "descriptorTableSlot"` and `binding.index == 0`.
- `tests/gpu_test_images.cpp` (fixture `GpuTest`; `push = {width, height}` as `struct { std::uint32_t w, h; }`, 8 bytes):
  1. `a compute shader writes a storage image through a push descriptor`: R16_UINT 37×23 image with `STORAGE | TRANSFER_SRC`; pipeline `writeImage()` with `{.storageImages = 1}`; one submission: `GpuImage* raw = image.get();` then `dispatch(pipeline, push, {&raw, 1}, 5, 3)`, then `copyImageToBuffer` into a HostReadback buffer of 37·23·2 bytes. CHECK that every `uint16` equals `x + y * 37` and that `image->layout() == VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL`. A second submission with only the dispatch leaves `layout() == VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL`.
  2. `two storage images in one dispatch`: `writeTwoImages()` with `{2}`, R8_UINT 16×16 and R16_UINT 16×16 images, passed as a `std::array<GpuImage*, 2>` of their `.get()` pointers. The first holds `(x + y * 16) & 0xFF`; the second holds `x + y * 16 + 1`.
  3. `an image appears once per dispatch`: the two-image pipeline with `std::array<GpuImage*, 2> both{a.get(), a.get()}`. The submission result is an error with `Errc::Internal` whose message contains `appears twice`.
  4. `dispatch binds exactly the pipeline's storage images`: the old overload on the `writeImage` pipeline gives `Internal` containing `binds 1 storage images, not 0`; the `addOne` pipeline with one image gives `binds 0 storage images, not 1`.
  5. `a storage image needs STORAGE usage`: an R16_UINT image with `TRANSFER_SRC` only gives `Internal` containing `lacks the usage`.
  6. `view() exists for STORAGE and SAMPLED images only`: TRANSFER_DST R8G8B8A8_UNORM → `VK_NULL_HANDLE`; SAMPLED R8G8B8A8_UNORM → not null; STORAGE R16_UINT → not null.
  7. `pipeline bindings must fit the device and the shader`:
     - `writeImage()` with `{max + 1}`, where `max = detail::Access::core(context()).maxPushDescriptors()`, gives `Internal` containing `maxPushDescriptors`.
     - `writeImage()` with `{0}` and with `{2}`, and `addOne()` with `{1}`, each give `Internal` containing `declares descriptor bindings`.
     - CHECK `max >= 32`.
  8. `views and set layouts are retired and freed`: record `context().retireStats().freed`; create a STORAGE image and a `{1}` pipeline and drop both; `context().drain()`. `freed` grew by exactly 5 (image, view, pipeline, pipeline layout, set layout) and `retiring == 0`.
  9. `formatFeatures reports storage writes without format for R8_UINT and R16_UINT`: both have `VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_IMAGE_BIT` and `VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT_BIT`.
  10. `a storage image of a format without storage support is Unsupported`: choose the first of `{VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8_UNORM, VK_FORMAT_D32_SFLOAT, VK_FORMAT_D16_UNORM}` whose `formatFeatures` lacks `STORAGE_IMAGE_BIT` (none found: `MESSAGE` and return). Creating it with STORAGE usage gives `Errc::Unsupported`, and the message contains `no storage images`.
  
  Every case ends with the fixture's check of zero validation messages; run with `DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync`.

The first failure: with the tests and shaders added before the code, the build fails (`ComputeBindings`, the `dispatch` overload, `view()` and `formatFeatures` are unknown).

**Steps:**
- [ ] **Step 1:** `git -C dvv-gpu branch --show-current` → `m2`. Record `<base>`: `git -C dvv-gpu rev-parse HEAD`.
- [ ] **Step 2: Write the shaders and the tests**, and add them to `tests/CMakeLists.txt`. Run `Push-Location dvv-gpu; cmake --preset vs2026; cmake --build --preset vs2026; Pop-Location`. Expected: C++ compile errors for the missing API. Before that, slangc compiles `write_image.slang`; if it rejects `[format("unknown")]`, go to Step 3 first.
- [ ] **Step 3: Confirm the Slang spelling.** Once `test_shaders.cpp`'s SPIR-V case builds (it needs only the shader), run `ctest --preset vs2026 -R dvv_gpu.unit` in `dvv-gpu`. If OpTypeImage reports format 0, keep `[format("unknown")]`. Otherwise:
  1. Try `[vk::image_format("unknown")]`.
  2.
