### Task 6: GPU unpack shader, bit-exact for every format (dvv-media)

**Goal:** Add one generic Slang compute shader that unpacks any catalogue (or valid custom) format from a device buffer into one `R8_UINT`/`R16_UINT` storage image per component. Prove it bit-exact against the CPU reference for all 37 formats on NVIDIA: this is exit criterion 3 (M2 design D5, D7, §5.4).

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/shaders/unpack.slang`: the shader (complete code below).
- Create `dvv-media/src/gpu_unpack.h` and `src/gpu_unpack.cpp` (private):
  - `UnpackPush` and `unpackPush()`;
  - `unpackedImageFormat()`;
  - `createUnpackPipeline()`;
  - `recordUnpack()`.
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`: add `dvv_add_shaders(dvv_media NAMESPACE dvv_media_shaders SHADERS shaders/unpack.slang)` (with the Slang spelling or `SLANGC_OPTIONS` Task 5 recorded) and `src/gpu_unpack.cpp`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_gpu_unpack.cpp` (unit, no Vulkan) and add it to `dvv_media_tests`.
- Create the GPU test executable `dvv_media_gpu_tests` (CTest `dvv_media.gpu`), unless Task 1 already created it (then only add the sources):
  - `tests/gpu_main.cpp`: a copy of dvv-gpu's `tests/gpu_main.cpp` (exit 77 rules, `DVV_REQUIRE_GPU`, leak check).
  - `tests/gpu_fixture.h`, `tests/gpu_fixture.cpp`: `dvv::media::test::GpuTest`, the `GpuTest`, `submitAndWait` and `TemporaryDirectory` parts of dvv-gpu's fixture, in namespace `dvv::media::test`.
  - `tests/gpu_test_unpack.cpp`.
  - `tests/tsan.supp`: a copy of dvv-gpu's (portable build file for the `ninja-clang-tsan` preset; not run in M2, decision U-WIN).
  - `tests/CMakeLists.txt` block below.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 2: `descriptor()`, `catalogue()`, `findFormat()`, `geometry()`, `componentSize()`, `PixelFormatDescriptor`, `PlaneDesc`, `SampleSlot`, `MAX_COMPONENTS`.
  - Task 3: `pack()`, `CodeValues`, and `detail::generate(Pattern::Noise, format, width, height, seed, index)` from `src/patterns.h`.
  - Task 5: `ComputeBindings`, `CommandRecorder::dispatch(pipeline, push, images, x, y)`, STORAGE images, `ExecutionContext::formatFeatures`, and the confirmed `format("unknown")` spelling.
  - Task 1: `FrameDesc`, the `dvv_media` target and `dvv_media_tests`.
- Produces (private to dvv-media; Task 8's `GpuUnpack` converter uses it):

```cpp
// src/gpu_unpack.h
#pragma once

#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <span>

#include <vulkan/vulkan_core.h>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/compute_pipeline.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "dvv/media/pixel_format.h"
#include "dvv/media/types.h"

namespace dvv::media::detail {

// Push constants of shaders/unpack.slang (M2 design §5.4). The layout is std430/scalar-safe: two
// 8-byte members first, then 4-byte members, slots as a uint3 at offset 48, explicit padding.
struct UnpackPush {
    std::uint64_t base = 0;         // device address of the frame's first byte; 4-aligned
    std::uint64_t planeOffset = 0;  // byte offset of the component's plane inside the frame
    std::uint32_t stride = 0;       // bytes per plane row
    std::uint32_t groupBytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t wordBytes = 0;    // 1, 2 or 4
    std::uint32_t bigEndian = 0;    // 1: words are big-endian
    std::uint32_t bits = 0;         // significant bits of the component
    std::uint32_t width = 0;        // the component's grid size
    std::uint32_t height = 0;
    std::uint32_t samplesPerGroup = 0;
    std::array<std::uint32_t, 3> slots{};  // slot k: (word << 8 | shift) in bits 16*(k%2) of [k/2]
    std::uint32_t pad = 0;
};
static_assert(sizeof(UnpackPush) == 64);
static_assert(offsetof(UnpackPush, planeOffset) == 8 && offsetof(UnpackPush, stride) == 16 &&
              offsetof(UnpackPush, samplesPerGroup) == 44 && offsetof(UnpackPush, slots) == 48 &&
              offsetof(UnpackPush, pad) == 60);

inline constexpr std::size_t MAX_GPU_SLOTS = 6;   // per component and group (validate() checks it)
inline constexpr std::uint32_t UNPACK_GROUP = 8;  // [numthreads(8, 8, 1)]

// R8_UINT for <= 8 bits, else R16_UINT (D5).
[[nodiscard]] VkFormat unpackedImageFormat(const ComponentDesc& component) noexcept;

// The push constants for one component. Errc::Internal when the component has no slots, more than
// MAX_GPU_SLOTS, slot indices that are not 0..n-1, or n != groupPixels >> log2SubX; Errc::Format
// from geometry().
[[nodiscard]] Result<UnpackPush> unpackPush(FormatId format, std::uint32_t width,
                                            std::uint32_t height, std::size_t component,
                                            std::uint64_t base);

// The unpack pipeline (bindings {1}, 64 bytes of push constants). Errc::Unsupported when R8_UINT
// or R16_UINT lacks STORAGE_IMAGE or STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT; Errc::Internal when the
// shader's reflected push-constant block is not sizeof(UnpackPush).
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<gpu::ComputePipeline>> createUnpackPipeline(
    gpu::ExecutionContext& context);

// Records one dispatch per component, reading the frame at buffer + offset and writing
// images[c] for component c. Errc::Internal (nothing recorded) when (deviceAddress + offset) is
// not 4-aligned, when the buffer is smaller than offset + alignUp(bytes, 4) + 4 (the size rule of
// §5.4), or when the images do not match (count = componentCount, each componentSize() and
// unpackedImageFormat()). Recorder misuse (missing STORAGE usage, another device) fails the
// submission as usual.
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> recordUnpack(gpu::CommandRecorder& recorder,
                                        const gpu::ComputePipeline& pipeline,
                                        const gpu::GpuBuffer& buffer, std::uint64_t offset,
                                        const FrameDesc& desc,
                                        std::span<gpu::GpuImage* const> images);

// Bytes a buffer needs to hold a frame at `offset` for the unpack: offset + alignUp(bytes, 4) + 4.
[[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t unpackBufferBytes(std::uint64_t offset, std::uint64_t frame_bytes) noexcept;

}  // namespace dvv::media::detail
```

Note (plan choice): the design writes `uint slots[3]` and `uint64 base`. The shader uses `uint3 slots` because an array in a std140-laid-out block has a 16-byte element stride, while `uint3` at offset 48 gives the same 12 bytes in every layout. It uses `uint* base` because a Slang pointer is 8 bytes in the same place and can be indexed directly. The bytes are identical to the design's struct; the reflection check guards them.

Note (plan choice): `recordUnpack` takes `(buffer, offset)` rather than a `VkBufferB`, because `frame.h` arrives in Task 7a. Task 8 passes `VkBufferB::buffer` and `VkBufferB::offset`.

**Behaviour:**
- `unpackPush(format, w, h, c, base)`:
  1. Let `d = descriptor(format)`. Find the plane `p` whose slots include component `c` (there is exactly one; none gives `Internal`).
  2. Collect the slots of `c` and sort them by `index`; the indices must be `0..n-1`.
  3. Set `base`. From `geometry(format, w, h)`: `planeOffset = planes[p].offset` and `stride = planes[p].stride`.
  4. From `d.planes[p]`: `groupBytes`, `wordBytes`, and `bigEndian = order == Big`. `bits = d.components[c].bits`.
  5. `{width, height} = componentSize(format, c, w, h)`.
  6. `samplesPerGroup = n`. Slot `k` goes into `slots[k / 2] |= (word << 8 | shift) << (16 * (k % 2))`.
- `createUnpackPipeline`:
  1. For `VK_FORMAT_R8_UINT` and `VK_FORMAT_R16_UINT`, `context.formatFeatures(f)` must contain `VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_IMAGE_BIT | VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT_BIT`. Otherwise return `Unsupported "device <id> cannot write <format> storage images without a format (M2 R1)"`.
  2. Parse `dvv_media_shaders::unpack().reflectionJson` with `dvv::json`. The `pushConstantBuffer` parameter's `type.elementVarLayout.binding.size` must be `sizeof(UnpackPush)`, else `Internal`.
  3. Return `context.createComputePipeline(dvv_media_shaders::unpack(), sizeof(UnpackPush), {.storageImages = 1})`.
- `recordUnpack`: do every check first, then for each component `c`: `push = unpackPush(…, buffer.deviceAddress() + offset)`, then `recorder.dispatch(pipeline, std::as_bytes(std::span(&push, 1)), std::span<gpu::GpuImage* const>(&images[c], 1), ceilDiv(push.width, 8), ceilDiv(push.height, 8))`.

**Code (binding): `shaders/unpack.slang`**

```slang
// Generic unpack (M2 design §5.4): one dispatch per output component. Each invocation reads one
// sample of the component from the original bytes (by buffer device address) and writes its
// LSB-aligned code value into the component's R8_UINT or R16_UINT storage image. The two dwords
// around the sample's word are loaded at the absolute byte address, so planes and words need no
// alignment beyond the 4-aligned frame base (buffer size rule: offset + alignUp(bytes, 4) + 4).
struct UnpackPush
{
    uint* base;            // device address of the frame's first byte; 4-aligned
    uint64_t planeOffset;  // byte offset of the component's plane inside the frame
    uint stride;           // bytes per plane row
    uint groupBytes;       // bytes per group
    uint wordBytes;        // 1, 2 or 4
    uint bigEndian;        // 1: words are big-endian
    uint bits;             // significant bits of the component
    uint width;            // the component's grid size
    uint height;
    uint samplesPerGroup;  // samples of the component per group
    uint3 slots;           // slot k: (word << 8 | shift) in bits 16 * (k % 2) of slots[k / 2]
    uint pad;              // explicit padding to 64 bytes
};

[[vk::push_constant]] ConstantBuffer<UnpackPush> pc;

[[vk::binding(0, 0)]] [format("unknown")] RWTexture2D<uint> plane;

uint swapBytes(uint word, uint bytes)
{
    if (bytes == 2u)
    {
        return ((word & 0xFFu) << 8u) | (word >> 8u);
    }
    if (bytes == 4u)
    {
        return (word << 24u) | ((word & 0xFF00u) << 8u) | ((word >> 8u) & 0xFF00u) | (word >> 24u);
    }
    return word;
}

[shader("compute")]
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void main(uint3 id: SV_DispatchThreadID)
{
    if (id.x >= pc.width || id.y >= pc.height)
    {
        return;
    }
    const uint group = id.x / pc.samplesPerGroup;
    const uint sample = id.x - group * pc.samplesPerGroup;
    const uint slot = (pc.slots[sample >> 1u] >> ((sample & 1u) * 16u)) & 0xFFFFu;
    const uint word = slot >> 8u;
    const uint shift = slot & 0xFFu;
    const uint64_t byteAddress = pc.planeOffset + uint64_t(id.y) * uint64_t(pc.stride) +
                                 uint64_t(group) * uint64_t(pc.groupBytes) +
                                 uint64_t(word) * uint64_t(pc.wordBytes);
    const uint dword = uint(byteAddress >> 2ull);
    const uint64_t pair = (uint64_t(pc.base[dword + 1u]) << 32ull) | uint64_t(pc.base[dword]);
    uint value = uint(pair >> ((byteAddress & 3ull) * 8ull));
    if (pc.wordBytes < 4u)
    {
        value &= (1u << (pc.wordBytes * 8u)) - 1u;
    }
    if (pc.bigEndian != 0u)
    {
        value = swapBytes(value, pc.wordBytes);
    }
    plane[id.xy] = (value >> shift) & ((1u << pc.bits) - 1u);
}
```

Use the attribute spelling Task 5 confirmed. If Task 5 chose `SLANGC_OPTIONS -default-image-format-unknown`, drop `[format("unknown")]` and pass the option here.

The dword index is a `uint`, which limits the frame plus padding to 16 GiB. `recordUnpack` returns `Internal` above that limit.

**Code (binding): `src/gpu_unpack.cpp` checks and dispatch loop**

```cpp
std::uint64_t unpackBufferBytes(std::uint64_t offset, std::uint64_t frame_bytes) noexcept {
    return offset + ((frame_bytes + 3) & ~std::uint64_t{3}) + 4;
}

Result<void> recordUnpack(gpu::CommandRecorder& recorder, const gpu::ComputePipeline& pipeline,
                          const gpu::GpuBuffer& buffer, std::uint64_t offset,
                          const FrameDesc& desc, std::span<gpu::GpuImage* const> images) {
    DVV_TRY(const FrameGeometry frame, geometry(desc.format, desc.width, desc.height));
    const PixelFormatDescriptor& format = descriptor(desc.format);
    const std::uint64_t base = buffer.deviceAddress() + offset;
    if (base % 4 != 0) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal,
                         std::format("unpack: the frame in buffer '{}' starts at offset {}, which "
                                     "is not 4-aligned",
                                     buffer.desc().name, offset));
    }
    const std::uint64_t needed = unpackBufferBytes(offset, frame.bytes);
    if (buffer.size() < needed || needed > (std::uint64_t{1} << 34)) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal,
                         std::format("unpack: buffer '{}' holds {} bytes; a {}x{} {} frame at "
                                     "offset {} needs {} (frame bytes rounded up to 4, plus 4)",
                                     buffer.desc().name, buffer.size(), desc.width, desc.height,
                                     format.name, offset, needed));
    }
    if (images.size() != format.componentCount) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal, std::format("unpack: {} has {} components, {} images given",
                                                     format.name, format.componentCount,
                                                     images.size()));
    }
    std::array<UnpackPush, MAX_COMPONENTS> pushes{};
    for (std::size_t c = 0; c < images.size(); ++c) {
        DVV_TRY(pushes[c], unpackPush(desc.format, desc.width, desc.height, c, base));
        const gpu::GpuImage* image = images[c];
        if (image == nullptr || image->width() != pushes[c].width ||
            image->height() != pushes[c].height ||
            image->format() != unpackedImageFormat(format.components[c])) {
            return makeError(Errc::Internal,
                             std::format("unpack: the image of component {} of {} must be {}x{} "
                                         "{}",
                                         c, format.name, pushes[c].width, pushes[c].height,
                                         unpackedImageFormat(format.components[c]) ==
                                                 VK_FORMAT_R8_UINT
                                             ? "R8_UINT"
                                             : "R16_UINT"));
        }
    }
    for (std::size_t c = 0; c < images.size(); ++c) {
        recorder.dispatch(pipeline, std::as_bytes(std::span(&pushes[c], 1)),
                          std::span<gpu::GpuImage* const>(&images[c], 1),
                          (pushes[c].width + UNPACK_GROUP - 1) / UNPACK_GROUP,
                          (pushes[c].height + UNPACK_GROUP - 1) / UNPACK_GROUP);
    }
    return {};
}
```

(`DVV_TRY(pushes[c], …)` assigns into an existing element. If the macro needs a declaration, use `DVV_TRY(const UnpackPush push, …); pushes[c] = push;`.)

**Tests first:**

Unit (`tests/test_gpu_unpack.cpp`, `TEST_SUITE("gpu_unpack")`, no Vulkan; include `src/` privately):
1. `unpack push constants of yuv420p10le at 33x17`:
   - Y (c = 0): `planeOffset 0, stride 66, groupBytes 2, wordBytes 2, bigEndian 0, bits 10, width 33, height 17, samplesPerGroup 1, slots {0,0,0}`.
   - U (c = 1): `planeOffset 1122, stride 34, width 17, height 9`.
   - V (c = 2): `planeOffset 1428`.
   - `base` equals the value passed (here 4096).
2. `unpack push constants of v210 at 12x1`:
   - Y: `stride 128, groupBytes 16, wordBytes 4, bits 10, width 12, samplesPerGroup 6, slots {0x0100000A, 0x020A0114, 0x03140300}`.
   - U: `width 6, samplesPerGroup 3, slots {0x010A0000, 0x00000214, 0}`.
   - V: `slots {0x02000014, 0x0000030A, 0}`.
   - These follow FFmpeg's `v210dec.c` word order: w0 = Cb0 | Y0<<10 | Cr0<<20; w1 = Y1 | Cb1<<10 | Y2<<20; w2 = Cr1 | Y3<<10 | Cb2<<20; w3 = Y4 | Cr2<<10 | Y5<<20.
3. `unpack push constants of p010le and rgb48be`:
   - p010le at 4×2: U `slots[0] == 0x0006`, V `slots[0] == 0x0106`, `bits 10, wordBytes 2, groupBytes 4, samplesPerGroup 1, width 2, height 1`, U/V `planeOffset 16`, stride 8.
   - rgb48be at 3×1: R/G/B `slots[0] == 0x0000 / 0x0100 / 0x0200`, `bigEndian 1, bits 16, groupBytes 6, wordBytes 2, stride 18`.
4. `the unpack shader's push block matches UnpackPush`: parse `dvv_media_shaders::unpack().reflectionJson`. Find the parameter with `binding.kind == "pushConstantBuffer"`; its `type.elementVarLayout.binding.size == 64`. Its fields (`type.elementType.fields[i].binding.offset` by name) are `base 0, planeOffset 8, stride 16, groupBytes 20, wordBytes 24, bigEndian 28, bits 32, width 36, height 40, samplesPerGroup 44, slots 48, pad 60`. If Slang names the JSON keys differently, print the reflection once and adapt the key names, never the expected offsets.
5. `the unpack shader writes an image of unknown format`: the SPIR-V walk from Task 5's `test_shaders.cpp` (OpTypeImage Sampled 2, Format 0; capability 56).
6. `unpackBufferBytes follows the size rule`: `(0, 561) → 568`, `(260, 1) → 268`, `(0, 64) → 68`.
7. `unpackPush rejects a component without slots`: component index 3 of `yuv420p` (3 components) gives `Errc::Internal`.

GPU (`tests/gpu_test_unpack.cpp`, fixture `dvv::media::test::GpuTest` with `context()`, `executor()`, `submitAndWait(record)`; validation count 0 at the end of each case):
1. `GPU unpack is bit-exact for every catalogue format` (exit criterion 3). For each `FormatId f` in `catalogue()`, in a `SUBCASE(descriptor(f).name.c_str())`, one submission covers all cases of the format:
   - Sizes `{1x1, 2x2, 33x17, 49x3, 64x64}`; for `widthMultiple == 2` (v210) the widths are rounded up to the multiple and 1×1 is dropped, giving `{2x2, 34x17, 50x3, 64x64}`.
   - Seeds `{1, 2}`. Seed 1 uses buffer offset 0 and seed 2 uses offset 260 (4-aligned, not 16-aligned).
   - Per case:
     - `values = detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, f, w, h, seed, 0).value()` and `bytes = pack(values).value()`.
     - A HostUpload `TRANSFER_SRC` buffer of `unpackBufferBytes(offset, geometry.bytes)` bytes, with `bytes` copied in at `offset` and then `flush()`.
     - A DeviceOnly buffer of the same size (`TRANSFER_DST | STORAGE_BUFFER`).
     - Per component, an image of `componentSize` and `unpackedImageFormat` (`STORAGE | TRANSFER_SRC`) and a HostReadback buffer of `w_c · h_c · (1 or 2)` bytes (`TRANSFER_DST`).
   - Record: per case, `copyBuffer(staging, device)`, `recordUnpack(…)` (store its result in a captured `std::vector<Result<void>>`), then `copyImageToBuffer` per component.
   - After the submission: `REQUIRE` the submission result and every recorded result. Then `invalidate()` each readback, and compare every texel (R8: byte; R16: little-endian `uint16`) with `values.components[c]`. On the first mismatch, `FAIL_CHECK` with format, size, seed, component, x, y, expected and got.
2. `an unpadded buffer is Internal`: yuv420p 33×17 (561 + 153 + 153 = 867 bytes, not a multiple of 4) in a DeviceOnly buffer of exactly 867 bytes. `recordUnpack` returns `Internal` containing `needs 872`, and the submission (which recorded nothing else) succeeds.
3. `a frame base that is not 4-aligned is Internal`: gray 4×4 at offset 2 gives `Internal` containing `not 4-aligned`.
4. `images must match the components`: yuv420p 4×4 with 2 images gives `Internal` (`3 components, 2 images`). With the U image 4×4 instead of 2×2, or with an R16_UINT image for 8-bit Y, it gives `Internal`.
5. `the unpack pipeline binds one storage image`: `createUnpackPipeline(context())` has a value, `bindings().storageImages == 1`, and `pushConstantSize() == 64`.

The first failure: with the header, the tests and an empty `gpu_unpack.cpp`, linking fails. Once the functions exist, unit case 4 is the first meaningful check of the shader layout.

**CMake for the GPU tests** (`tests/CMakeLists.txt`; skip whatever Task 1 already has):

```cmake

    PATHS ${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH} PATH_SUFFIXES bin share/vulkan/explicit_layer.d NO_DEFAULT_PATH)

Expected: slangc compiles `unpack.slang`, and the link fails on the undefined functions. A slangc error here means the shader text is wrong: fix it before going on (common causes: an implicit 64→32-bit conversion warning promoted to an error; add the explicit casts).

**Expected results:**
- `dvv_media.unit` gains 7 cases.
- `dvv_media.gpu` runs 5 cases. Case 1 is 37 subcases and 37 submissions, covering 36 × 10 + 4 × 2 = 368 unpack cases.
- Validation reports 0 messages, the run is green on NVIDIA under vs2026 Debug, `FORMAT-OK`, and the privacy lint is clean. (Release, ASan and clang-tidy: Task 7b checkpoint.)

**Commit:** `feat: unpack every catalogue format on the GPU, bit-exact against the CPU reference`, ending with the trailer.

**Notes:** Design D4, D5, D7, §5.4, §7 (unpack rows), §8.1 (dvv-media gpu row: exit criterion 3, unpadded buffer), R1, R3, R9; arbiter FIX 2, 19.
- The test 33×17 gray frame is 561 bytes and 33×17 yuv420p is 867: neither is a multiple of 4, which covers R9. Seed 2's offset 260 moves every plane off 16-byte alignment.
- Only NVIDIA runs the shader in M2 (decision U-WIN). Undefined behaviour in the shader may not show on one driver, so the reviewers check every shift: an out-of-range shift (`1u << 32`) must never happen, which is why `wordBytes < 4u` guards the mask. When lavapipe returns (v1.x), a lavapipe-only mismatch is compared against the same `-sc="<format>"` run on NVIDIA first.
- The 16-bit `bits` mask is `(1u << 16) - 1u = 0xFFFF`, which is well defined.

---

### Task 7a: Frames, source interface and store basics (dvv-media)

**Goal:** Build the frame store's core, all of it testable without Vulkan:
- records and entries;
- pending entries with waiter lists and single-flight creation;
- read and pin leases (released under the store mutex);
- `adopt`, `attach`/`detach`, `trim` and `outstandingLeases`;
- resumption on each requester's own executor.

Also add the frame and source interfaces the store needs (M2 design §4.4, §4.5, §5.1, §5.2, D10–D12). Plans here are fixed and at most "Produce, then one converter"; Task 7b adds the real planner, tiers and fidelity.

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/frame.h`: `HostPlanesB`, `VkBufferB`, `VkImageB`, `Binding`, `IFrame`, `HostFrame` and `FrameRef`.
- Create `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/source.h`: `SourceAccess`, `SourceInfo`, `ProduceContext` and `ISource`.
- Create `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/frame_store.h`:
  - `LeaseKind`, `detail::LeaseCore`, `ReadLease` and `PinLease`;
  - the stats types `TierStats`, `ConverterStats`, `StoreStats` and `LeaseInfo`;
  - `FrameStoreOptions` and `FrameStore`.

  `OriginalRead`, `acquireOriginal`, `originalView` and `planesView` are added by Task 8.
- Create `dvv-media/src/converters.h` (private): `StateKey`, `ConvertInput`, `ConvertContext` and the `Converter` interface. The real converters come in Task 8.
- Create `dvv-media/src/store_state.h` (private): `Entry`, `Waiter`, `Record`, `Origin`, `Tier`, `ConverterSlot`, `StoreState`, `Step`, `Plan`, `Prepared`, `JoinAwaiter` and `StoreAccess`.
- Create `dvv-media/src/frames.cpp` (`HostFrame`) and `dvv-media/src/frame_store.cpp` (the store, `ProduceContext`, the leases).
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`: add `src/frames.cpp` and `src/frame_store.cpp`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/store_fakes.h` and `tests/store_fakes.cpp`: `FakeFrame`, `FakeSource`, `FakeConverter`, `Gate`, `CountingExecutor`, `waitUntil` and the fixture.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_frame_store.cpp`. Add both sources to `dvv_media_tests` (with `target_include_directories(... PRIVATE ${dvv_media_SOURCE_DIR}/src)` if missing).

If Task 1 created no compile-fail infrastructure, add it following dvv-gpu's `tests/CMakeLists.txt` block (control case 0 creates a store through `FrameStore::create`; `RESOURCE_LOCK dvv_build_tree`; runner `${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/run-compile-fail.cmake`).

Public headers (complete declarations; comments may be shortened, behaviour may not):

```cpp
// include/dvv/media/frame.h
namespace dvv::media {
class FrameStore;

struct HostPlanesB {                       // read-only host bytes of one view
    std::array<std::span<const std::byte>, MAX_PLANES> planes{};
    std::array<std::uint32_t, MAX_PLANES> strides{};
    std::uint8_t planeCount = 0;
};
struct VkBufferB {                         // the whole original in one buffer, tight
    std::shared_ptr<gpu::GpuBuffer> buffer;
    std::uint64_t offset = 0;
    FrameGeometry geometry{};
};
struct VkImageB {                          // one image per unpacked component, layout GENERAL
    std::array<std::shared_ptr<gpu::GpuImage>, MAX_COMPONENTS> planes{};
    std::uint8_t planeCount = 0;
};
using Binding = std::variant<HostPlanesB, VkBufferB, VkImageB>;   // + AvFrameB in M4

class IFrame {                              // spec §6.2
public:
    virtual ~IFrame() = default;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual const FrameDesc& desc() const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::span<const ViewSpec> nativeViews() const noexcept = 0;
    // No I/O, no GPU work. A view that is not native gives a default Binding (HostPlanesB with
    // planeCount 0); the store binds native views only.
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Binding bind(const ViewSpec& native) const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::size_t bytes() const noexcept = 0;
};

// Host memory frame: aligned buffer; the frame data starts at data_offset inside it. Writable
// until the store publishes or adopts it; then memory() and data() are empty (read through bind()).
class HostFrame final : public IFrame, public Managed<HostFrame> {
public:
    HostFrame(CreationKey<FrameStore> key, FrameDesc desc, ViewSpec view,
              platform::AlignedBuffer memory, std::size_t capacity,
              std::size_t data_offset) noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<std::byte> memory() noexcept;  // the whole buffer
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<std::byte> data() noexcept;    // geometry().bytes at dataOffset()
    void setDataOffset(std::size_t offset) noexcept;        // before publishing only
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t dataOffset() const noexcept { return data_offset_; }
    [[nodiscard]] const FrameDesc& desc() const noexcept override { return desc_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const ViewSpec> nativeViews() const noexcept override;  // {view}
    [[nodiscard]] Binding bind(const ViewSpec& native) const override;  // planes per geometry()
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t bytes() const noexcept override { return capacity_; }
private:
    friend class FrameStore;
    void seal() noexcept;   // published: memory() and data() become empty
    FrameDesc desc_;
    ViewSpec view_;
    platform::AlignedBuffer memory_;
    std::size_t capacity_;
    std::size_t data_offset_;
    std::atomic<bool> sealed_{false};
};

struct FrameRef {                           // handle: key + desc + store (§6.2)
    FrameKey key;
    FrameDesc desc;
    std::shared_ptr<FrameStore> store;
};
}
```

```cpp
// include/dvv/media/source.h
namespace dvv::media {
enum class SourceAccess : std::uint8_t { RandomCheap, RandomCostly, Sequential };
struct SourceInfo {
    FrameDesc desc;
    Rational fps;
    std::optional<std::int64_t> frameCount;   // nullopt = unbounded generator
    SourceAccess access = SourceAccess::RandomCheap;
    bool deterministic = true;
    std::string resolvedUri;                  // includes the format: reopens without guessing
    std::string provider;                     // component id
    std::vector<std::string> warnings;        // e.g. "N trailing bytes ignored"
};

class ProduceContext {                        // made by the store for one produce() call
public:
    ProduceContext(const ProduceContext&) = delete;
    ProduceContext& operator=(const ProduceContext&) = delete;
    ProduceContext(ProduceContext&&) = delete;
    ProduceContext& operator=(ProduceContext&&) = delete;
    ~ProduceContext() = default;
    [[nodiscard]] const FrameKey& key() const noexcept { return key_; }
    [[nodiscard]] IExecutor& io() const noexcept { return *io_; }
    // A host frame for this key: `capacity` bytes aligned to `alignment`, frame data at
    // `data_offset`. Errc::Internal when desc differs from the source's, the alignment is not a
    // power of two, or data_offset + geometry().bytes > capacity; Errc::OutOfMemory (Task 7b: after
    // one eviction retry).
    [[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<HostFrame>> allocateHost(
        const FrameDesc& desc, std::size_t capacity, std::size_t alignment,
        std::size_t data_offset);
    // Hands the frame to the store, which publishes it as the key's pending original when
    // produce() returns. Does not call adopt. Errc::Internal for a null frame, a second publish,
    // a desc that differs, or a frame without the original host view
    // {desc.format, desc.colour, Tight, HostPlanes, hostDomain()}.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<FrameRef> publish(std::shared_ptr<IFrame> frame);
private:
    friend class FrameStore;
    ProduceContext(FrameStore& store, FrameKey key, FrameDesc desc, IExecutor& io) noexcept;
    FrameStore* store_;
    FrameKey key_;
    FrameDesc desc_;
    IExecutor* io_;
    std::shared_ptr<IFrame> frame_;
};

class ISource {                               // spec §7.1
public:
    virtual ~ISource() = default;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual const SourceInfo& info() const noexcept = 0;
    // May be called concurrently for different indices. Errc::Internal for an index outside
    // [0, frameCount); Errc::Io for read errors and truncated frames.
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Task<Result<FrameRef>> produce(std::int64_t index,
                                                         ProduceContext& ctx) = 0;
};
}
```

```cpp
// include/dvv/media/frame_store.h
namespace dvv::media {
class FrameStore;
enum class LeaseKind : std::uint8_t { Read, Pin };
namespace detail {
struct Entry;
struct StoreState;
struct StoreAccess;
struct LeaseCore {           // what ReadLease and PinLease hold (§5.2: the store and the entry)
    std::shared_ptr<FrameStore> store;
    std::shared_ptr<Entry> entry;
    ViewSpec view{};
    Binding binding{};
    std::uint64_t id = 0;
    LeaseKind kind = LeaseKind::Read;
};
}  // namespace detail

class ReadLease {            // move-only handle; keeps the view alive
public:
    ReadLease() = default;
    ReadLease(ReadLease&& other) noexcept;
    ReadLease& operator=(ReadLease&& other) noexcept;
    ReadLease(const ReadLease&) = delete;
    ReadLease& operator=(const ReadLease&) = delete;
    ~ReadLease();            // under the store mutex: readers-- and LRU touch (§5.2)
    [[nodiscard]] explicit operator bool() const noexcept { return core_.entry != nullptr; }
    // Valid leases only:
    [[nodiscard]] const FrameKey& key() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] const FrameDesc& desc() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] const ViewSpec& view() const noexcept { return core_.view; }
    [[nodiscard]] Fidelity fidelity() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] const Binding& binding() const noexcept { return core_.binding; }
    [[nodiscard]] SyncPoint ready() const noexcept;   // M2: always reached (D10)
private:
    friend class FrameStore;
    explicit ReadLease(detail::LeaseCore core) noexcept : core_(std::move(core)) {}
    void reset() noexcept;
    detail::LeaseCore core_;
};
class PinLease {             // the same shape and accessors; counts as a pin, not a reader
    /* identical members to ReadLease */
};

struct TierStats { Domain domain; std::size_t bytes = 0, budget = 0, views = 0; };
struct ConverterStats { std::string name; std::uint64_t runs = 0, bytes = 0, nanoseconds = 0; };
struct StoreStats {
    std::vector<TierStats> tiers;          // host first, then staging and device when present
    std::uint64_t frames = 0;              // published entries in the index
    std::uint64_t leases = 0, pins = 0;    // live ReadLeases / PinLeases
    std::uint64_t hits = 0;                // acquires served by an existing (published or pending) entry
    std::uint64_t misses = 0;              // acquires that planned
    std::uint64_t creations = 0;           // published entries made by Produce or a converter
    std::uint64_t evictions = 0, failures = 0;
    std::vector<ConverterStats> converters;  // "Produce" first, then each converter name
};
struct LeaseInfo { FrameKey key; ViewSpec view; LeaseKind kind; std::source_location where; };

struct FrameStoreOptions {
    std::shared_ptr<IExecutor> cpu;                    // converters, the hop before Produce (required)
    std::shared_ptr<IExecutor> io;                     // handed to sources (required)
    std::shared_ptr<gpu::ExecutionContext> gpu{};      // null: host-only store
    std::size_t hostBudget = 2ull << 30;
    std::size_t stagingBudget = 1ull << 30;
    std::size_t deviceBudget = 0;                      // 0: half of deviceLocalMemoryBytes
    std::size_t maxPlanDepth = 4;
};

class FrameStore final : public Managed<FrameStore>, public std::enable_shared_from_this<FrameStore> {
public:
    // Own authority. Errc::Internal for a missing cpu or io executor.
    [[nodiscard]] static Result<std::shared_ptr<FrameStore>> create(
        FrameStoreOptions options, std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());
    FrameStore(CreationKey<FrameStore> key, FrameStoreOptions options) noexcept;
    ~FrameStore();  // leases hold the store, so no entry can be leased when the store dies

    [[nodiscard]] Domain hostDomain() const noexcept;                    // {Host}
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<Domain> stagingDomain() const noexcept;  // {HostVisible, d, gen}
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<Domain> deviceDomain() const noexcept;   // {DeviceLocal, d, gen}

    [[nodiscard]] Result<OriginId> attach(std::shared_ptr<ISource> source);
    void detach(OriginId origin);

    [[nodiscard]] Task<Result<ReadLease>> acquire(
        FrameKey key, ViewSpec want, IExecutor& resume_on,
        std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());
    [[nodiscard]] Task<Result<PinLease>> pin(
        FrameKey key, ViewSpec view, IExecutor& resume_on,
        std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());
    [[nodiscard]] Result<ReadLease> adopt(const FrameKey& key, std::shared_ptr<IFrame> frame,
                                          SyncPoint ready = {},
                                          std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());
    [[nodiscard]] StoreStats stats() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<LeaseInfo> outstandingLeases() const;
    void trim(Domain domain, std::size_t targetBytes);
private:
    friend class ReadLease;
    friend class PinLease;
    friend class ProduceContext;
    friend struct detail::StoreAccess;
    // ... private members listed under Code ...
    std::unique_ptr<detail::StoreState> state_;
};
}
```

Note (plan choice): `acquire` and `pin` take `FrameKey` and `ViewSpec` **by value**, not `const&` as in the design's sketch. They are lazy coroutines, and a reference parameter dangles when the caller creates the task from temporaries and awaits it later (C++ Core Guidelines CP.53). Callers are unaffected.

Note (plan choice): `adopt` works for any origin id, attached or not. The record's description comes from the attached source when there is one, else from the first adopted frame. Only Produce needs an attached source.

**Behaviour:**
- **create:**
  - `FrameStore::create(options)` = `createWith(options, {} /* Task 8: the default converters */, nullopt, nullopt, where)`. `createWith` is private; `StoreAccess::create` calls it with fake converters and fake domains.
  - Tiers: index 0 is the host tier `{hostDomain(), hostBudget}`.
  - With `options.gpu`: a staging tier `{HostVisible, gpu->deviceId(), gpu->generation()}` (stagingBudget), then a device tier `{DeviceLocal, …}` (deviceBudget, or `gpu->deviceInfo().deviceLocalMemoryBytes / 2` when 0).
  - Test domains replace or add the staging and device tiers (deviceBudget 0 then means 1 GiB).
  - Converter slots: `"Produce"` first, then one slot per distinct converter name.
- **attach:** `Internal` for a null source. Otherwise it reads `source->info().desc` and `unpackedFormat(desc.format)` outside the lock, stores `Origin{source, desc, unpacked}` under id `nextOrigin++` (ids start at 1 and are never reused), and returns the id.
- **acquire / pin:**
  - Run `acquireCore` (see Code), then always `co_await resumeOn(resume_on)` (D12: every requester resumes on its own executor, never inline, hits included). The lease is wrapped into `ReadLease`/`PinLease` **before** that hop, so a throwing `post` cannot leak a reader.
  - `prepareLocked(key, want, kind, where)` under the mutex:
    1. Find the record. If there is none: if the origin is not attached, return `Internal "origin <o> is not attached"`; else create the record from the origin.
    2. Choose an existing entry with `selectEntry` (code below). Published: `++readers`, touch the LRU, `++hits`, and the result is `Hit`. Pending: `++hits`, and the result is `Join`.
    3. If no entry fits: `want.colour != rec.desc.colour` returns `Unsupported "no colour conversion in M2: frame <i> of origin <o> has colour <a>, the request <b>"`.
    4. Otherwise `planLocked` (the 7a rules below). On success, insert the plan's pending entries and `++misses`; the result is `Plan`. On failure, erase the record if it is empty and return the error.
- **7a plan rules** (`planLocked`; Task 7b replaces them):
  - `target = stateOf(want)`. `want.format` must be `rec.desc.format` (original) or `rec.unpacked` (unpacked); `want.downscaleLog2 == 0`; and `want.domain` must equal the store's domain of `want.domain.kind`. Otherwise return `Unsupported "no converter path from <format> to <view>"`.
  - The **root** is the record's entry with `root == true` (made by Produce or adopt; not failed).
  - If `target == PRODUCED` (`{original, HostPlanes, Host}`) and `want.layout` is `Any` or `Tight`: when there is no root and the origin has a source, the plan is `[Produce]`.
  - Else, the first converter with `from() == PRODUCED`, `to() == target` and a fitting layout. If a root exists and offers a view in `PRODUCED`, the plan starts from it: if the root is published, the plan holds one reader on it; if it is pending, `startPending = true`. Without a root, the plan is `[Produce, converter]`.
  - Anything else returns `Unsupported "no converter path from <format> to <view>"`.
- **Pending entries:**
  - Each step output gets `key`, `desc`, `planned = {state.unpacked ? rec.unpacked : rec.desc.format, rec.desc.colour, outputLayout (Produce: Tight), state.api, domainOf(state.kind), 0}`, a fidelity (Produce: Exact; else the minimum of the input's fidelity and the converter's), `root` (Produce only), and `tier`.
  - All of a plan's entries are built first. Then `rec.entries.reserve(size + n)` and `push_back`, so no exception can leave a pending entry without a creator.
- **Plan execution** (`runPlan`, code below):
  - Each step runs outside the lock. A Produce step does `co_await resumeOn(cpu)`, then `source->produce(index, ctx)`; the step holds `shared_ptr<ISource>` (R11). A converter step calls `run(input, ctx)`.
  - After each step, under the lock: publish the output (`publishLocked`) or fail it and every later output (`failStepsLocked`), and release the step's reader on its input. After unlocking, `wake` posts every waiter to its own executor.
  - An exception from `produce` or a converter becomes `Internal "<name> threw: <what>"`.
  - A source that returns success without `publish` gives `Internal "source did not publish frame <i> of origin <o>"`.
- **Leases:**
  - The requester's reader is reserved by `prepareLocked` (Hit), by the completer (Join), or by `publishLocked` of the last step (Plan).
  - `finishLease` computes `binding = entry->frame->bind(view)` outside the lock. If that throws, it releases the reserved reader and rethrows.
  - Then, under the lock, it registers `LeaseInfo{key, view, kind, where}` under a fresh id. For `Pin` it converts the reader into a pin (`--readers; ++pins`).
  - A lease's destructor locks, decrements `readers` (or `pins`), erases its `LeaseInfo`, touches the LRU when the entry is linked, unlocks, and then releases the binding, the entry and the store, in that order.
- **adopt:**
  - `Internal` for a null frame, or a frame whose native views are not all `{format == desc.format, colour == desc.colour}` in one of the store's domains.
  - `Internal "frame <i> of origin <o> already has an original"` when the record holds a root (published or pending).
  - `Internal` when an existing record's desc differs from the frame's.
  - Otherwise it inserts a published root entry (Exact, `planned = views[0]`, `tier` of `views[0].domain`), links it in its tier, seals a `HostFrame`, and returns a `ReadLease` with location `where`.
- **detach:**
  - Under the lock: erase the origin, and for every record of that origin take all entries out of the index (unlink from the LRU, subtract their bytes, set `inRecord = false`), then erase the record.
  - After unlocking, release the dropped entries and the source.
  - Leased entries live on with their leases. Pending entries are still published to their waiters by their creators, but not into the index. A later `acquire` of the origin returns `Internal "origin <o> is not attached"`.
- **trim(domain, target):** under the lock, evict unleased, unpinned, published entries of that tier in LRU order (front = least recently used) until `bytes <= target`; `++evictions` per entry. Dropped entries are released after unlocking. An unknown domain does nothing.
- **stats():**
  - Under the lock: `tiers` (bytes, budget, and `views` = linked entries), `frames` (published in-index entries), `leases` and `pins` (counted from the `LeaseInfo` map by kind), and the counters.
  - `converters`: relaxed loads of the slots.
- **outstandingLeases():** a copy of the `LeaseInfo` map's values.
- **Lock rules** (§5.2): one mutex. It is never held across `co_await`, `post`, `produce`, a converter body, a frame release or a logger call. Entries and frames that leave the index are moved into a local vector and released after unlocking.
- **HostFrame:**
  - `g = geometry(view_.format, desc_.width, desc_.height)`; `data()` and `bind()` use `g`.
  - `memory()` is `{memory_.get(), capacity_}` until sealed, empty afterwards.
  - `data()` is `memory().subspan(data_offset_, g.bytes)`; it is empty when sealed, or when the geometry fails or does not fit.
  - `bind(view)` lays out `g`'s planes: for `view == view_`, `HostPlanesB` with plane `p` = `{memory_.get() + data_offset_ + g.planes[p].offset, g.planes[p].stride * g.planes[p].rows}`, `strides[p] = g.planes[p].stride` and `planeCount = g.planeCount`. Otherwise a default `HostPlanesB`.
- **ProduceContext::allocateHost:**
  - `desc != desc_` gives `Internal`. A failing `geometry(desc.format, …)` passes its error on; `data_offset + bytes > capacity` gives `Internal`.
  - Then `allocateAligned(capacity, alignment)`, whose errors pass through (Task 7b adds the eviction retry).
  - Then `std::make_shared<HostFrame>(FrameStore::frameKey<HostFrame>(store objectId), desc, {desc.format, desc.colour, Tight, HostPlanes, hostDomain(), 0}, buffer, capacity, data_offset)`.
- **ProduceContext::publish:** the checks in the header; on success it stores `frame_` and returns `FrameRef{key_, desc_, store_->shared_from_this()}`.

**Code (binding): `src/converters.h`**

```cpp
#pragma once
// Converters (M2 design §5.3): edges of the store's static graph. Private to dvv-media.

#include <memory>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/executor.h"
#include "dvv/base/task.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/media/frame.h"
#include "dvv/media/types.h"

namespace dvv::media {
class FrameStore;
}

namespace dvv::media::detail {

// A graph state: the original format or its unpacked form, the API and the memory kind.
struct StateKey {
    bool unpacked = false;
    Api api = Api::HostPlanes;
    MemKind kind = MemKind::Host;
    bool operator==(const StateKey&) const = default;
};
// What Produce makes: the original, as host planes in host memory.
inline constexpr StateKey PRODUCED{.unpacked = false, .api = Api::HostPlanes, .kind = MemKind::Host};

struct ConvertInput {
    const FrameKey& key;      // the key being converted
    const FrameDesc& desc;    // the key's frame description
    const ViewSpec& view;     // the input view (a native view of `frame`)
    const IFrame& frame;      // kept alive by the step for the whole call
    const Binding& binding;   // frame.bind(view)
};
struct ConvertContext {
    FrameStore& store;              // makes frames: StoreAccess::makeFrame<T>(store, ...)
    IExecutor& cpu;
    gpu::ExecutionContext* gpu;     // null in a host-only store
    const ViewSpec& output;         // the planned output view; the frame must offer it
};

class Converter {
public:
    Converter() = default;
    Converter(const Converter&) = delete;
    Converter& operator=(const Converter&) = delete;
    Converter(Converter&&) = delete;
    Converter& operator=(Converter&&) = delete;
    virtual ~Converter() = default;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string_view name() const noexcept = 0;   // stats key
    [[nodiscard]] virtual StateKey from() const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual StateKey to() const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual LayoutPolicy outputLayout() const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Fidelity fidelity() const noexcept = 0;
    // Weighted bytes moved (§5.3): host copy 1x, PCIe 2x, GPU-local 0.5x, CPU unpack 4x.
    [[nodiscard]] virtual double cost(const FrameDesc& desc) const noexcept = 0;
    // Makes the output frame. Hops to its own executor itself (cpu, or a GPU submission that
    // resumes on cpu); a GPU converter co_awaits its submission (D10).
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Task<Result<std::shared_ptr<IFrame>>> run(ConvertInput input,
                                                                   ConvertContext context) const = 0;
};

}  // namespace dvv::media::detail
```

**Code (binding): `src/store_state.h`**

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <atomic>
#include <coroutine>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <list>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

#include "converters.h"
#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/executor.h"
#include "dvv/media/frame.h"
#include "dvv/media/frame_store.h"
#include "dvv/media/source.h"

namespace dvv::media::detail {

enum class EntryState : std::uint8_t { Pending, Published, Failed };

// A coroutine waiting for a pending entry. Lives in the waiting coroutine's frame; the store
// never destroys it (no cancellation in M2; M3 removes nodes from Entry::waiters, D10 hook).
struct Waiter {
    std::coroutine_handle<> continuation;
    IExecutor* resumeOn = nullptr;
    // Set before resumption when the entry failed. When it was published, the completer counted
    // one reader of the entry for this waiter instead.
    std::optional<Error> error;
};

struct Entry {
    // Fixed at insertion.
    FrameKey key;
    FrameDesc desc;
    ViewSpec planned{};           // the promised view (pending); views[0] when adopted
    Fidelity fidelity = Fidelity::Exact;
    bool root = false;            // the key's original: made by Produce or adopt
    bool adopted = false;
    std::size_t tier = 0;         // index into StoreState::tiers
    // Guarded by StoreState::mutex.
    EntryState state = EntryState::Pending;
    bool inRecord = true;         // false once evicted, detached or failed
    bool counted = false;         // `bytes` are included in tiers[tier].bytes
    bool linked = false;          // in tiers[tier].lru at lruPosition
    std::list<Entry*>::iterator lruPosition{};
    std::uint32_t readers = 0;    // read leases, plan steps reading it, results reserved for waiters
    std::uint32_t pins = 0;
    std::vector<Waiter*> waiters;
    std::optional<Error> failure;
    // Written under the mutex before `state` becomes Published, immutable afterwards: lease
    // holders and plan steps read them without the mutex.
    std::shared_ptr<IFrame> frame;
    std::vector<ViewSpec> views;  // frame->nativeViews()
    SyncPoint ready{};
    std::size_t bytes = 0;
};

struct Record {
    FrameDesc desc;
    FormatId unpacked = 0;                         // unpackedFormat(desc.format)
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Entry>> entries;   // published and pending
};

struct Origin {
    std::shared_ptr<ISource> source;
    FrameDesc desc;
    FormatId unpacked = 0;
};

struct Tier {
    Domain domain;
    std::size_t budget = 0;
    std::size_t bytes = 0;
    std::list<Entry*> lru;   // published in-index entries; front = least recently used
};

struct ConverterSlot {       // relaxed counters; Produce is slot 0
    std::string name;
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> runs{0};
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> bytes{0};
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> nanoseconds{0};
};

struct Counters {
    std::uint64_t hits = 0, misses = 0, creations = 0, evictions = 0, failures = 0;
};

struct StoreState {
    FrameStoreOptions options;
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Converter>> converters;   // fixed after create
    std::vector<std::size_t> converterSlot;               // converters[i] -> slots index
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<ConverterSlot>> slots;    // [0] = "Produce"

    mutable std::mutex mutex;                             // guards everything below
    std::unordered_map<OriginId, Origin> origins;
    OriginId nextOrigin = 1;
    std::unordered_map<FrameKey, Record, FrameKeyHash> records;
    std::vector<Tier> tiers;                              // [0] host, then staging, device
    std::unordered_map<std::uint64_t, LeaseInfo> leases;
    std::uint64_t nextLease = 1;
    Counters counters;
};

struct Step {
    const Converter* converter = nullptr;   // null: Produce
    std::shared_ptr<Entry> output;          // pending, created by this plan
};
struct Plan {
    std::shared_ptr<Entry> start;           // input of steps[0]; null when steps[0] is Produce
    ViewSpec startView{};
    bool startPending = false;              // join `start` first; otherwise one reader is held
    std::shared_ptr<ISource> source;        // Produce only (R11)
    std::vector<Step> steps;
};
struct Prepared {
    enum class Kind : std::uint8_t { Hit, Join, Plan };
    Kind kind = Kind::Hit;
    std::shared_ptr<Entry> entry;           // Hit/Join: the chosen entry; Plan: the last output
    ViewSpec view{};                        // the view the lease will have
    Plan plan;
};

// co_await JoinAwaiter(state, entry, executor): waits until a pending entry is published (one
// reader reserved for the caller) or failed (its error). Resumed by the completer through a post
// to `resume_on`; continues inline when the entry completed before the caller could register.
class JoinAwaiter {
public:
    JoinAwaiter(StoreState& state, std::shared_ptr<Entry> entry, IExecutor& resume_on) noexcept
        : state_(&state), entry_(std::move(entry)) {
        waiter_.resumeOn = &resume_on;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] bool await_ready() const noexcept { return false; }
    bool await_suspend(std::coroutine_handle<> continuation) {
        waiter_.continuation = continuation;
        const std::scoped_lock lock(state_->mutex);
        switch (entry_->state) {
            case EntryState::Pending:
                entry_->waiters.push_back(&waiter_);   // nothing of *this is touched after unlock
                return true;
            case EntryState::Published:
                ++entry_->readers;
                return false;
            case EntryState::Failed:
                waiter_.error = entry_->failure;
                return false;
        }
        return false;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] Result<void> await_resume() {
        if (waiter_.error) {
            return std::unexpected(std::move(*waiter_.error));
        }
        return {};
    }

private:
    StoreState* state_;
    std::shared_ptr<Entry> entry_;
    Waiter waiter_;
};

// Test and converter hooks.
struct StoreAccess {
    struct TestDomains {
        std::optional<Domain> staging;
        std::optional<Domain> device;
    };
    // A store with these converters (instead of the defaults) and optional fake domains.
    [[nodiscard]] static Result<std::shared_ptr<FrameStore>> create(
        FrameStoreOptions options, std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Converter>> converters,
        TestDomains domains = {});
    // A frame owned by the store (CreationKey<FrameStore>), for converters.
    template <class T, class... Args>
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<T> makeFrame(FrameStore& store, Args&&... args);
    // Waiters registered on the pending entries of a key.
    [[nodiscard]] static std::size_t waiterCount(const FrameStore& store, const FrameKey& key);
    // Internal with the first violated rule: per tier, bytes == the sum of its counted entries
    // and every linked entry is published, in the index and counted; no empty record; per entry,
    // read leases <= readers and pin leases == pins. Call while no operation runs.
    [[nodiscard]] static Result<void> checkInvariants(const FrameStore& store);
    [[nodiscard]] static StoreState& state(FrameStore& store) noexcept;
};

}  // namespace dvv::media::detail
```

`StoreAccess::makeFrame<T>` is defined inline in `store_state.h`: `return std::make_shared<T>(FrameStore::frameKey<T>(store.objectId()), std::forward<Args>(args)...);`. `frameKey` is a private static member of `FrameStore`, `template <class T> static CreationKey<FrameStore> frameKey(ObjectId owner) { return CreationKey<FrameStore>(LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<T>(owner)); }`, because only `FrameStore` can construct its key; `StoreAccess` is a friend.

The private members of `FrameStore` are the functions in the next block plus `prepareLocked`, `planLocked`, `createWith`, `slotOf` and `frameKey`.

**Code (binding): selection, acquire, plan execution, publication, release** (`src/frame_store.cpp`; `s` is `*state_`):

```cpp
namespace {

[[nodiscard]] bool satisfies(const ViewSpec& view, const ViewSpec& want) noexcept {
    return view.format == want.format && view.colour == want.colour && view.api == want.api &&
           view.domain == want.domain && view.downscaleLog2 == want.downscaleLog2 &&
           (want.layout == LayoutPolicy::Any || view.layout == want.layout);
}

[[nodiscard]] int rank(Fidelity fidelity) noexcept {
    return fidelity == Fidelity::Exact ? 2 : fidelity == Fidelity::Lossless ? 1 : 0;
}

}  // namespace

// The best entry for `want` (§5.2): Exact > Lossless > Derived, then published before pending,
// then insertion order. Derived never serves an original-format request. Caller holds the mutex.
// Task 7b moves this function to planner.h unchanged, so it can be tested on hand-made entries.
std::pair<std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry>, ViewSpec> selectEntry(
    std::span<const std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry>> entries, const ViewSpec& want,
    FormatId original) {
    std::pair<std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry>, ViewSpec> best{};
    int best_score = -1;
    for (const auto& entry : entries) {
        if (entry->state == detail::EntryState::Failed ||
            (entry->fidelity == Fidelity::Derived && want.format == original)) {
            continue;
        }
        const bool published = entry->state == detail::EntryState::Published;
        const std::span<const ViewSpec> views =
            published ? std::span<const ViewSpec>(entry->views) : std::span(&entry->planned, 1);
        for (const ViewSpec& view : views) {
            if (!satisfies(view, want)) {
                continue;
            }
            const int score = rank(entry->fidelity) * 2 + (published ? 1 : 0);
            if (score > best_score) {
                best_score = score;
                best = {entry, view};
            }
            break;
        }
    }
    return best;
}

Task<Result<ReadLease>> FrameStore::acquire(FrameKey key, ViewSpec want, IExecutor& resume_on,
                                            std::source_location where) {
    const std::shared_ptr<FrameStore> self = shared_from_this();  // the store outlives the call
    Result<detail::LeaseCore> core =
        co_await acquireCore(key, want, LeaseKind::Read, resume_on, where);
    Result<ReadLease> result = core ? Result<ReadLease>(ReadLease(std::move(*core)))
                                    : Result<ReadLease>(std::unexpected(std::move(core).error()));
    co_await resumeOn(resume_on);  // D12: never inline, hits included
    co_return result;
}
// pin(): the same with LeaseKind::Pin and PinLease.

Task<Result<detail::LeaseCore>> FrameStore::acquireCore(FrameKey key, ViewSpec want,
                                                        LeaseKind kind, IExecutor& resume_on,
                                                        std::source_location where) {
    detail::StoreState& s = *state_;
    Result<detail::Prepared> prepared = makeError(Errc::Internal, "not prepared");
    {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(s.mutex);
        prepared = prepareLocked(key, want, kind);
    }
    if (!prepared) {
        co_return std::unexpected(std::move(prepared).error());
    }
    detail::Prepared& p = *prepared;
    if (p.kind == detail::Prepared::Kind::Join) {
        const Result<void> joined = co_await detail::JoinAwaiter(s, p.entry, resume_on);
        if (!joined) {
            co_return std::unexpected(joined.error());
        }
    } else if (p.kind == detail::Prepared::Kind::Plan) {
        const Result<void> ran = co_await runPlan(std::move(p.plan));
        if (!ran) {
            co_return std::unexpected(ran.error());
        }
    }
    // One reader of p.entry is reserved for this requester now.
    co_return finishLease(std::move(p.entry), p.view, kind, where);
}

detail::LeaseCore FrameStore::finishLease(std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry> entry, ViewSpec view,
                                          LeaseKind kind, std::source_location where) {
    Binding binding;
    try {
        binding = entry->frame->bind(view);  // published: immutable, read without the mutex
    } catch (...) {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(state_->mutex);
        releaseReaderLocked(*entry);
        throw;
    }
    std::uint64_t id = 0;
    {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(state_->mutex);
        id = state_->nextLease++;
        state_->leases.emplace(id, LeaseInfo{entry->key, view, kind, where});
        if (kind == LeaseKind::Pin) {
            --entry->readers;
            ++entry->pins;
        }
        touchLocked(*entry);
    }
    return {.store = shared_from_this(), .entry = std::move(entry), .view = view,
            .binding = std::move(binding), .id = id, .kind = kind};
}

Task<Result<void>> FrameStore::runPlan(detail::Plan plan) {
    detail::StoreState& s = *state_;
    std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry> input = plan.start;
    ViewSpec input_view = plan.startView;
    if (input && plan.startPending) {
        // Another acquire makes the input: wait for it on the CPU executor.
        const Result<void> joined = co_await detail::JoinAwaiter(s, input, *s.options.cpu);
        if (!joined) {
            failSteps(plan, 0, joined.error());
            co_return std::unexpected(joined.error());
        }
    }
    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < plan.steps.size(); ++index) {
        detail::Step& step = plan.steps[index];
        Result<std::shared_ptr<IFrame>> made = co_await runStep(plan, step, input, input_view);
        std::vector<detail::Waiter*> woken;
        std::optional<Error> error;
        {
            const std::scoped_lock lock(s.mutex);
            if (made) {
                // On failure the frame stays in `made` and is released after unlocking.
                if (Result<void> published = publishLocked(*step.output, *made, woken);
                    !published) {
                    error = std::move(published).error();
                }
            } else {
                error = made.error();
            }
            if (input) {
                releaseReaderLocked(*input);   // the step is done with its input
            }
            if (error) {
                failStepsLocked(plan, index, *error, woken);
            }
        }
        wake(woken);
        if (error) {
            co_return std::unexpected(std::move(*error));
        }
        input = step.output;   // publishLocked counted one reader for the plan
        input_view = step.output->planned;
    }
    co_return Result<void>{};
}

Task<Result<std::shared_ptr<IFrame>>> FrameStore::runStep(const detail::Plan& plan,
                                                          const detail::Step& step,
                                                          std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry> input,
                                                          ViewSpec input_view) {
    detail::StoreState& s = *state_;
    const std::size_t slot =
        step.converter == nullptr ? 0 : slotOf(*step.converter);   // index into s.slots
    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    Result<std::shared_ptr<IFrame>> made = makeError(Errc::Internal, "not run");
    const std::string_view name =
        step.converter == nullptr ? std::string_view("Produce") : step.converter->name();
    try {
        if (step.converter == nullptr) {
            co_await resumeOn(*s.options.cpu);   // D12: the hop before every Produce
            ProduceContext context(*this, step.output->key, step.output->desc, *s.options.io);
            const Result<FrameRef> produced =
                co_await plan.source->produce(step.output->key.index, context);
            if (!produced) {
                made = std::unexpected(produced.error());
            } else if (!context.frame_) {
                made = makeError(Errc::Internal,
                                 std::format("source did not publish frame {} of origin {}",
                                             step.output->key.index, step.output->key.origin));
            } else {
                made = std::move(context.frame_);
            }
        } else {
            const Binding input_binding = input->frame->bind(input_view);
            made = co_await step.converter->run(
                detail::ConvertInput{input->key, input->desc, input_view, *input->frame, input_binding},
                detail::ConvertContext{*this, *s.options.cpu, s.options.gpu.get(),
                                       step.output->planned});
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& thrown) {
        made = makeError(Errc::Internal, std::format("{} threw: {}", name, thrown.what()));
    } catch (...) {
        made = makeError(Errc::Internal, std::format("{} threw", name));
    }
    detail::ConverterSlot& counters = *s.slots[slot];
    counters.runs.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (made && *made) {
        counters.bytes.fetch_add((*made)->bytes(), std::memory_order_relaxed);
    }
    counters.nanoseconds.fetch_add(
        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::nanoseconds>(
                                       std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - started)
                                       .count()),
        std::memory_order_relaxed);
    co_return made;
}

// Publishes a step's output: checks the frame, records it, reserves one reader for the plan (its
// next step or the requester) and one per waiter, and hands the waiters to the caller to wake
// after unlocking. On failure nothing changes and `frame` keeps the frame.
Result<void> FrameStore::publishLocked(detail::Entry& entry, std::shared_ptr<IFrame>& frame,
                                       std::vector<detail::Waiter*>& woken) {
    detail::StoreState& s = *state_;
    if (!frame || frame->desc() != entry.desc) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal,
                         std::format("frame {} of origin {}: the step made no frame or one with "
                                     "another description",
                                     entry.key.index, entry.key.origin));
    }
    const std::span<const ViewSpec> views = frame->nativeViews();
    if (std::ranges::find(views, entry.planned) == views.end()) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal,
                         std::format("frame {} of origin {}: the made frame lacks the planned "
                                     "view",
                                     entry.key.index, entry.key.origin));
    }
    // Everything that can throw comes first.
    entry.views.assign(views.begin(), views.end());
    woken.reserve(woken.size() + entry.waiters.size());
    detail::Tier& tier = s.tiers[entry.tier];
    if (entry.inRecord) {
        tier.lru.push_back(&entry);
        entry.lruPosition = std::prev(tier.lru.end());
        entry.linked = true;
    }
    if (auto* host = dynamic_cast<HostFrame*>(frame.get())) {
        host->seal();
    }
    entry.frame = std::move(frame);
    entry.bytes = entry.frame->bytes();
    entry.state = detail::EntryState::Published;
    if (entry.inRecord) {
        tier.bytes += entry.bytes;
        entry.counted = true;
    }
    entry.readers += 1 + static_cast<std::uint32_t>(entry.waiters.size());
    woken.insert(woken.end(), entry.waiters.begin(), entry.waiters.end());
    entry.waiters.clear();
    ++s.counters.creations;
    return {};
}

// Fails the pending outputs from step `from` on: their waiters get the error; the entries leave
// the index (failures are not cached).
void FrameStore::failStepsLocked(detail::Plan& plan, std::size_t from, const Error& error,
                                 std::vector<detail::Waiter*>& woken) {
    for (std::size_t index = from; index < plan.steps.size(); ++index) {
        detail::Entry& entry = *plan.steps[index].output;
        if (entry.state != detail::EntryState::Pending) {
            continue;
        }
        entry.state = detail::EntryState::Failed;
        entry.failure = error;
        for (detail::Waiter* waiter : entry.waiters) {
            waiter->error = error;
            woken.push_back(waiter);
        }
        entry.waiters.clear();
        static_cast<void>(removeFromIndexLocked(entry));   // the plan still holds the entry
        ++state_->counters.failures;
    }
}

void FrameStore::failSteps(detail::Plan& plan, std::size_t from, const Error& error) {
    std::vector<detail::Waiter*> woken;
    {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(state_->mutex);
        failStepsLocked(plan, from, error, woken);
    }
    wake(woken);
}

// Posts every waiter to its own executor (D12), after the mutex is released. A throwing post
// would strand a coroutine, so it terminates (noexcept).
void FrameStore::wake(std::span<detail::Waiter* const> waiters) noexcept {
    for (detail::Waiter* const waiter : waiters) {
        const std::coroutine_handle<> continuation = waiter->continuation;
        IExecutor* const executor = waiter->resumeOn;
        executor->post([continuation] { continuation.resume(); });
    }
}

void FrameStore::releaseReaderLocked(detail::Entry& entry) noexcept {
    --entry.readers;
    touchLocked(entry);
}

void FrameStore::touchLocked(detail::Entry& entry) noexcept {
    if (entry.linked) {
        std::list<detail::Entry*>& lru = state_->tiers[entry.tier].lru;
        lru.splice(lru.end(), lru, entry.lruPosition);
    }
}

// Takes the entry out of the index: LRU, tier bytes, record (erased when empty). Returns the
// record's reference to it, for release after unlocking (null when it was not in a record).
std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry> FrameStore::removeFromIndexLocked(detail::Entry& entry) noexcept {
    detail::StoreState& s = *state_;
    detail::Tier& tier = s.tiers[entry.tier];
    if (entry.linked) {
        tier.lru.erase(entry.lruPosition);
        entry.linked = false;
    }
    if (entry.counted) {
        tier.bytes -= entry.bytes;
        entry.counted = false;
    }
    std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry> owned;
    if (entry.inRecord) {
        entry.inRecord = false;
        const auto record = s.records.find(entry.key);
        if (record != s.records.end()) {
            auto& entries = record->second.entries;
            const auto found = std::ranges::find_if(
                entries, [&entry](const auto& candidate) { return candidate.get() == &entry; });
            if (found != entries.end()) {
                owned = std::move(*found);
                entries.erase(found);
            }
            if (entries.empty()) {
                s.records.erase(record);
            }
        }
    }
    return owned;
}

// Evicts unleased, unpinned entries of a tier, least recently used first, until it holds at most
// `target` bytes. Task 7b adds the fidelity order (Derived, Lossless, Exact, then LRU).
void FrameStore::evictLocked(std::size_t tier_index, std::size_t target,
                             std::vector<std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry>>& dropped) {
    detail::Tier& tier = state_->tiers[tier_index];
    dropped.reserve(dropped.size() + tier.lru.size());   // push_back below cannot throw
    for (auto position = tier.lru.begin(); position != tier.lru.end() && tier.bytes > target;) {
        detail::Entry& entry = **position;
        ++position;   // removal invalidates only the removed entry's iterator
        if (entry.readers == 0 && entry.pins == 0) {
            dropped.push_back(removeFromIndexLocked(entry));
            ++state_->counters.evictions;
        }
    }
}

void FrameStore::releaseLease(detail::LeaseCore& core) noexcept {
    const std::scoped_lock lock(state_->mutex);
    detail::Entry& entry = *core.entry;
    if (core.kind == LeaseKind::Pin) {
        --entry.pins;
    } else {
        --entry.readers;
    }
    state_->leases.erase(core.id);
    touchLocked(entry);
}

ReadLease::~ReadLease() {
    reset();
}

void ReadLease::reset() noexcept {
    if (core_.store) {
        core_.store->releaseLease(core_);
    }
    core_.binding = {};
    core_.entry.reset();   // may free the frame: after unlocking
    core_.store.reset();   // last: may destroy the store
}

ReadLease& ReadLease::operator=(ReadLease&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
        reset();
        core_ = std::exchange(other.core_, {});
    }
    return *this;
}
```

`prepareLocked`, `planLocked`, `attach`, `detach`, `adopt`, `trim`, `stats`, `outstandingLeases`, `createWith`, `ProduceContext` and `HostFrame` are routine code that follows the Behaviour rules. `trim` and `detach` collect dropped entries into a local `std::vector<std::shared_ptr<detail::Entry>>` declared **before** the lock, so they are released after it.

**Test fakes** (`tests/store_fakes.h/.cpp`, namespace `dvv::media::test`):
- `Gate`: `auto wait(IExecutor& executor)` returns an awaiter. It suspends until `open()` and then resumes through `executor.post`; it passes at once if the gate is already open. `open()` posts every queued waiter. `std::size_t waiting() const`. Mutex-protected vector of `{handle, executor}`:

  ```cpp
  class Gate {
  public:
      auto wait(IExecutor& executor) {
          struct Awaiter {
              Gate* gate;
              IExecutor* executor;
              bool await_ready() const noexcept { return false; }
              bool await_suspend(std::coroutine_handle<> handle) {
                  const std::scoped_lock lock(gate->mutex_);
                  if (gate->open_) {
                      return false;
                  }
                  gate->waiters_.emplace_back(handle, executor);
                  return true;
              }
              void await_resume() const noexcept {}
          };
          return Awaiter{this, &executor};
      }
      void open() {
          std::vector<std::pair<std::coroutine_handle<>, IExecutor*>> waiters;
          {
              const std::scoped_lock lock(mutex_);
              open_ = true;
              waiters.swap(waiters_);
          }
          for (auto& [handle, executor] : waiters) {
              executor->post([handle] { handle.resume(); });
          }
      }
      std::size_t waiting() const {
          const std::scoped_lock lock(mutex_);
          return waiters_.size();
      }
  private:
      mutable std::mutex mutex_;
      bool open_ = false;
      std::vector<std::pair<std::coroutine_handle<>, IExecutor*>> waiters_;
  };
  ```
- `FakeSource final : ISource, Managed<FakeSource>`: constructor `(CreationKey<dvv::test::TestAuthority>, FrameDesc desc, std::int64_t frames)`; created with `dvv::test::TestAuthority::makeShared<FakeSource>(…)`. Public knobs:
  - `std::atomic<int> attempts`, `produced`;
  - `std::atomic<int> failNext` (that many produces return `Io "fake read error"`);
  - `std::atomic<bool> throwNext` (throws `std::runtime_error("boom")`);
  - `Gate* gate` (awaited on `ctx.io()` before allocating).

  `produce`:
  1. Index check, then `++attempts`.
  2. `co_await resumeOn(ctx.io())`; gate; failure and throw knobs.
  3. `allocateHost(desc, bytes, 64, 0)`; fill `data()` with `pattern(index, i) = (index * 31 + i) & 0xFF`.
  4. `publish`, then `++produced`.
- `FakeFrame final : IFrame, Managed<FakeFrame>`: `(CreationKey<TestAuthority>, FrameDesc, std::vector<ViewSpec> views, std::vector<std::byte> payload)`. `bind` returns a `HostPlanesB` over the payload (one plane) for any native view; `bytes() = payload.size()`.
- `FakeConverter : detail::Converter`: constructed from `Config{std::string name; StateKey from, to; LayoutPolicy layout = Tight; Fidelity fidelity = Lossless; double cost = 1; Gate* gate = nullptr;}`. Knobs (all `mutable`, because `run` is `const`): `mutable std::atomic<int> runs`, `failNext`, `mutable std::atomic<Errc> failWith{Errc::Io}`, `mutable std::atomic<bool> wrongView` (the frame's view gets `layout = RowAlign256`). `run` (`const`):
  1. `co_await resumeOn(ctx.cpu)`, then the gate, then `++runs`.
  2. The failure knob.
  3. Copy plane 0 of the input `HostPlanesB` into the payload (the fake frames and host frames both bind as `HostPlanesB`).
  4. Return `TestAuthority::makeShared<FakeFrame>(input.desc, {ctx.output}, payload)`.
- `CountingExecutor final : IExecutor`: forwards `post` to a target and counts posts (atomic).
- `waitUntil(std::function<bool()> predicate)`: polls every 1 ms for up to 10 s and `REQUIRE`s that it became true.
- Fixture `StoreTest`:
  - Pools `cpu` (4 threads), `io` (2) and `requester` (1), from `ExecutorFactory::makeThreadPool`.
  - `desc = FrameDesc{*findFormat("gray"), 0, 16, 8}` (128 bytes).
  - `hostView = ViewSpec{desc.format, 0, LayoutPolicy::Any, Api::HostPlanes, Domain{}, 0}`.
  - `planesView`, the same with `unpackedFormat(desc.format)`.
  - `makeStore(converters, domains, options tweaks)` via `StoreAccess::create`.

**Tests first** (`tests/test_frame_store.cpp`, `TEST_SUITE("store")`, fixture `StoreTest`; `syncWait` drives the coroutines from the test thread):
1. `acquire produces the original once and then hits`: attach a `FakeSource` (4 frames). `syncWait(store->acquire({o, 1}, hostView, *requester))` has a value.
   - The lease: `fidelity() == Exact`; `HostPlanesB` with `planeCount 1`, `strides[0] == 16`, `planes[0].size() == 128`, bytes `== pattern(1, i)`.
   - A second acquire has the same `planes[0].data()`.
   - `produced == 1`; stats `hits 1, misses 1, creations 1, frames 1, leases 2`.
2. `32 concurrent acquires of one view produce once`: the source's gate is closed. Run `syncWait(whenAll(std::vector<Task<Result<ReadLease>>>{32 × store->acquire({o,0}, hostView, *requester)}))` on the test thread. A `std::jthread` opener does `waitUntil([&]{ return gate.waiting() == 1 && StoreAccess::waiterCount(*store, {o,0}) == 31; })`, then `gate.open()`.
   - All 32 have values with the same `planes[0].data()`.

3. `a failed produce is not cached`: `failNext = 1`. The first acquire is `Errc::Io`; stats `failures 1`, `frames 0`. The second acquire has a value. `attempts == 2`, `produced == 1`.
4. `joiners of a failed creation get its error`: gate plus `failNext = 1`, 4 concurrent acquires (open after `waiterCount == 3`). All 4 are `Errc::Io` with message `fake read error`; `failures == 1`. A fifth acquire succeeds.
5. `an exception in produce is Internal`: `throwNext`. The result is `Errc::Internal` and the message contains `threw: boom`.
6. `every requester resumes on its own executor`:
   - Pools `a` and `b` (1 thread each); their thread ids are learnt by posting.
   - Gate closed. Coroutine `X` acquires with `resume_on = *a` and records `std::this_thread::get_id()` after its `co_await`; `Y` does the same with `*b`, for the same key. Both run through `whenAll`; open after `waiterCount == 1`.
   - `X`'s id equals `a`'s and `Y`'s equals `b`'s.
   - A later hit with `resume_on = counting` (a `CountingExecutor` over `b`) gives `counting.posts() == 1` (resumed by a post, not inline).
7. `a released lease touches the LRU`:
   - Acquire frames 0, 1 and 2 and drop the leases, then acquire frame 0 again and drop it.
   - `trim(hostDomain(), 256)` (two 128-byte frames) evicts frame 1: re-acquiring 1 increments `produced`, while 0 and 2 hit (`hits` grows by 2).
   - `checkInvariants` succeeds.
8. `leased and pinned entries are not trimmed`:
   - Hold a lease on 0 and `syncWait(store->pin({o,1}, hostView, *requester))` on 1, then `trim(host, 0)`.
   - `tiers[0].bytes == 256`, `frames == 2`, `pins == 1`, `leases == 1`.
   - Drop both, then `trim(host, 0)`: `bytes 0`, `frames 0`, `evictions 2`.
9. `outstandingLeases names the acquiring location`:
   - `const auto here = std::source_location::current();` then `auto lease = syncWait(store->acquire(key, hostView, *requester, here)).value();`.
   - `outstandingLeases()` has one entry with `where.line() == here.line()`, `kind Read`, `key == key`, `view == lease.view()`.
   - A pin adds one with `kind Pin`. After both are dropped the list is empty.
10. `adopt inserts an original; a second adopt is Internal`:
    - With no attach, adopt a `FakeFrame` with views `{desc.format, 0, Tight, HostPlanes, Domain{}, 0}` for `{77, 0}`: a lease with `fidelity Exact`.
    - `acquire({77,0}, hostView)` hits.
    - Adopting again gives `Internal` containing `already has an original`.
    - A frame with a view of another format gives `Internal`.
    - With the source's gate closed and a pending acquire of `{o,2}`, adopting for `{o,2}` gives `Internal`; open the gate afterwards.
11. `detach drops unleased entries and refuses later acquires`:

    - `detach(1)`. The lease on 0 still reads `pattern(0, i)`; `frames` counts only origin 2's entries (0); `acquire({1, 1})` gives `Internal` containing `origin 1 is not attached`.
    - Origin 2 still produces.

12. `acquire and drop leases while another thread trims`: 8 `std::jthread`s each run 200 iterations of `syncWait(store->acquire({o, i % 4}, hostView, *requester))` and drop the lease. A trimmer `std::jthread` loops `store->trim(hostDomain(), 0)` until the workers are done. Afterwards `checkInvariants` succeeds, every acquire had a value, and stats show `leases 0` and `pins 0` (the ASan stress target; also the TSan target once Linux returns).
13. `the store and its frames are gone after the last reference`: take the baseline `LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount()` after the pools exist. Create the store and source, acquire 3 frames, drop all leases, the store and the source. `liveCount()` is back at the baseline.
14. `a view no converter makes is Unsupported`: a store without converters; `acquire(planesView)` gives `Unsupported` containing `no converter path`, and `produced == 0`.
15. `Produce then one converter, single flight`: converter `Unpack` (PRODUCED → `{unpacked, HostPlanes, Host}`, Lossless).
