16. `a frame without the planned view fails the creation`: `Unpack` with `wrongView = true` (the fake frame's only view has `layout RowAlign256`) makes `acquire(planesView)` fail with `Internal` containing `lacks the planned view`. Stats `failures 1`; `checkInvariants` succeeds; the produced original stays published (`frames 1`).

- `ready()` returns the entry's `SyncPoint`, which is always reached in M2: converters complete their GPU work before publishing.
- Review rule for `Entry::frame` and `views` (and the hint if ASan or a later TSan run reports them): these are written once under the mutex before `state` becomes Published and read only by holders of a reader. Look for a reader that was not counted (a missing `++readers` in a path), not for a missing lock.
- A coroutine frame that is never resumed would leak; the waiter design (completer posts every waiter) and `wake` being `noexcept` prevent that.

### Task 7b: Planner, tiers and fidelity (dvv-media)

**Goal:** Replace Task 7a's fixed plans with the planner of §5.2/§5.3: the cheapest converter path over the store's static graph, starting from Produce or from the key's published and pending entries. Add the rest of the store's memory behaviour:
- a permanent no-path cache;
- the fidelity rules;
- eviction order and budgets;
- out-of-memory retry;
- converter statistics.

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/src/planner.h` and `src/planner.cpp`: `PlanSource`, `FoundPath`, `findPath()`, and `selectEntry()` (moved from `frame_store.cpp` unchanged).
- Modify `dvv-media/src/store_state.h`:
  - `NoPathKey` and `StoreState::noPath`;
  - `StoreState::hostAllocator`;
  - new `StoreAccess` hooks `noPathCount`, `setHostAllocator` and `views`.
- Modify `dvv-media/src/frame_store.cpp`:
  - `planLocked` uses `findPath` and the no-path cache;
  - `evictLocked` follows the fidelity order;
  - `evictIfOverBudget` runs after every acquire and adopt;
  - out-of-memory retry in `runPlan` and `ProduceContext::allocateHost`;
  - `createWith` drops converters whose states use a memory kind the store has no tier for.
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`: add `src/planner.cpp`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_planner.cpp` and add it to `dvv_media_tests`. Extend `tests/test_frame_store.cpp`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7a (all of `store_state.h`, `converters.h`, the store core and the fakes in `tests/store_fakes.h`); Task 2 `geometry()`.
- Produces: the complete store behaviour of §5.2/§5.3 that Task 8 relies on. Task 8 only registers real converters in `FrameStore::create` and adds `acquireOriginal`. Public headers do not change.

```cpp
// src/planner.h
namespace dvv::media::detail {

// One way into the graph: an existing entry's view (cost 0) or Produce (the frame's bytes).
struct PlanSource {
    StateKey state;
    LayoutPolicy layout = LayoutPolicy::Tight;
    Fidelity fidelity = Fidelity::Exact;
    double cost = 0;
};
struct FoundPath {
    std::size_t source = 0;                  // index into the sources given to findPath
    std::vector<const Converter*> steps;     // in execution order; may be empty
    Fidelity fidelity = Fidelity::Exact;     // the worst of the source's and the steps'
    double cost = 0;                         // the source's cost plus the steps' costs
};

// The cheapest path from any source to `target` with at most `max_depth` converters. Its last
// converter (or a zero-step source) must give `layout` unless that is Any. Pass 1 ignores Derived
// sources and Derived converters; only when pass 1 finds nothing and `allow_derived` does pass 2
// use them. Ties: fewer steps, then the lower source index, then the converters' order.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<FoundPath> findPath(std::span<const PlanSource> sources,
                                                std::span<const Converter* const> converters,
                                                const FrameDesc& desc, StateKey target,
                                                LayoutPolicy layout, std::size_t max_depth,
                                                bool allow_derived);

// Unchanged from Task 7a (moved here so it can be tested on hand-made entries).
[[nodiscard]] std::pair<std::shared_ptr<Entry>, ViewSpec> selectEntry(
    std::span<const std::shared_ptr<Entry>> entries, const ViewSpec& want, FormatId original);
}

// src/store_state.h additions
struct NoPathKey {
    FormatId format = 0;       // the original format
    StateKey target;
    LayoutPolicy layout = LayoutPolicy::Any;
    bool operator==(const NoPathKey&) const = default;
};
// StoreState: std::vector<NoPathKey> noPath;   (guarded by the mutex; a handful of entries)
//             std::function<Result<platform::AlignedBuffer>(std::size_t, std::size_t)>
//                 hostAllocator = platform::allocateAligned;   (test seam, set before use)
// StoreAccess:
//   static std::size_t noPathCount(const FrameStore&);
//   static void setHostAllocator(FrameStore&, std::function<Result<platform::AlignedBuffer>(std::size_t, std::size_t)>);
//   // planned view and fidelity of every published in-index entry of a key, in insertion order
//   static std::vector<std::pair<ViewSpec, Fidelity>> views(const FrameStore&, const FrameKey&);
```

Note (plan choice): `findPath` does bounded relaxation over `(depth, state)` (Bellman–Ford limited to `max_depth` steps) instead of the design's "Dijkstra". On at most 18 states it gives the same cheapest path, honours `maxPlanDepth` exactly, and needs no priority queue.

Note (plan choice): the no-path cache is filled only when the key's origin has an attached source **and** its record holds no adopted root. Then every existing state is reachable from Produce, so "no path" depends only on `(original format, target, layout)` and is permanent (§5.2). With an adopted root (no source, or another state), a failure is returned but not cached. The design's "colour-equal" part of the key is implicit: a colour mismatch returns before the cache is consulted, and is never cached.

**Behaviour:**
- **`findPath`.** Edges are the converters whose `from()` and `to()` are states. The weight of an edge is `converter->cost(desc)`.
  - The algorithm:
    1. `best[0][s] = min cost over sources at state s`, with fidelity tracked per entry.
    2. For each `d` in `1..max_depth`, relax every edge from `best[d-1]`.
    3. A path ends at `target` when the last edge's `outputLayout()` matches `layout` (`Any` matches all). A zero-step path ends there when its source's `layout` matches.
    4. Choose the cheapest ending over all depths; ties go to fewer steps.
  - Pass 1 skips sources and converters with fidelity `Derived`. Pass 2 runs (when `allow_derived`) only if pass 1 found nothing.
  - The path's fidelity is the minimum (Exact > Lossless > Derived) of the source's and every step's.
- **`planLocked` (replaces Task 7a's rules).** Steps 1 and 2 (the colour check and `stateOf`) are unchanged from 7a.
  1. Colour check.
  2. `target = stateOf(want)` (an unrepresentable target returns `Unsupported "no converter path from <format> to <view>"`, not cached).
  3. If `noPath` contains `{rec.desc.format, target, want.layout}`, return `Unsupported "no converter path from <format> to <view>"`.
  4. Sources:
     - every in-index entry that is not failed, with each of its views (published: `views`; pending: `planned`), at cost 0 with the entry's fidelity;
     - plus Produce `{PRODUCED, Tight, Exact, cost = geometry(desc).bytes}` when the origin has a source and the record has no root.
  5. `findPath(sources, converters, rec.desc, target, want.layout, options.maxPlanDepth, allow_derived = want.format != rec.desc.format)`.
  6. If nothing is found: cache (under the rule above), then return `Unsupported "no converter path from <format> <api>/<kind> to <view>"`.
  7. Otherwise build the plan:
     - When the source is an entry, `start` = that entry with that view. If it is published, take one reader; if it is pending, set `startPending`.
     - When the source is Produce, `steps[0]` is Produce with `plan.source` from the origin.
     - Then one step per converter. Pending outputs are as in Task 7a, with fidelity cumulative from the source.
  8. `++misses`.
- **Eviction order** (`evictLocked(tier, target, dropped)`): up to three passes over the tier's LRU list, front to back. The first pass takes only Derived entries, the second Lossless, the third Exact. Each pass evicts unleased, unpinned entries until `bytes <= target`.
- **Budgets:** `evictIfOverBudget()` runs under the lock for every tier with `bytes > budget`: `evictLocked(t, budget, dropped)`. It is called:
  - at the end of `acquireCore` after a Plan (the requester's reader is already reserved by `publishLocked`, so its own entry is safe);
  - after `adopt`;
  - after `allocateHost`.

  Dropped entries are released after unlocking.
- **Out of memory:**
  - In `runPlan`, a converter step whose result is `OutOfMemory` triggers `trimForRetry(step.output->tier)`, i.e. `evictLocked(t, budget / 2)` under the lock with release afterwards. The step then runs **once** more; a second failure fails the plan with that error.
  - `ProduceContext::allocateHost` calls `state.hostAllocator(capacity, alignment)`. On `OutOfMemory` it calls `trimForRetry(0)` and retries once; a second failure returns that error.
  - A Produce step that returns `OutOfMemory` is not retried by `runPlan`: `allocateHost` already retried.
- **createWith:** a converter whose `from().kind` or `to().kind` has no tier in this store is not registered (a host-only store keeps only host-to-host converters). Slots are made only for registered converters.
- **Stats:** `converters` lists `"Produce"` first, then each registered converter name once, with `runs`, `bytes` (output frame bytes) and `nanoseconds` (wall time of `run`, including its executor hops).

**Tests first:**

`tests/test_planner.cpp` (`TEST_SUITE("planner")`, no store). It uses `FakeConverter` from `store_fakes.h` only as `Converter` objects; `desc` is gray 16×8. States: `P = PRODUCED`, `X = {false, VkBuffer, Host}`, `T = {true, HostPlanes, Host}`.
1. `the cheapest path wins`: converters `A: P→X cost 1`, `B: X→T cost 1` and `C: P→T cost 5`; source `{P, Tight, Exact, 0}`; target `T`, layout Any, depth 4. `steps == {&A, &B}` and `cost == 2`.
2. `the depth limit is exact`: `A` and `B` only. Depth 1 gives `nullopt`; depth 2 gives `{&A, &B}`.
3. `an existing entry is a free source`: sources `[{P, Tight, Exact, 10}, {X, Tight, Exact, 0}]`, converters `A` and `B`. `source == 1`, `steps == {&B}`, `cost == 1`.
4. `Derived only when allowed and nothing else exists`: `D: P→T Derived cost 1`, `L: P→T Lossless cost 9`.
   - Both with `allow_derived = true` gives `{&L}`, fidelity Lossless.
   - `D` alone with `allow_derived = false` gives `nullopt`; with `true`, `{&D}` and fidelity Derived.
5. `the last step must give the wanted layout`: `Q1: P→T layout Tight cost 1`, `Q2: P→T layout VkOptimal cost 9`. Layout `VkOptimal` gives `{&Q2}`; `Any` gives `{&Q1}`; `RowAlign256` gives `nullopt`.
6. `a source at the target is a zero-step path`: target `P`, layout `Tight`, source `{P, Tight, Exact, 128}` gives `steps.empty()` and `cost == 128`.
7. `selectEntry ranks fidelity, then published over pending`: hand-made `Entry` objects with planned and views equal to `planesView`.
   - `[Derived published, Lossless published]` selects the Lossless one.
   - Adding `Exact pending` selects the pending Exact one.
   - For an original-format want, `[Derived published at hostView]` selects nothing (`first == nullptr`).
   - Failed entries are never selected.

`tests/test_frame_store.cpp` additions (`TEST_SUITE("store")`). Each case ends with `REQUIRE(StoreAccess::checkInvariants(*store))`. `Unpack` means `FakeConverter{"Unpack", P, T, Tight, Lossless, 4}`.
1. `one produce for two concurrent views of a key`:
   - Converter `Unpack`, gate closed. `whenAll` of `acquire(hostView)` and `acquire(planesView)`; the opener waits for `gate.waiting() == 1 && waiterCount == 1`, then opens.
   - Both have values; `produced == 1`, `Unpack.runs == 1`, `noPathCount == 0`, `misses == 2`.
2. `no path is cached by format, colour and target only`: converter `Unpack`.
   - `acquire({o,0}, {gray, 0, Any, VkBuffer, Domain{}, 0})` gives `Unsupported` containing `no converter path`; `attempts == 0`, `noPathCount == 1`, `frames == 0`.
   - The same target for `{o,3}`, and for a second origin with the gray desc: still `noPathCount == 1`.
   - An origin with a `yuv420p` 16×8 desc: `noPathCount == 2`.
3. `a colour the frame does not have is Unsupported and not cached`: `hostView` with `colour = internColour({ColourMatrix::Bt709, ColourRange::Limited, TransferFn::Bt1886, Primaries::Bt709})` gives `Unsupported` containing `no colour conversion`, and `noPathCount == 0`.
4. `a domain the store does not have is Unsupported`: a host-only store; `hostView` with `domain = Domain{MemKind::DeviceLocal}` gives `Unsupported`, `noPathCount == 0`, `attempts == 0`.
5. `a multi-step plan publishes every step and accounts every tier`:
   - Fake domains: staging `Domain{MemKind::HostVisible}`, device `Domain{MemKind::DeviceLocal}`.
   - Converters: `ToStaging: P→{false, VkBuffer, HostVisible}` (Exact, 1); `Upload: {false, VkBuffer, HostVisible}→{false, VkBuffer, DeviceLocal}` (Exact, 2); `GpuUnpack: {false, VkBuffer, DeviceLocal}→{true, VkImage, DeviceLocal}` (layout VkOptimal, Lossless, 0.5).
   - `acquire({unpacked, 0, Any, VkImage, deviceDomain, 0})` has a value with fidelity Lossless; `creations == 4`; `tiers[0].bytes == 128`, `tiers[1].bytes == 128`, `tiers[2].bytes == 256`.
   - Then `acquire` of `{gray, VkBuffer, deviceDomain}` and of `{gray, VkBuffer, stagingDomain}` both hit (`hits` grows by 2, `creations` stays 4).
6. `maxPlanDepth bounds the plan`: the converters of 5 with `maxPlanDepth = 2`. The same acquire gives `Unsupported`; `noPathCount == 1`, `attempts == 0`.
7. `an original-format request never returns Derived`:
   - Converters `LossyCopy: P→X` (Derived, 1) and `BufUnpack: X→{true, VkBuffer, Host}` (Lossless, 1).
   - `acquire({unpacked, VkBuffer, Host})` has fidelity Derived.
   - `acquire({gray, VkBuffer, Host})` then gives `Unsupported` (the Derived entry exists but does not serve an original-format request).
   - A second store that also has `Copy: P→X` (Exact, 5) runs the same two acquires: the second returns fidelity Exact, `Copy.runs == 1`, and `StoreAccess::views` lists the X view twice, once Derived and once Exact.
8. `the planner prefers Lossless over a cheaper Derived converter`:
   - Converters `Lossy: P→T` (Derived, 1) and `Unpack` (Lossless, 4): `acquire(planesView)` has fidelity Lossless, `Unpack.runs == 1`, `Lossy.runs == 0`.
   - A store with `Lossy` only gives fidelity Derived.
9. `eviction order is Derived, Lossless, Exact, then LRU`:
   - Converters `Unpack` and `LossyBuf: P→{true, VkBuffer, Host}` (Derived, 1). Acquire and drop `hostView`, `planesView` and `{unpacked, VkBuffer, Host}` (384 bytes).
   - `trim(host, 256)`: `views` no longer has the Derived one.
   - `trim(host, 128)`: only the Exact root remains.
   - `trim(host, 0)`: `views` is empty, `evictions == 3`.
10. `over budget, unleased entries are evicted after an acquire`: `hostBudget = 256`.
    - Acquire frames 0–4 and keep the leases: `tiers[0].bytes == 640`, `evictions == 0`.
    - Drop the leases of 0–2, then acquire frame 5 and keep it: `tiers[0].bytes == 384`, `evictions == 3`, `frames == 3`.
11. `an OutOfMemory step evicts to half the budget and retries once`: `hostBudget = 512`, converter `Unpack` with `failWith = OutOfMemory`, `failNext = 1`.
    - Acquire and drop frames 1–3 (384 bytes).
    - `acquire(planesView of frame 0)` has a value; `Unpack.runs == 2`, `evictions == 2` (frames 1 and 2 go: 512 → 256).
    - With `failNext = 2` on a fresh store: the result is `Errc::OutOfMemory`, `Unpack.runs == 2`, `failures == 1`.
12. `allocateHost evicts and retries once`: `hostBudget = 512`.
    - Acquire and drop frames 1–3 (384). Then `setHostAllocator` to a counter that returns `OutOfMemory` on its first call and calls `platform::allocateAligned` afterwards.
    - `acquire(hostView of frame 0)` has a value, `calls == 2`, `evictions == 1` (384 → 256).
    - An allocator that always fails gives `Errc::OutOfMemory` and `calls == 2`.
13. `stats count converter runs and bytes`: converter `Unpack`; acquire `planesView` of frames 0 and 1.
    - `converters[0] == {"Produce", runs 2, bytes 256}` and `converters[1].name == "Unpack"` with `runs 2`, `bytes 256` (`nanoseconds` is not asserted).
    - `misses == 2`, `creations == 4`.
14. `layout Any matches; an unmade layout is Unsupported`: `hostView` with layout `Tight` hits after a first acquire; layout `RowAlign256` gives `Unsupported`, `noPathCount == 1`.
15. `trim of an unknown domain does nothing`: in a host-only store with one frame, `trim(Domain{MemKind::DeviceLocal}, 0)` leaves `frames == 1` and `evictions == 0`.

**Commit:** `feat: plan converter paths over the store's static graph with fidelity, budgets and eviction`, ending with the trailer.

**Notes:** Design §5.2 (pseudo-code and bullets: satisfies, fidelity, noPath, eviction order), §5.3, D10 (one mutex, OOM rule, no pooling), §7 (no-path and allocation rows), §8.1 (store row remainder), R7; arbiter FIX 1(a)–(c), (e), FIX 8 (converter stats), FIX 13.
- `StoreStats::converters` is the evidence for the `--via` path checks of Tasks 8 and 13 and the input of `bench` (Task 14), so its names must be exactly the converter names Task 8 registers: `HostToStaging`, `Upload`, `GpuUnpack`, `Readback`, `CpuUnpack`.
- Eviction under the budget never touches entries with readers or pins, so a tier may stay above budget while leases are held (case 10). That is intended (spec §6.5: leases keep views alive).
- No-path entries are never removed. The graph is static per store and the key holds no per-frame data, so the vector stays tiny (at most formats × 18 states × 4 layouts).

### Task 8: GPU frames and converters

**Goal:** Give the frame store its real converters (HostToStaging, Upload, GpuUnpack, Readback, CpuUnpack), the three GPU frame types, `acquireOriginal`/`OriginalRead` and the `originalView`/`planesView` helpers. An original read then reaches the unpacked planes on the GPU or on the CPU, bit-exact, and the converter stats prove which path ran.

**Files:**
- Modify `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/frame.h`: declare `StagingFrame`, `VkBufferFrame`, `VkPlanesFrame`.
- Modify `dvv-media/src/frames.cpp`: implement them.
- Modify `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/frame_store.h`: add `OriginalRead`, `FrameStore::acquireOriginal`, `originalView`, `planesView`. Add only what Tasks 7a/7b have not declared yet.
- Modify `dvv-media/src/frame_store.cpp`:
  - `acquireOriginal`;
  - `FrameStore::create` installs the real converters (and the unpack pipeline when `options.gpu` is set) instead of an empty or fake set.
- Modify `src/converters.h` (Task 7a; keep its interface): add `makeHostConverters`, `makeGpuConverters`. Create `src/converters.cpp`: the five converters.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/packed_source.h`: a test `ISource` whose frame *i* is `pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, format, w, h, seed, i).value())`.
- Create the tests:
  - `dvv-media/tests/test_original_read.cpp` (unit, host-only store);
  - `dvv-media/tests/gpu_test_store.cpp` (GPU).
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt` (`src/converters.cpp`) and `dvv-media/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (the new test files).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 2: `geometry`, `componentSize`, `unpackedFormat`, `descriptor`.
  - Task 3: `pack`, `unpackToPlanes` and the code-value generator `detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, format, width, height, seed, index)` of `src/patterns.h`.
  - Task 5: `GpuImage::view()`, STORAGE images, the `dispatch` overload.
  - Task 6: `src/gpu_unpack.h`, with the pipeline setup that checks `STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT` for R8_UINT/R16_UINT, `recordUnpack(recorder, pipeline, const GpuBuffer&, offset, desc, images)` with its `base` alignment and buffer-size checks, and `detail::unpackBufferBytes`.
  - Task 6: the `dvv_media_gpu_tests` executable and its fixture. The fixture gives a registry configured from the environment, a context on the best device and a CPU thread pool, checks a validation count of 0 at the end, and exits 77 without a usable device.
  - Task 7a: `IFrame`, the bindings, `HostFrame`, `FrameRef`, `ProduceContext`, `ISource`, leases, `attach`/`detach`, `adopt`, the `detail::Converter` interface (`from`, `to`, `outputLayout`, `fidelity`, `cost`, `run`), `StateKey`, `ConvertInput`/`ConvertContext`, `detail::StoreAccess::makeFrame`, and `stagingDomain()`/`deviceDomain()`.
  - Task 7b: the planner, tiers, `ConverterStats` counting around `run`, and the OOM retry.
- Produces (public, `frame.h`):

```cpp
namespace dvv::media {
// A frame in one host-visible GpuBuffer holding the tight layout of `format` (desc.format or
// unpackedFormat(desc.format)) at offset 0. Native views (format, desc.colour, Tight, domain):
// - HostReadback memory (made by Readback): HostPlanes and VkBuffer, the same bytes (one store
//   entry, counted once);
// - HostUpload memory (made by HostToStaging): VkBuffer only (the CPU never reads
//   write-combined memory).
class StagingFrame final : public IFrame, public Managed<StagingFrame> {
public:
    StagingFrame(CreationKey<FrameStore> key, FrameDesc desc, FormatId format, Domain domain,
                 std::shared_ptr<gpu::GpuBuffer> buffer, FrameGeometry geometry) noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] const std::shared_ptr<gpu::GpuBuffer>& buffer() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] const FrameDesc& desc() const noexcept override;
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const ViewSpec> nativeViews() const noexcept override;
    [[nodiscard]] Binding bind(const ViewSpec& native) const override;  // HostPlanesB or VkBufferB
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t bytes() const noexcept override;          // buffer->size()
};
// The original in a DeviceOnly buffer, padded to detail::unpackBufferBytes(0, geometry.bytes).
// One native view: (desc.format, desc.colour, Tight, VkBuffer, domain DeviceLocal).
class VkBufferFrame final : public IFrame, public Managed<VkBufferFrame> {
public:
    VkBufferFrame(CreationKey<FrameStore> key, FrameDesc desc, Domain domain,
                  std::shared_ptr<gpu::GpuBuffer> buffer, FrameGeometry geometry) noexcept;
    /* IFrame overrides; bind() gives VkBufferB{buffer, 0, geometry}; bytes() = buffer->size() */
};
// The unpacked planes (D5): one R8_UINT/R16_UINT image per component, layout GENERAL.
// One native view: (unpacked, desc.colour, VkOptimal, VkImage, domain DeviceLocal).
class VkPlanesFrame final : public IFrame, public Managed<VkPlanesFrame> {
public:
    VkPlanesFrame(CreationKey<FrameStore> key, FrameDesc desc, FormatId unpacked, Domain domain,
                  std::array<std::shared_ptr<gpu::GpuImage>, MAX_COMPONENTS> planes,
                  std::uint8_t plane_count) noexcept;
    /* IFrame overrides; bind() gives VkImageB; bytes() = sum of width*height*(1 or 2) */
};
}
```

- Produces (public, `frame_store.h`; exactly design §4.4):

```cpp
class OriginalRead {                        // §6.6: only FrameStore::acquireOriginal makes one
public:
    [[nodiscard]] const ReadLease& lease() const noexcept;  // fidelity Exact or Lossless
private:
    friend class FrameStore; explicit OriginalRead(ReadLease) noexcept;
    ReadLease lease_;
};
// In FrameStore:
// requireExact: the original format (Exact); else the unpacked format (Lossless). The colour is
// forced to the frame's own colour. Errc::Internal for a detached or unknown origin.
[[nodiscard]] Task<Result<OriginalRead>> acquireOriginal(
    FrameKey key, Api api, Domain domain, IExecutor& resume_on, bool requireExact = false,
    std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());
// Free functions:
[[nodiscard]] ViewSpec originalView(const FrameDesc&, Api, Domain);  // {desc.format, desc.colour, Any, api, domain, 0}
[[nodiscard]] ViewSpec planesView(const FrameDesc&, Api, Domain);    // {unpackedFormat(desc.format), desc.colour, Any, api, domain, 0}
```

Note (plan choice): `key` by value, as for `acquire`/`pin` (Task 7a, CP.53).

- Produces (private, `src/converters.h`):

```cpp
namespace dvv::media::detail {
// Host-only store: CpuUnpack.
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Converter>> makeHostConverters();
// Store with a context: HostToStaging, Upload, GpuUnpack, Readback, CpuUnpack. Creates the unpack
// pipeline through Task 6's setup (its Unsupported passes through).
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Converter>>> makeGpuConverters(
    std::shared_ptr<gpu::ExecutionContext> context, std::shared_ptr<IExecutor> cpu);
}
```

- Produces (stats names, used by Tasks 13 and 14 and their tests): `stats().converters` has exactly one entry per name. The names are `"Produce"`, `"HostToStaging"`, `"Upload"`, `"GpuUnpack"`, `"Readback"` and `"CpuUnpack"`; a host-only store has only `"Produce"` and `"CpuUnpack"`.

**Behaviour:**

States are `(original|unpacked, Api, MemKind)` (design §5.3). Use these edges. The cost factor applies to the bytes of the input view.

| Converter | From → to | Fidelity | Factor | outputLayout | Run |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| HostToStaging | (original, HostPlanes, Host) → (original, VkBuffer, HostVisible) | Exact | 1 | `Tight` | `co_await resumeOn(cpu)`; create a `HostUpload` buffer of `detail::unpackBufferBytes(0, bytes)` with usage `TRANSFER_SRC`; `memcpy` the original; `flush()`; output a `StagingFrame` (HostUpload: VkBuffer view only). |
| Upload | (original, VkBuffer, HostVisible) → (original, VkBuffer, DeviceLocal) | Exact | 2 | `Tight` | Create a `DeviceOnly` buffer of `detail::unpackBufferBytes(0, bytes)` with usage `TRANSFER_DST \| TRANSFER_SRC`; `co_await context->submit(*cpu, …copyBuffer(src, offset, dst, 0, bytes)…)`; output a `VkBufferFrame`. |
| GpuUnpack | (original, VkBuffer, DeviceLocal) → (unpacked, VkImage, DeviceLocal) | Lossless | 0.5 | `VkOptimal` | One image per component; one submission whose record function calls `recordUnpack`; output a `VkPlanesFrame` (code below). |
| Readback (buffer) | (original, VkBuffer, DeviceLocal) → (original, HostPlanes, HostVisible) | Exact | 2 | `Tight` | Create a `HostReadback` buffer of `geometry.bytes` with usage `TRANSFER_DST`; submit `copyBuffer`; `invalidate()`; output a `StagingFrame` (HostReadback: both views). |
| Readback (image) | (unpacked, VkImage, DeviceLocal) → (unpacked, HostPlanes, HostVisible) | Exact | 2 | `Tight` | Create a `HostReadback` buffer of `geometry(unpacked).bytes` with usage `TRANSFER_DST`; one submission with `copyImageToBuffer(*plane[c], *buffer, geometry.planes[c].offset)` per component; `invalidate()`; output a `StagingFrame` (both views). |
| CpuUnpack | (original, HostPlanes, Host) and (original, HostPlanes, HostVisible) → (unpacked, HostPlanes, Host) | Lossless | 4 | `Tight` | `co_await resumeOn(cpu)`; `auto buffer = co_try(platform::allocateAligned(g.bytes, 64))` (the coroutine form of `DVV_TRY`) with `g = geometry(unpacked, w, h)`; `frame = detail::StoreAccess::makeFrame<HostFrame>(context.store, input.desc, context.output, std::move(buffer), g.bytes, 0)`; `unpackToPlanes(…, frame->data())`. `OutOfMemory` goes through 7b's converter-step retry. |

- Readback and CpuUnpack each have two edges and **one** stats entry.
- Every converter's `run` is `const` (Task 7a's interface); a converter's members are only `shared_ptr`s set at creation.
- **Readback output.** Both Readback outputs (`HostReadback`) offer two native views, so the store files them as one entry under both states (Task 7b's multi-view entry). A `HostUpload` StagingFrame offers only `VkBuffer`. A request for `HostPlanes` in the staging domain can therefore be served only by Readback (D13, §5.3).
- **Host originals are contiguous.** Every original host binding in M2 is tight and contiguous: plane *k* starts at `planes[0].data() + geometry.planes[k].offset`. HostToStaging and CpuUnpack check this and return `Internal` "host original is not contiguous" otherwise. They copy or unpack the single span `planes[0].first(geometry.bytes)`.
- **GPU images.** An image is `R8_UINT` when the component has ≤ 8 bits and `R16_UINT` otherwise, sized `componentSize(format, c, w, h)`. Its usage is `STORAGE | SAMPLED | TRANSFER_SRC` (D5: later read as storage or sampled). Its debug name is `"planes <origin>:<index> <c>"`. After the unpack each image is in `GENERAL` (the Task 5 overload does the transition); Readback copies from `GENERAL` without a transition.
- **Domains.** Staging is `{HostVisible, context->deviceId(), context->generation()}`; device is `{DeviceLocal, same}`. These come from `stagingDomain()`/`deviceDomain()` (Task 7a's `create` makes them).
- **Error codes:**
  - errors of `createBuffer`/`createImage`/`submit` are returned unchanged (`OutOfMemory` reaches Task 7b's evict-and-retry rule);
  - `recordUnpack`'s checks fail inside the record function (nothing recorded); GpuUnpack returns that `Internal` after the submission completes;
  - `FrameStore::create` with a context returns the unpack-pipeline setup error (`Unsupported` when R8_UINT/R16_UINT cannot be written without a format, R1).
- **`acquireOriginal`:**
  1. Look up the origin's `FrameDesc` (the attached source's `info().desc`); `Internal` "origin N is not attached" when detached.
  2. `want = requireExact ? originalView(desc, api, domain) : planesView(desc, api, domain)` (colour forced to `desc.colour`).
  3. `co_await acquire(key, want, resume_on, where)` (that already resumes on `resume_on`) and wrap the lease in `OriginalRead`.

  It plans nothing itself.
- **Lifetimes.** The record function captures `shared_ptr`s of everything the GPU touches: the input buffer (through the `VkBufferB` copy), the output images, the pipeline and the output buffer. That is the M1a `ResourcePool` rule (D10). The store holds the input lease until the step finishes (§5.2).

**Code** (GpuUnpack's run; adapt the frame of it to Task 7a's `run` signature, keep the capture list):

```cpp
// run(ConvertInput input_, ConvertContext context) const; desc = input_.desc;
// unpacked = unpackedFormat(desc.format)
const auto& input = std::get<VkBufferB>(input_.binding);
const PixelFormatDescriptor& d = descriptor(desc.format);
std::array<std::shared_ptr<gpu::GpuImage>, MAX_COMPONENTS> planes{};
for (std::size_t c = 0; c < d.componentCount; ++c) {
    const auto [w, h] = componentSize(desc.format, c, desc.width, desc.height);
    planes[c] = co_try(context_->createImage({
        .width = w, .height = h,
        .format = d.components[c].bits <= 8 ? VK_FORMAT_R8_UINT : VK_FORMAT_R16_UINT,
        .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT | VK_IMAGE_USAGE_SAMPLED_BIT |
                 VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT,
        .name = std::format("planes {}:{} {}", input_.key.origin, input_.key.index, c)}));
}
const auto count = d.componentCount;
auto checked = std::make_shared<Result<void>>();
co_try(co_await context_->submit(
    *cpu_, [pipeline = pipeline_, input, planes, desc, count, checked](gpu::CommandRecorder& recorder) {
        std::array<gpu::GpuImage*, MAX_COMPONENTS> targets{};
        for (std::size_t c = 0; c < count; ++c) { targets[c] = planes[c].get(); }
        *checked = recordUnpack(recorder, *pipeline, *input.buffer, input.offset, desc,
                                std::span<gpu::GpuImage* const>(targets).first(count));
    }));
co_try(std::move(*checked));
co_return detail::StoreAccess::makeFrame<VkPlanesFrame>(context.store, input_.desc, unpacked, context.output.domain, planes, count);
```

**Tests first:**

`dvv-media/tests/packed_source.h` holds the test source:
- `PackedSource final : public ISource`, a test double that is not a component;
- its constructor takes `(FrameDesc desc, std::int64_t frames, std::uint64_t seed)`;
- `info()` gives that desc, fps 25/1, `frameCount = frames`, `RandomCheap` access and `provider = "test"`;
- `produce(i)`:
  1. `Internal` for an index outside `[0, frames)`;
  2. `bytes = pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, desc.format, w, h, seed, i).value())`;
  3. `ctx.allocateHost(desc, bytes.size(), 64, 0)`;
  4. copy into `data()`;
  5. `co_return ctx.publish(frame)`.

Also add a helper `std::uint64_t runsOf(const StoreStats&, std::string_view name)` that returns 0 for an absent name.

Unit file `tests/test_original_read.cpp`. Each case uses a host-only store (`FrameStore::create({.cpu = pool, .io = pool})`) and a `PackedSource` attached with `attach`. Unless a case says otherwise, the source is v210 34×17 (3 frames, seed 7) and yuv420p10le 33×17 runs as a SUBCASE.
- `original read: view helpers`. `originalView(desc, Api::HostPlanes, host) == ViewSpec{desc.format, desc.colour, LayoutPolicy::Any, Api::HostPlanes, host, 0}`. `planesView(...)` is the same with `unpackedFormat(desc.format)`.
- `original read: a host-only store unpacks on the CPU`. `acquireOriginal({origin, 1}, Api::HostPlanes, store->hostDomain(), *pool)`:
  - succeeds with `fidelity() == Fidelity::Lossless`;
  - `view().format == unpackedFormat(desc.format)`;
  - the planes concatenated equal `unpackToPlanes(f, w, h, pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, f, w, h, 7, 1).value()))`;
  - `runsOf(stats, "Produce") == 1` and `runsOf(stats, "CpuUnpack") == 1`;
  - the sorted converter names are `{"CpuUnpack", "Produce"}`.
- `original read: requireExact returns the original bytes`. Same call with `requireExact = true`: `Fidelity::Exact`, the bytes equal `pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, f, w, h, 7, 1).value())`, and `runsOf(stats, "CpuUnpack") == 0`.
- `original read: a second read is a hit`. Two identical reads: `stats().hits` grows by 1 and `CpuUnpack` runs stay 1.
- `original read: a host-only store has no staging domain`:
  - `stagingDomain() == std::nullopt` and `deviceDomain() == std::nullopt`;
  - `acquireOriginal(key, HostPlanes, Domain{MemKind::HostVisible, gpu::DeviceId{0}, 1}, …)` is `Unsupported`.
- `original read: a detached origin is Internal`. Run `detach(origin)`, then `acquireOriginal` returns `Internal`.

GPU file `tests/gpu_test_store.cpp` (fixture of Task 6). The store is `FrameStore::create({.cpu, .io, .gpu = context().shared_from_this()})`; destroy it before the fixture's context. The six formats are `yuv420p10le, v210, nv12, p010le, rgb48be, yuyv422`, each at 34×17 with seed 3, as SUBCASEs.
- `store gpu: planes via the GPU equal the CPU reference`. `acquireOriginal({origin, 2}, Api::HostPlanes, *store->stagingDomain(), executor())`:
  - `Fidelity::Lossless`;
  - the bytes equal `unpackToPlanes(...)` of the same frame;
  - `HostToStaging`, `Upload`, `GpuUnpack` and `Readback` runs are each 1, and `CpuUnpack == 0`.
- `store gpu: requireExact via staging returns the original bytes`. `Fidelity::Exact`, the bytes equal `pack(...)`, `Upload == 1`, `Readback == 1`, `GpuUnpack == 0`.
- `store gpu: HostReadback serves both views, HostUpload only the buffer view` (v210):
  1. After the exact read above, keep its lease and call `trim(*stagingDomain(), 0)`. This evicts the unleased HostUpload entry; the leased Readback entry stays. Then `acquire(originalView(desc, Api::VkBuffer, staging))` is a hit: `hits` +1, and the `Readback` and `HostToStaging` runs are unchanged.
  2. On a new key, `acquire(planesView(desc, Api::VkImage, device))` creates a HostUpload staging.
  3. Then `acquire(originalView(desc, Api::HostPlanes, staging))`: `Readback` grows by 1, while `Upload` and `HostToStaging` do not change (the device original is reused).
- `store gpu: the planner picks the CPU for host targets and the GPU for device targets` (fresh store, p010le):
  1. `acquireOriginal(…, HostPlanes, host)` gives `CpuUnpack == 1`, `GpuUnpack == 0` and `Upload == 0`.
  2. Then `acquire(planesView(desc, Api::VkImage, device))` gives `GpuUnpack == 1`.
  3. Its `VkImageB` has `planeCount == componentCount`, and each image has:
     - `format() == R16_UINT`;
     - extent equal to `componentSize`;
     - `layout() == VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL`;
     - `view() != VK_NULL_HANDLE`.
- `store gpu: device-tier eviction frees memory` (yuv420p10le 64×64):
  1. Set `deviceBudget = 3 × (detail::unpackBufferBytes(0, frame bytes) + plane bytes)`.
  2. Acquire the VkImage planes of frames 0..9 one at a time, dropping each lease.
  3. The device tier bytes stay `≤ deviceBudget` and `evictions > 0`.
  4. `trim(*deviceDomain(), 0)` brings the device tier bytes to 0.
  5. After `context().drain()`, `retireStats().freed` is greater than its value before step 2 and `retiring == 0`.
- `store gpu: an adopted unpadded buffer fails the unpack` (yuv420p10le 33×17):
  1. Make a `DeviceOnly` buffer of exactly `geometry.bytes` with `TRANSFER_DST`.
  2. Wrap it in a test `UnpaddedBufferFrame : IFrame`, a test double that is not managed, with the one view `{format, colour, Tight, VkBuffer, device}`.
  3. `adopt({origin, 0}, frame)` succeeds.
  4. `acquire(planesView(desc, VkImage, device))` fails with `Internal`.

The unit file fails first: it does not compile because `acquireOriginal`, `originalView` and `planesView` are missing. The GPU file then fails at run time: requests in the staging domain return `Unsupported` ("no converter path") until `makeGpuConverters` exists.

**Notes:**

- Note (plan choice): converter costs are factors of the input view's bytes (1, 2, 0.5, 2, 4). In M2 the graph leaves one path per target state, so the factors only order alternatives that cannot occur yet.
- Note (plan choice): unpacked images also get `SAMPLED` usage, as D5 says analysers read them as storage or sampled images. R8_UINT/R16_UINT support SAMPLED on every Vulkan device.
- Note (plan choice): converters live in one `src/converters.cpp`.
- Keep the GPU case sizes small (they must stay fast on a software device such as lavapipe once Linux returns); only the eviction case uses 64×64.

### Task 9: `SourceFactory` and the kind check

**Goal:**
- dvv-registry checks that a component's `info().kind` matches the kind of the interface it is registered for (`dvv::InterfaceKind<I>`).
- dvv-media gets `ISourceProvider`, `OpenTicket` and `SourceFactory` with the decision rules of §5.5.
- The (still empty) component library `dvv_media_sources` and a provider conformance case are added.

**Files:**
- dvv-registry (branch `m2`):
  - Modify `include/dvv/registry/component_info.h`: the `InterfaceKind` trait and the `HasInterfaceKind` concept.
  - Modify `include/dvv/registry/component_registry.h`: `RegistrationNode` stores the interface kind.
  - Modify `src/component_registry.cpp`: add the check in `create(caps)`.
  - Create `tests/test_interface_kind.cpp`; modify `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (add the file) and `tests/test_component_registry.cpp` (one count).
- dvv-media:
  - Create `include/dvv/media/source_factory.h` (§4.5 provider part) and `src/source_factory.cpp`.
  - Create `src/sources/uri_params.h` and `src/sources/uri_params.cpp`: shared URI-parameter parsing for the providers; also the first source file of `dvv_media_sources`.
  - Modify `CMakeLists.txt`: the `dvv_media_sources` target, its install/export, and `src/source_factory.cpp`.
  - Create the tests: `tests/fake_providers.h`, `tests/fake_providers.cpp`, `tests/test_source_factory.cpp`, `tests/test_provider_conformance.cpp`, `tests/test_uri_params.cpp`.
  - Modify `tests/CMakeLists.txt`: add the new executable `dvv_media_factory_tests`, link `dvv_media_sources` into `dvv_media_tests`, and add compile-fail cases.
  - Modify `tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp`: two cases.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - M1b: `ComponentRegistry::createComponent<I>`.
  - Task 4: `platform::FileReader` (buffered head read).
  - Task 1: `pathFromUtf8`.
  - Task 7a: `ISource`, `SourceInfo`, `source.h`.
  - Task 2: `findFormat`.
  - Task 1: `internColour`, `ColourParams`.
- Produces (dvv-registry, `component_info.h`; code):

```cpp
// The component kind every implementation of interface I must report (spec §9.3). Specialise it
// in the interface's header:
//     template <> struct dvv::InterfaceKind<dvv::media::ISourceProvider>
//         : std::integral_constant<dvv::Kind, dvv::Kind::Source> {};
// Without a specialisation the registry does not check the kind.
template <class I>
struct InterfaceKind {};

template <class I>
concept HasInterfaceKind = requires {
    { InterfaceKind<I>::value } -> std::convertible_to<Kind>;
};
```

- `RegistrationNode` gains a member `std::optional<Kind> interface_kind_` and a constructor parameter in the same position. `ComponentRegistration<I>` passes:

```cpp
[] {
    if constexpr (HasInterfaceKind<I>) {
        return std::optional<Kind>(InterfaceKind<I>::value);
    } else {
        return std::optional<Kind>();
    }
}()
```

- `ComponentRegistry::create(caps)`: when `schema()` succeeded and `node->interface_kind_` is set and differs from `status.info.kind`:
  - the component is unavailable with the reason `std::format("kind {} does not match interface kind {}", kindName(info.kind), kindName(*interface_kind_))`;
  - `probe` is not called (`schema` stays set).

  The existing log line reports it.
- Produces (dvv-media, `source_factory.h`): exactly design §4.5's `FormatHint`, `SourceRequest`, `FormatGuess`, `Match`, `Candidate`, `Ambiguous`, `OpenTicket`, `OpenContext`, `ISourceProvider` and `SourceFactory`, plus:

```cpp
namespace dvv::media {
struct ProviderEntry {                      // one provider instance owned by the factory
    std::string_view id;                    // ComponentInfo::id (static storage)
    std::unique_ptr<ISourceProvider> provider;
};
class OpenTicket {                          // move-only; only SourceFactory makes one
public:
    OpenTicket(OpenTicket&& other) noexcept;             // leaves `other` empty
    OpenTicket& operator=(OpenTicket&& other) noexcept;
    OpenTicket(const OpenTicket&) = delete;
    OpenTicket& operator=(const OpenTicket&) = delete;
    ~OpenTicket() = default;
private:
    friend class SourceFactory;
    explicit OpenTicket(ObjectId owner, std::source_location where) noexcept;
    ObjectId owner_ = NO_OWNER;
    std::source_location where_;
    bool valid_ = false;
};
// In SourceFactory (header, template):
template <class T>
CreationKey<SourceFactory> SourceFactory::issueKey(OpenTicket&& ticket) {
    if (!ticket.valid_) {
        throw std::logic_error("SourceFactory::issueKey: the ticket was already used");
    }
    ticket.valid_ = false;
    return CreationKey<SourceFactory>(
        LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<T>(ticket.owner_, ticket.where_));
}
}
template <>
struct dvv::InterfaceKind<dvv::media::ISourceProvider>
    : std::integral_constant<dvv::Kind, dvv::Kind::Source> {};
```

- Produces (private, `src/sources/uri_params.h`, namespace `dvv::media::detail`; for Tasks 10-12):

```cpp
// "WxH", both 1..65535 decimal. Errc::Format "bad size 'x' (expected WxH)".
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::pair<std::uint32_t, std::uint32_t>> parseSize(std::string_view text);
// One dimension 1..65535 for w=/h=. Errc::Format naming the parameter.
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::uint32_t> parseDimension(std::string_view name, std::string_view text);
// "N", "N/D" (integers > 0) or "I.F" (decimal; 29.97 -> 2997/100), reduced by the gcd.
// Errc::Format "bad fps 'x'".
[[nodiscard]] Result<Rational> parseFps(std::string_view text);
// "N" when den == 1, else "N/D" (the resolved-URI form).
[[nodiscard]] std::string formatFps(Rational fps);
// Unsigned decimal integer. Errc::Format naming the parameter.
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::uint64_t> parseUnsigned(std::string_view name, std::string_view text);
// range=full|limited, matrix=bt601|bt709|bt2020|rgb (bt2020 -> Bt2020Ncl); absent -> Unspecified.
// Errc::Format "bad range 'x' (full, limited)" / "bad matrix 'x' (bt601, bt709, bt2020, rgb)".
[[nodiscard]] Result<ColourParams> colourFromQuery(const Uri& uri);
// Errc::Format "unknown parameter 'x'" for a query name outside `allowed` ("impl" is always allowed).
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> checkParameters(const Uri& uri, std::span<const std::string_view> allowed);
// Size from size=WxH, or w= and h= (both required together); nullopt when none is given.
// Errc::Format when both forms are given or one of w/h is missing.
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::optional<std::pair<std::uint32_t, std::uint32_t>>> sizeFromQuery(const Uri&);
```

- CMake target `dvv_media_sources`:

```cmake
# Built-in source providers (D1): a component library. Consumers link it only with
# dvv_link_components(<target> dvv::media_sources), so the DVV_REGISTER lines survive linking.
add_library(dvv_media_sources STATIC src/sources/uri_params.cpp)
add_library(dvv::media_sources ALIAS dvv_media_sources)
set_target_properties(dvv_media_sources PROPERTIES EXPORT_NAME media_sources)
target_link_libraries(dvv_media_sources PUBLIC dvv::media)
target_include_directories(dvv_media_sources PRIVATE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src)
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_media_sources)
install(TARGETS dvv_media_sources EXPORT dvv_mediaTargets)
```

**Behaviour:**

- `SourceFactory::create(registry, io, where)`:
  - `Internal` for a null `io`.
  - For every component of `registry.list(Kind::Source)` that is `available()`, call `registry.createComponent<ISourceProvider>(status.info.id)`. A failure is logged at warning level and that component is skipped.
  - No provider left → `Unsupported` "no source providers registered" (R12).
  - The factory is created with `CreationKey<SourceFactory>(LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<SourceFactory>(NO_OWNER, where))`.
  - The providers are stateless and owned by the factory. A source never keeps a pointer to its provider, so sources may outlive the factory.
- `open(request)` (§5.5) is thread-safe; the disabled flags are guarded by a mutex or atomics. The steps:
  1. **Providers by scheme.** The providers whose `schemes()` contain `request.uri.scheme` (a bare path is already `file`) and that are not disabled. None → `Unsupported` "no source provider for scheme '<s>'".
  2. **Head.** For `file`:
     1. `co_await resumeOn(*io_)`.
     2. `platform::FileReader::open(pathFromUtf8(uri.path), FileReadMode::Buffered)`; an error (`Io`) is returned at once.
     3. Read `min(size, 4096)` bytes at offset 0 into a `std::vector<std::byte>` (resized to the bytes read).

     Other schemes get an empty head.
  3. **`impl`.** With `uri.param("impl")`, only the provider with that id is considered. An unknown id → `Unsupported` "unknown source provider '<id>' (available: <ids sorted, comma-separated>)". Its `match` is called; confidence 0 → `Unsupported` "<id> cannot open '<uri>': <why>". Any confidence > 0 opens it: the user chose, so the 60 threshold and ties do not apply.
  4. **Decide.** Call `match(request, head)` on each provider. Drop the matches with confidence 0, then sort by confidence descending, ties by provider id.
     - Nothing left → `Unsupported` "no source provider can open '<uri>': <id>: <why>; <id>: <why>" (every provider of the scheme and its `why`).
     - Best < 60, or a second whose confidence is greater than `best − 10` → `Ambiguous{candidates}`, best first, with only the candidates whose confidence is > 0. A difference of exactly 10 is not a tie.
     - Otherwise open the best.
  5. **Open.** `co_await provider.open(OpenTicket(objectId(), std::source_location::current()), request, guess, OpenContext{*io_})`. The factory awaits it directly, so `request`, `guess` and the context outlive the provider's task. A null source → `Internal` "source provider '<id>' returned no source". A provider error is returned unchanged.
- **Exception boundary** (§7, §11). An exception from a provider's `match` or `open` (`co_await` of the provider task inside `try`) is caught, logged at error level, and returned as `Internal` "source provider '<id>' failed: <what>". The provider is disabled for the factory's lifetime: later `open` calls skip it.
- `OpenTicket` is move-only. Only `SourceFactory` constructs one; a moved-from ticket is empty, and `issueKey` with an empty ticket throws `std::logic_error`, which the exception boundary turns into `Internal`.
- Concrete sources (Tasks 10-12) are `final`, `Managed<T>`, constructed with `SourceFactory::issueKey<T>(std::move(ticket))`; their owner is the factory's object id.

**Tests first:**

Write the dvv-registry tests first.

`tests/test_interface_kind.cpp` (linked into `dvv_registry_tests`) defines:
- an interface `IKindedWidget` (virtual destructor; one method `int value() const`) with `template <> struct dvv::InterfaceKind<IKindedWidget> : std::integral_constant<dvv::Kind, dvv::Kind::Analyser> {};`;
- the components `test.kinded_good` (kind `Analyser`) and `test.kinded_bad` (kind `Exporter`), both registered with `DVV_REGISTER(IKindedWidget, …)`, with empty schemas and `probe` returning `{}`. `test.kinded_bad`'s probe counts its calls.

The cases:
- `kind check: a matching kind is available` — `test.kinded_good` is `available()` and `createComponent<IKindedWidget>("test.kinded_good")` succeeds.
- `kind check: a different kind is unavailable with the reason`:
  - `test.kinded_bad`'s reason is exactly `"kind Exporter does not match interface kind Analyser"`;
  - its probe was not called;
  - `createComponent<IKindedWidget>("test.kinded_bad")` is `Unsupported` containing "is unavailable";
  - `status.schema != nullptr`.
- `kind check: interfaces without InterfaceKind are unchanged` — `static_assert(!dvv::HasInterfaceKind<IGreeter-like test interface>)`. The existing cases still pass.

In `tests/test_component_registry.cpp`, change `CHECK(registry->list().size() == tools.size() + metrics.size());` to add `+ 2` (the comment names the two kind-check components). That test fails first only because of this count.

dvv-media `tests/test_uri_params.cpp` (unit) checks each helper:
- `parseSize("34x17")`.
- `parseSize` errors: `"0x5"`, `"34x"` and `"34*17"` → `Format`.
- `parseFps`:
  - `"25"` → 25/1;
  - `"30000/1001"`;
  - `"29.97"` → 2997/100;
  - `"50/2"` → 25/1;
  - `"0"`, `"1/0"` and `"x"` → `Format`.
- `formatFps(Rational{25,1}) == "25"` and `formatFps(Rational{30000,1001}) == "30000/1001"`.
- `colourFromQuery`: `parseUri("synthetic:x?range=full&matrix=bt2020")` → `{Bt2020Ncl, Full, Unspecified, Unspecified}`, and `range=wide` → `Format`.
- `checkParameters` with `{"fmt"}` on `?fmt=a&impl=raw` → ok, and on `?foo=1` → `Format` "unknown parameter 'foo'".
- `sizeFromQuery`:
  - `?w=3&h=4` → {3,4};
  - `?size=3x4` → {3,4};
  - `?w=3` → `Format`;
  - `?size=3x4&w=3&h=4` → `Format`;
  - no size → nullopt.

dvv-media factory tests: the separate executable `dvv_media_factory_tests` (CTest `dvv_media.factory`) links `dvv::media` but **not** `dvv_media_sources`, so its fake providers never compete with the real ones of Tasks 10-12.

`tests/fake_providers.h/.cpp` define three providers `fake_a`, `fake_b` and `fake_c`:
- each is a `final` `Managed<T>` registered with `DVV_REGISTER(dvv::media::ISourceProvider, …)`;
- kind `Source`, schemes `{"fake", "file"}`, an empty schema;
- `probe` returns `require(caps, {"fake"})`.

A global `FakeScript` per id controls each provider:
- `confidence`, `why`;
- `throwInMatch`, `throwInOpen`;
- counters `matchCalls`, `openCalls`;
- `lastHead` (copied bytes).

`open` returns `std::make_unique<FakeSource>(SourceFactory::issueKey<FakeSource>(std::move(ticket)), info)`, where `FakeSource final : ISource, Managed<FakeSource>` has `info().provider` = the id and a `produce` that returns `Internal`.

Each case resets the scripts. The registry is `ComponentRegistry::create(Capabilities{"fake"})`, and the factory gets an io pool of 2 threads.

`tests/test_source_factory.cpp` cases. The URI is `fake:x` unless a case says otherwise; each case drives `syncWait(factory->open({*parseUri(uri)}))`.
- `factory: a clear best at or above 60 opens it` — a 95, b 30: the result holds a source with `info().provider == "fake_a"`, `a.openCalls == 1` and `b.openCalls == 0`.
- `factory: a near tie is Ambiguous` — a 70, b 65: `Ambiguous` with candidates `[fake_a 70, fake_b 65]` (in that order, `why` copied) and no open call.
- `factory: a difference of exactly 10 is not a tie` — a 70, b 60: opens a.
- `factory: a best below 60 is Ambiguous` — a 30, b 0, c 0: `Ambiguous` with exactly one candidate (`fake_a`).
- `factory: no confidence is Unsupported naming every provider` — all 0, with whys "why a", "why b" and "why c": `Unsupported`, and the message contains "fake_a: why a", "fake_b: why b" and "fake_c: why c".
- `factory: impl selects one provider` — `fake:x?impl=fake_b` with a 95, b 30: opens b, and `a.matchCalls == 0`.
- `factory: an unknown impl is Unsupported listing the ids` — `?impl=nope`: `Unsupported`, the message contains "fake_a, fake_b, fake_c".
- `factory: an unknown scheme is Unsupported` — `nosuch:x` → `Unsupported`.
- `factory: a missing file is Io before any match` — a bare path to a missing file in a fresh temporary directory: `Io`, and every `matchCalls == 0`.
- `factory: the file head is passed to match`:
  - for a 5000-byte file whose byte *i* is `i % 251`, `a.lastHead.size() == 4096` and the bytes equal;
  - for a 10-byte file, the head is the whole file.
- `factory: an exception in match is Internal and disables the provider`:
  1. With a throwing in `match`, the first open is `Internal` with a message containing "fake_a".
  2. A second open (b 95) succeeds with b, and `a.matchCalls` is still 1.
- `factory: an exception in open is Internal and disables the provider` — the same with `throwInOpen`.
- `factory: create without available providers is Unsupported` — a registry made with `Capabilities{}` (the probes fail): `SourceFactory::create` is `Unsupported` "no source providers registered".
- `factory: create with a null io executor is Internal`.
- `factory: the ticket keys the source to the factory` — `dynamic_cast<const ManagedObject&>(*source).ownerId() == factory->objectId()`.
- `factory: InterfaceKind of ISourceProvider is Source` — `static_assert(dvv::InterfaceKind<ISourceProvider>::value == dvv::Kind::Source)`.

`tests/test_provider_conformance.cpp` (in `dvv_media_tests`, which now links `dvv_link_components(dvv_media_tests dvv::media_sources)`) has the case `providers: conformance`:

```cpp
// Tasks 10-12 extend this list; it catches a provider dropped at link time (R12).
constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 0> EXPECTED_PROVIDERS{};
```

The case:
1. `registry = ComponentRegistry::create(Capabilities{})`; the ids of `registry->list(Kind::Source)` equal `EXPECTED_PROVIDERS` (sorted).
2. For each status:
   - `status.available()` (so neither `schema()` nor `probe()` threw);
   - `status.schema != nullptr` and `status.schema->resolve({})` has a value.
3. Take `liveCount()` before `createComponent<ISourceProvider>(id)`. The provider is a `ManagedObject` whose `ownerId() == registry->objectId()`. After the provider is destroyed, `liveCount()` equals the value before.

**Notes:**
- Design references: D14, §4.5, §5.5, §7, R12, arbiter FIX 11.
- Note (plan choice): the fake providers get their own test executable (`dvv_media_factory_tests`). If they were linked with `dvv_media_sources`, the real `file` providers of Tasks 11-12 would take part in the decision tests.
- Note (plan choice): "no other within 10" means a difference smaller than 10 is a near tie.
- Note (plan choice): with `?impl=`, any confidence above 0 opens the provider; the user's choice overrides the threshold.
- Note (plan choice): the kind check runs after `schema()` and before `probe()`, so a mismatched component keeps its schema and is never probed.
- Note (plan choice): `src/sources/uri_params.{h,cpp}` is shared by the three providers. It is the first file of `dvv_media_sources`, because a static library needs a source.

### Task 10: Synthetic provider

**Goal:** The `synthetic` source provider: deterministic gradient and noise frames of any catalogue format, opened through `SourceFactory` and produced through the store.

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/src/sources/synthetic.cpp`: `SyntheticProvider` (component) and `SyntheticSource`.
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`: add the file to `dvv_media_sources`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/source_harness.h` and `dvv-media/tests/source_harness.cpp`: the shared test harness for Tasks 10-12.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_synthetic_source.cpp`.
- Modify `dvv-media/tests/test_provider_conformance.cpp` (expected list).
- Modify the package test consumer `dvv-media/tests/package/` (`CMakeLists.txt`, `main.cpp`).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 9: `ISourceProvider`, `SourceFactory::issueKey`, `uri_params.h`.
  - Task 3: the generator `detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Gradient|Noise, format, w, h, seed, index)` of `src/patterns.h` (returns `Result<CodeValues>`), and `pack`.
  - Task 2: `findFormat`, `geometry`.
  - Task 7a: `ProduceContext`.
- Produces (internal to `dvv_media_sources`):

```cpp
class SyntheticProvider final : public ISourceProvider, public Managed<SyntheticProvider> {
public:
    SyntheticProvider(CreationKey<ComponentRegistry> key, const ParameterValues& values) noexcept;
    static ComponentInfo info() noexcept {
        return {.id = "synthetic", .name = "Synthetic test patterns", .kind = Kind::Source,
                .version = 1};
    }
    static const ParameterSchema& schema();                                  // empty
    static std::expected<void, Unavailable> probe(const Capabilities&) { return {}; }
    std::span<const std::string_view> schemes() const noexcept override;     // {"synthetic"}
    Match match(const SourceRequest&, std::span<const std::byte>) const override;
    Task<Result<std::unique_ptr<ISource>>> open(OpenTicket, const SourceRequest&,
                                                const FormatGuess&, const OpenContext&) override;
};
DVV_REGISTER(ISourceProvider, SyntheticProvider);
// SyntheticSource final : ISource, Managed<SyntheticSource>;
// constructor (CreationKey<SourceFactory>, SourceInfo, Pattern, seed)
```

- Produces (tests; used by Tasks 11 and 12), `tests/source_harness.h`:

```cpp
// A registry with every linked component, cpu and io pools (2 threads each), a SourceFactory and
// a host-only FrameStore. Destroys the store, the factory, the registry and then the pools.
class SourceHarness {
public:
    SourceHarness();
    // Opens a URI; fails the test (REQUIRE) on an error or Ambiguous.
    [[nodiscard]] std::shared_ptr<dvv::media::ISource> open(std::string_view uri);
    // The raw result, for error and ambiguity tests.
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::variant<std::unique_ptr<dvv::media::ISource>, dvv::media::Ambiguous>>
        tryOpen(std::string_view uri);
    // attach + acquireOriginal(requireExact, HostPlanes, host) + copy of the contiguous bytes.
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::vector<std::byte>> originalBytes(
        const std::shared_ptr<dvv::media::ISource>& source, std::int64_t index);
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::media::FrameStore& store();
};
```

**Behaviour:**
- URI: `synthetic:<pattern>?fmt=&size=WxH | w=&h=&fps=&frames=&seed=&range=&matrix=`. The pattern is `gradient` or `noise`; anything else is `Format` "unknown synthetic pattern 'x' (gradient, noise)".
- `fmt` (a catalogue name, `findFormat`) and the size (`sizeFromQuery`) are required. A missing one → `Format` "synthetic source needs fmt" / "synthetic source needs size=WxH or w= and h=". An unknown `fmt` → `Format` "unknown pixel format 'x'".
- Defaults: fps 25/1, frames 10, seed 1. `frames` must be ≥ 1 (else `Format`); `seed` is any unsigned 64-bit value.
- Allowed parameters: `fmt size w h fps frames seed range matrix` (plus `impl`). Any other → `Format` (`checkParameters`).
- `geometry(fmt, w, h)` errors pass through as `Format`, e.g. v210 at an odd width.
- `match`: scheme `synthetic` → confidence 100, why "synthetic source"; the guess holds the format and size when they parse, else stays empty. It does no validation; `open` reports parameter errors.
- `SourceInfo`:
  - `desc = {fmt, internColour(colourFromQuery(uri)), w, h}`;
  - `fps`;
  - `frameCount = frames`;
  - `access = RandomCheap`, `deterministic = true`;
  - `provider = "synthetic"`, `warnings = {}`;
  - `resolvedUri = "synthetic:<pattern>?fmt=<name>&size=<W>x<H>&fps=<formatFps>&frames=<n>&seed=<s>"`, followed by `&range=<v>` and `&matrix=<v>` only when they were given. Build it with `formatUri` (the parameters in this order).
- `produce(index, ctx)`:
  1. Index outside `[0, frames)` → `Internal` "frame N is outside [0, F)".
  2. Compute the code values with `detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Gradient, fmt, w, h, 0, index)` or `detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, fmt, w, h, seed, index)`, propagating the `Result` (`DVV_TRY`; inside the `produce` coroutine its form `co_try`).
  3. `bytes = pack(codes)` (error → returned).
  4. `ctx.allocateHost(desc, bytes.size(), 64, 0)` and copy the bytes into `data()`.
  5. `co_return ctx.publish(frame)`.

  It runs on the CPU executor (the store hops there before Produce) and does no I/O.

**Tests first** (`tests/test_synthetic_source.cpp`, using `SourceHarness`):
- `synthetic: frames equal the pattern generators` — `synthetic:noise?fmt=yuv420p10le&size=33x17&seed=7`: frame 3's bytes equal `pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, yuv420p10le, 33, 17, 7, 3).value())`. `synthetic:gradient?fmt=v210&size=34x17`: frame 0 equals `pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Gradient, v210, 34, 17, 0, 0).value())`.
- `synthetic: deterministic` — two opens of the same noise URI give equal bytes for frame 2. Frames 2 and 3 differ.
- `synthetic: every catalogue format produces in-range code values` — for every `FormatId` in `catalogue()`, open `synthetic:gradient?fmt=<name>&size=6x2`. Frame 0 succeeds, and `unpack(f, 6, 2, bytes)` gives values `< (1 << bits)` for each component.
- `synthetic: resolved URI` — `synthetic:noise?w=34&h=17&fmt=v210&seed=9&range=full` has `info().resolvedUri == "synthetic:noise?fmt=v210&size=34x17&fps=25&frames=10&seed=9&range=full"`. Reopening it gives an equal `desc`, `fps` and `frameCount`, and equal bytes for frame 1.
- `synthetic: colour from range and matrix` — `…&range=limited&matrix=bt709` gives `colourParams(info().desc.colour) == ColourParams{Bt709, Limited, Unspecified, Unspecified}`.
- `synthetic: parameter errors are Format`:
  - missing `fmt`;
  - `fmt=nope`;
  - `size=0x5`;
  - `frames=0`;
  - `fps=0`;
  - pattern `bars`;
  - `foo=1`;
  - `fmt=v210&size=33x17`, whose message contains "multiple of 2".
- `synthetic: an index outside the source is Internal` — with `frames=2`, `originalBytes(source, 2)` → `Internal`.
- Conformance: change `EXPECTED_PROVIDERS` to `{"synthetic"}`. The conformance case fails first (0 providers listed) until the provider is registered.

Package consumer: `tests/package/CMakeLists.txt` links `dvv_link_components(<consumer> dvv::media_sources)`. `main.cpp` creates a `ComponentRegistry` and checks that `list(Kind::Source)` contains `"synthetic"` (exit 1 otherwise). This proves the installed and the in-tree export of the component library.

Expected: the conformance case and the synthetic cases fail ("no source provider for scheme 'synthetic'").
- [ ] **Step 2: implement `synthetic.cpp`.** Rerun Step 1. Expected: `dvv_media.unit` passes.

### Task 11: Raw file provider

**Goal:** The `raw` source provider. It opens headerless files, taking the format and size from the query, a hint or the file name (`<name>_WxH[_fmt][_fps]`); it also accepts a custom format through `fmt=@<file.json>`. It reads frames with aligned, unbuffered reads on the I/O pool.

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/src/sources/filename.h` and `dvv-media/src/sources/filename.cpp`: `parseFilename`.
- Create `dvv-media/src/sources/file_read.h` and `dvv-media/src/sources/file_read.cpp`: aligned frame reads, shared with Y4M (Task 12).
- Create `dvv-media/src/sources/raw_file.cpp`: `RawFileProvider` and `RawFileSource`.
- Modify `dvv-media/include/dvv/media/source.h`: `SourceInfo::reader`.
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`: add the three `.cpp` files to `dvv_media_sources`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_raw_source.cpp` and `dvv-media/tests/test_filename.cpp`.
- Modify `dvv-media/tests/test_provider_conformance.cpp`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/temporary_directory.h` only if Tasks 1-7b did not add an equivalent helper. Its contract is that of dvv-gpu's `TemporaryDirectory`: a new directory under `std::filesystem::temp_directory_path()`, removed with its content.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 4: `platform::FileReader` (`open`, `size`, `unbuffered`, `alignment`, `readAt`) and `allocateAligned`.
  - Task 3b: `formatFromJson`, `formatToJson`.
  - Task 1: `pathFromUtf8`.
  - Task 9: `uri_params.h`.
  - Task 10: `SourceHarness`.
  - Task 3: `pack`, `noise`.
- Produces (`source.h`, one field appended to `SourceInfo`):

```cpp
std::string reader;  // file sources: "unbuffered" or "buffered" (FileReader::unbuffered()); empty otherwise (D18 bench)
```

- Produces (private, namespace `dvv::media::detail`):

```cpp
// filename.h
struct FilenameInfo { std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0; std::optional<FormatId> format;
                      std::optional<Rational> fps; };
// Parses the stem of a UTF-8 file name (directories and extension are ignored).
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<FilenameInfo> parseFilename(std::string_view utf8_path);

// file_read.h
struct FrameRead {
    std::uint64_t alignedOffset;  // where the read starts
    std::size_t rangeBytes;       // bytes to read (a multiple of the alignment)
    std::size_t dataOffset;       // where the frame starts inside the range
    std::size_t frameBytes;
};
// Range [alignDown(offset - lead, a), alignUp(offset + frame_bytes, a)); a >= 1.
[[nodiscard]] FrameRead planFrameRead(std::uint64_t offset, std::uint64_t frame_bytes,
                                      std::uint64_t lead, std::uint32_t alignment) noexcept;
// Blocking (call on the I/O pool). Reads plan.rangeBytes at plan.alignedOffset into `memory`.
// Errc::Io "truncated frame <index> of '<file>'" when fewer than dataOffset + frameBytes bytes
// arrive; FileReader errors pass through.
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> readFrame(const platform::FileReader& reader, const FrameRead& plan,
                                     std::span<std::byte> memory, std::int64_t index,
                                     std::string_view file_utf8);
// Reads a small whole file (<= 1 MiB; else Errc::Format "'<file>' is too large") through a
// buffered FileReader; used for fmt=@<json>.
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::string> readSmallFile(std::string_view utf8_path);
```

- Produces: the component `raw` (`RawFileProvider`, name "Raw video file", kind Source, version 1, schemes `{"file"}`, empty schema, `probe` returns `{}`). `RawFileSource` is `final`, `Managed`, with constructor `(CreationKey<SourceFactory>, SourceInfo, platform::FileReader, std::uint64_t frame_bytes)`.

**Behaviour:**
- **`parseFilename`.** Split the stem at `_` and read from the right:
  1. An optional fps token: `\d+(\.\d+)?` with an optional `fps` suffix, parsed with `parseFps`.
  2. An optional format token: a catalogue name (`findFormat`).
  3. A required size token `\d+x\d+`, both values ≥ 1.

  Anything else → `nullopt`. Examples:
  - `clip_34x17_v210_50` → 34×17, v210, 50/1;
  - `clip_1920x1080_29.97fps` → 2997/100;
  - `10x20` → 10×20;
  - `a_10x20_bogus` → `nullopt`;
  - `a_0x20` → `nullopt`;
  - `a_10x20_nv12_v210` → `nullopt` (nv12 is not a size).
- **Fields.** Each field comes from the query, then from `request.hint`, then from the file name:
  - size: `size=WxH` or `w`+`h` (`sizeFromQuery`);
  - format: `fmt=<name>` or `fmt=@<path>`;
  - fps: `fps=`; the default is 25/1.
- **`match` confidence** (§5.5; no I/O; the head is ignored):
  - 95, "explicit format and size": the query or the hint gives both size and format (`fmt=@…` counts as given);
  - 70, "size and format from the file name": size and format are both known from any mix of sources;
  - 30, "size only; format guessed as yuv420p": only the size is known, and the guess format is `yuv420p`;
  - 0, "no frame size: name the file <name>_WxH[_fmt] or pass ?size=WxH&fmt=": nothing is known.

  The guess carries what was found.
- **`open`:**
  1. `co_await resumeOn(ctx.io)`.
  2. `checkParameters(uri, {fmt, size, w, h, fps, range, matrix})`.
  3. `fmt=@<path>`: `readSmallFile` (`Io` when missing), then `json::parse`, then `formatFromJson` (`Format`). The path is relative to the current directory. Otherwise `findFormat` (`Format` "unknown pixel format 'x'"); without any format, the guess's (`yuv420p`).
  4. `geometry(fmt, w, h)` (`Format`).
  5. `FileReader::open(pathFromUtf8(uri.path), FileReadMode::Unbuffered)` (`Io`).
  6. `frameCount = size / frameBytes`. A remainder *r* > 0 adds the warning `"<r> trailing bytes ignored"`. A file smaller than one frame opens with `frameCount == 0`, and an empty file with no warning.
  7. `reader = reader.unbuffered() ? "unbuffered" : "buffered"`.
  8. `resolvedUri = formatUri(Uri{"file", "", <path as given>, {fmt=<name or @path>, size=WxH, fps=<formatFps>, [range], [matrix]}})`.
  9. Set the colour with `colourFromQuery`, `access = RandomCheap` and `provider = "raw"`.
- **`produce(i)`** (§5.6):
  1. Index outside `[0, frameCount)` → `Internal`.
  2. `co_await resumeOn(ctx.io())`.
  3. `a = reader.alignment()`; `plan = planFrameRead(i · frameBytes, frameBytes, 0, a)`.
  4. `frame = ctx.allocateHost(desc, plan.rangeBytes, max(a, 64), plan.dataOffset)`.
  5. `readFrame(reader, plan, frame->memory(), i, uri.path)`.
  6. `co_return ctx.publish(frame)`.

  `readAt` is thread-safe, so concurrent `produce` calls share the reader.
- **Paths and text.** Every UTF-8 → path conversion goes through `pathFromUtf8`. Messages and the resolved URI use the UTF-8 URI path. A `std::filesystem::path` that must become text goes through `u8string()`, never `path::string()` (ANSI code page on Windows; arbiter minor note).

**Tests first.** Files are written into a per-test temporary directory from `pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, f, w, h, seed, i).value())` frames; the default content is 3 frames of yuv420p10le 33×17 with seed 4.

`tests/test_filename.cpp` has `filename: table`, with the rows above as assertions, plus:
- the extension and directories are ignored (`dir/clip_10x20.yuv`);
- a non-ASCII stem `clip_ä日本_33x17_yuv420p` parses.

`tests/test_raw_source.cpp` cases:
- `raw: frame read plan` (`planFrameRead`):
  - (5000, 1000, 0, 4096) → {4096, 4096, 904, 1000};
  - (0, 4096, 0, 4096) → {0, 4096, 0, 4096};
  - (100, 10, 6, 1) → {94, 16, 6, 10};
  - (4100, 10, 6, 4096) → {0, 8192, 4100, 10}.
- `raw: a truncated frame is Io`:
  1. Write a 100-byte file; open it with `FileReader` in both modes (SUBCASEs: `Unbuffered`, `Buffered`).
  2. `readFrame` with `planFrameRead(50, 80, 0, reader.alignment())` into an `allocateAligned` buffer → `Io`, and the message contains "truncated frame 3 of".
- `raw: query size and format` — `<file>?fmt=yuv420p10le&size=33x17`:
  - the decision opened `raw`, with the 95 match;
  - `frameCount == 3`;
  - the desc is right;
  - frame 1's bytes equal `pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, f, w, h, 4, 1).value())`.
- `raw: file-name detection`:
  - `clip_34x17_v210_50.yuv` (v210 content) opens with `info().fps == Rational{50,1}`;
  - `clip_33x17.yuv` → `tryOpen` is `Ambiguous` with one candidate `raw` at confidence 30;
  - `clip_33x17_nv12.yuv?fmt=yuv420p10le` opens as yuv420p10le (the query wins).
- `raw: custom format from JSON`:
  1. Write `formatToJson(*findFormat("yuv420p10le"))` with `name` changed to `"my10le"` to `my10le.json`.
  2. `<file>?fmt=@<json path>&size=33x17`: `descriptor(info().desc.format).name == "my10le"`, the resolved URI contains `fmt=@`, and frame 2's bytes equal the yuv420p10le frame.
  3. A JSON file holding `{` → `Format`; a missing JSON file → `Io`.
- `raw: trailing bytes warning` — 2 frames + 5 bytes: `frameCount == 2` and `warnings == {"5 trailing bytes ignored"}`.
- `raw: one-frame and zero-frame files`:
  - one frame: `frameCount == 1`, frame 0 is ok and frame 1 is `Internal`;
  - a 10-byte file: `frameCount == 0` and the warning `"10 trailing bytes ignored"`;
  - an empty file: `frameCount == 0` and no warning.
- `raw: unaligned frame offsets` — 5 frames of yuv420p 33×17 (odd frame bytes, so frames cross 4096-byte boundaries): every frame equals its `pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, f, w, h, seed, i).value())`, and `info().reader` is `"unbuffered"` or `"buffered"` (logged with `MESSAGE`).
- `raw: errors`:
  - `fmt=nope` → `Format`;
  - `size=12` → `Format`;
  - `foo=1` → `Format`;
  - `fmt=v210&size=33x17` → `Format`;
  - a missing file → `Io`;
  - a directory path with `?fmt=gray&size=2x2` → `Io`.
- `raw: non-ASCII path` — write `clip_ä日本_33x17_yuv420p.yuv` with `std::filesystem::path(u8"…")`. The bare-path URI is built from `reinterpret_cast<const char*>(path.u8string().c_str())`. The file opens with the 70 match and frame 0 equals.
- `raw: resolved URI reopens` — reopening `info().resolvedUri` gives an equal desc, fps, frameCount and frame 1 bytes.

Change the conformance list to `{"raw", "synthetic"}`.

`raw: frame read plan` fails first: it does not link without `file_read.cpp`. The other cases fail with "no source provider can open".

**Notes:**
- Design references: §4.5 URIs, §5.5, §5.6, D8, D9, D19, §7, R4, R5; arbiter minor notes (`u8string`).

- Note (plan choice): a file smaller than one frame opens with 0 frames (`info` shows it; `dump` then exits 1).
- Note (plan choice): the default fps is 25/1 for raw files, as for synthetic sources.
- Note (plan choice): the raw provider ignores the head. A Y4M file opened with an explicit `fmt` and `size` is `Ambiguous` (95 against 100); `?impl=raw` or `?impl=y4m` settles it.
