### Task 12: Y4M provider

**Goal:** The `y4m` source provider, which reads YUV4MPEG2 files with parameterless `FRAME` headers by random access, mapping the `C` tag onto the catalogue.

**Files:**
- Create `dvv-media/src/sources/y4m.cpp`: `Y4mProvider`, `Y4mSource` and the header parser.
- Create `dvv-media/src/sources/y4m_header.h` (private): the parser declaration, for the unit tests.
- Modify `dvv-media/CMakeLists.txt`: add the file to `dvv_media_sources`.
- Create `dvv-media/tests/test_y4m_source.cpp`.
- Modify `dvv-media/tests/test_provider_conformance.cpp`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 11: `planFrameRead`, `readFrame`, `SourceInfo::reader`.
  - Task 9: `uri_params.h`.
  - Task 4: `FileReader`.
  - Task 10: `SourceHarness`.
- Produces (private, `y4m_header.h`, namespace `dvv::media::detail`):

```cpp
struct Y4mHeader {
    std::size_t bytes = 0;                 // header length including its '\n'
    std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    Rational fps{25, 1};
    FormatId format = 0;
    ColourRange range = ColourRange::Unspecified;
};
// `head` = the first bytes of the file (up to 4096). Errors below.
[[nodiscard]] Result<Y4mHeader> parseY4mHeader(std::span<const std::byte> head);
```

- Produces: the component `y4m` (`Y4mProvider`, name "YUV4MPEG2 file", kind Source, version 1, schemes `{"file"}`, empty schema, `probe` returns `{}`) and `Y4mSource` (`final`, `Managed`, `CreationKey<SourceFactory>`).

**Behaviour:**
- **Magic.** The header starts with `YUV4MPEG2 `; otherwise `Format` "not a YUV4MPEG2 file".
- **Line ending.** The header ends at the first `\n` within the first 4096 bytes. Otherwise `Format` "Y4M header longer than 4096 bytes or not terminated". A `\r` before the `\n` → `Unsupported` "Y4M header ends with CR LF" (R10).
- **Tags** are separated by single spaces:
  - `W<n>`, `H<n>`: required, 1..65535; missing or bad → `Format`;
  - `F<n>:<d>`: n, d > 0; absent → 25/1; malformed → `Format`;
  - `I<c>`, `A<n>:<d>`: accepted and ignored (F6);
  - `C<cs>`: see below;
  - `X<anything>`: `XCOLORRANGE=FULL` → Full and `XCOLORRANGE=LIMITED` → Limited; any other value of `XCOLORRANGE` → `Format`; other `X` tags are ignored.
  - Any other tag letter → `Format` "unknown Y4M header tag 'Q'".
- **`C` mapping** (D15):
  - `420jpeg 420paldv 420mpeg2 420` → yuv420p;
  - `422` → yuv422p;
  - `444` → yuv444p;
  - `mono` → gray;
  - `420p10 422p10 444p10` → yuv420p10le yuv422p10le yuv444p10le;
  - `420p12 422p12 444p12` → yuv420p12le yuv422p12le yuv444p12le;
  - `420p16 444p16` → yuv420p16le yuv444p16le;
  - `mono16` → gray16le;
  - a missing `C` → yuv420p;
  - anything else → `Unsupported` "Y4M colourspace 'X' is not supported".

  Chroma siting is not carried (future `ChromaLocation`, M3).
- **`match`:** the head starts with the magic → confidence 100, why "YUV4MPEG2 magic", with a guess from `parseY4mHeader` when that succeeds (empty otherwise). Else 0, "no YUV4MPEG2 magic".
- **`open`:**
  1. `co_await resumeOn(ctx.io)`.
  2. `checkParameters(uri, {fmt, size, fps})`.
  3. `FileReader::open(path, Unbuffered)` (`Io`).
  4. Read the first `min(size, 4096)` bytes for the header: an `allocateAligned` buffer of `alignUp(4096, a)` bytes, read with `readAt(0, …)`.
  5. `parseY4mHeader`.
  6. Check the query: `fmt`, `size` and `fps` in the query must equal the header's values, else `Format` "Y4M header says <value>".
  7. `frameBytes = geometry(format, w, h).bytes`; `stride = 6 + frameBytes`.
  8. `frameCount = (size − header.bytes) / stride`. A remainder *r* > 0 adds `"<r> trailing bytes ignored"`.
  9. `desc.colour = internColour({.range = header.range})`.
  10. `resolvedUri = file:<path>?fmt=<name>&size=WxH&fps=<formatFps>&impl=y4m`.
  11. Set `reader`, `access = RandomCheap` and `provider = "y4m"`.
- **`produce(i)`:**
  1. Index outside → `Internal`.
  2. `co_await resumeOn(ctx.io())`.
  3. `data = header.bytes + i · stride + 6`; `plan = planFrameRead(data, frameBytes, 6, a)` (the range starts at `alignDown(data − 6, a)`, so the marker is inside it).
  4. `allocateHost(desc, plan.rangeBytes, max(a, 64), plan.dataOffset)`.
  5. `readFrame`.
  6. Check that the 6 bytes before the frame are `FRAME\n`; otherwise `Unsupported` "Y4M frame parameters are not supported (frame N of '<file>')".
  7. `publish`.

**Tests first.** `tests/test_y4m_source.cpp` writes files into a temporary directory: the header text, then per frame `FRAME\n` and the `pack(detail::generate(detail::Pattern::Noise, f, w, h, seed, i).value())` bytes.
- `y4m: header with every tag` — `YUV4MPEG2 W33 H17 F30000:1001 Ip A1:1 C420p10 XYSCSS=420P10 XCOLORRANGE=FULL\n`, 3 frames:
  - desc yuv420p10le 33×17;
  - `fps == {30000, 1001}`;
  - `colourParams(colour).range == Full`;
  - frames 0..2 equal the packed noise.
- `y4m: every C mapping` — a table of the 16 `C` values → expected format names, checked through `parseY4mHeader`. A missing `C` gives yuv420p; `C411` and `Cmono10` → `Unsupported`.
- `y4m: header errors`:
  - missing `W` → `Format`;
  - `W0` → `Format`;
  - `F30:0` → `Format`;
  - tag `Q1` → `Format`;
  - `XCOLORRANGE=WIDE` → `Format`;
  - CRLF → `Unsupported`;
  - no `\n` in 4096 bytes → `Format`;
  - a missing magic → `Format`.
- `y4m: XCOLORRANGE` — LIMITED → Limited; absent → Unspecified.
- `y4m: frame parameters are Unsupported` — frame 1 is written with the marker `FRAME Ip\n`: frame 0 is ok, frame 1 → `Unsupported` with "frame parameters".
- `y4m: frames at unaligned offsets` — 5 frames of yuv420p 33×17 (header 30-odd bytes): every frame equals its packed bytes.
- `y4m: trailing bytes` — 2 frames + 3 bytes: `frameCount == 2` and the warning `"3 trailing bytes ignored"`.
- `y4m: the magic decides`:
  - `clip.y4m` opens with `y4m`;
  - a headerless file named `x_33x17_yuv420p.y4m` opens with `raw` (70);
  - a Y4M file opened with `?fmt=yuv420p&size=33x17` is `Ambiguous`, with candidates y4m (100) and raw (95).
- `y4m: resolved URI reopens` — the resolved URI equals `file:<path>?fmt=yuv420p10le&size=33x17&fps=30000/1001&impl=y4m`, and reopening it gives the same frames. `…&impl=y4m&fmt=yuv420p` → `Format` (the header says yuv420p10le).

Change the conformance list to `{"raw", "synthetic", "y4m"}`.

`y4m: every C mapping` fails first: it does not compile without `y4m_header.h`.

**Steps:**
- [ ] **Step 1: write the tests and the expected list; see them fail.** Run `Push-Location dvv-media; cmake --build --preset vs2026; Pop-Location`. Expected: a compile error (`parseY4mHeader`).

**Notes:**
- Design references: D15, §5.6, §7, R10, F6.
- Note (plan choice): Y4M accepts `fmt`, `size` and `fps` only as checks against the header, and its resolved URI adds `impl=y4m`. Without it, reopening the resolved URI is `Ambiguous`: raw claims 95 for the explicit `fmt` and `size`, y4m 100. The rule "the resolved URI always carries fmt, size and fps" still holds.
- Note (plan choice): unknown header tag letters are `Format` errors (strict), while unknown `X` tags are ignored as the format intends.

### Task 13: `rawreel-cli` info and dump

**Goal:** `rawreel-cli` in the rawreel repo, with:
- `info <uri> [--json]`;
- `dump <uri> --out <file> [--frame N] [--count K] [--view original|planes] [--via cpu|gpu|auto] [--json]`;
- `--version` and `--help`.

It also has the exit codes of §10.3, `wmain` with UTF-8 arguments on Windows, the shutdown order of §5.7, and end-to-end CTest scripts, including the GPU-equals-CPU check of exit criterion 2.

**Files** (rawreel, branch `m2`):
- Modify the rawreel CMake file that declares its dvv dependencies (top-level `CMakeLists.txt`; M1b): `FetchContent_Declare(dvv_media …)`, the `FetchContent_MakeAvailable` list, the sibling check, `rawreel_dvv_version`, and the targets `rawreel_cli_core` and `rawreel_cli`. Put them next to `rawreel_render`, outside the `if(NOT DVV_SANITIZE)` block that guards `rawreel_gui`.
- Create the sources:
  - `rawreel/src/cli/main.cpp`: the entry point;

  - `rawreel/src/cli/utf8_arguments.h` and `.cpp`: the Windows wide → UTF-8 helper (the file body is under `#if defined(_WIN32)`, so the file is in the compile database everywhere).
- Create the unit tests: `rawreel/tests/cli_main.cpp`, `rawreel/tests/test_cli_arguments.cpp`, `rawreel/tests/test_cli_output.cpp`, `rawreel/tests/test_cli_utf8_arguments.cpp`. Sources and tests include `"cli/arguments.h"` etc., so M1b's `src/arguments.h` cannot be picked by mistake.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:

  - Task 9: `SourceFactory`, `Ambiguous`.

  - dvv-base: `ExecutorFactory`, `syncWait`, `finishWithLeakCheck`, `Logger`.
- Produces (design §4.6, exactly):

```cpp
namespace rawreel::cli {
enum class Command : std::uint8_t { Info, Dump, Bench, Version, Help };
enum class View : std::uint8_t { Original, Planes };
enum class Via : std::uint8_t { Auto, Cpu, Gpu };
struct Arguments { Command command; std::string uri, out; std::int64_t frame = 0, count = 1,
                   frames = 60, inflight = 4; View view = View::Original; Via via = Via::Auto;
                   bool json = false; };
[[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<Arguments> parseArguments(std::span<const std::string_view> args);
[[nodiscard]] int exitCodeFor(const dvv::Error& error, bool gpu_stage) noexcept;
// output.h
[[nodiscard]] std::string usageText();
[[nodiscard]] std::string infoText(const dvv::media::SourceInfo& info);
[[nodiscard]] dvv::json::Value infoJson(const dvv::media::SourceInfo& info);
[[nodiscard]] dvv::json::Value convertersJson(const dvv::media::StoreStats& stats);
[[nodiscard]] dvv::json::Value errorJson(std::string_view code, std::string_view message);
[[nodiscard]] dvv::json::Value ambiguousJson(std::string_view uri, const dvv::media::Ambiguous& ambiguous);
// commands.h: runs a parsed command, prints results and errors, returns the exit code.
[[nodiscard]] int run(const Arguments& arguments);
// utf8_arguments.h (Windows only)
[[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::vector<std::string>> utf8Arguments(int argc, wchar_t** argv);
}
```

- CMake: `rawreel_cli_core` is STATIC (arguments, output, commands, utf8_arguments) and links `dvv::media`. `rawreel_cli` (`OUTPUT_NAME rawreel-cli`, console) is `main.cpp`, linked to `rawreel_cli_core` plus `dvv_link_components(rawreel_cli dvv::media_sources)`. The CTest names are `rawreel.cli_unit`, `rawreel.cli_e2e.{info,dump,errors,unicode}`, and `rawreel.cli_e2e.gpu` (label `gpu`).
- Exit codes:
  - 0 success;
  - 1 bad arguments, including a frame range outside the source;
  - 2 input error;
  - 3 unsupported or ambiguous;
  - 4 GPU error.

**Behaviour:**
- **`parseArguments`** (args exclude the program name). Every error is `Errc::Format` with a message; `run`'s caller maps a parse failure to exit 1.
  - No arguments → "missing command".
  - The first argument is the command: `info`, `dump`, `bench`, `--version` or `--help`. Anything else → "unknown command 'x'".
  - `--version` and `--help` take no other argument.
  - Exactly one positional URI for `info`/`dump`/`bench`; missing → "missing <uri>"; a second → "unexpected argument 'x'".
  - Options take their value in the next argument (`--frame 3`; no `=` form). A missing value → "option --out needs a value".
  - Allowed options: `info`: `--json`; `dump`: `--out` (required), `--frame` (≥ 0), `--count` (≥ 1), `--view original|planes`, `--via cpu|gpu|auto`, `--json`; `bench`: `--frames` (≥ 1), `--inflight` (≥ 1), `--json`.
  - Another known option → "option --x is not valid for <command>"; an unknown one → "unknown option '--x'"; a repeated one → "option --x given twice".
  - Numbers are decimal `std::int64_t`; "bad number 'x' for --frame".
- **`exitCodeFor(error, gpu_stage)`:**

| Errc | gpu_stage = false | gpu_stage = true |
|---|---|---|
| Io, Format | 2 | 2 |
| Unsupported | 3 | 4 |
| OutOfMemory | 2 | 4 |
| DeviceLost, ShaderCompile | 4 | 4 |
| Internal, Cancelled | 2 | 4 |

  `gpu_stage = true` applies to errors from the GPU setup: `GpuRegistry::create`, `createContext` and `FrameStore::create` with a context (unpack pipeline). It also applies to `OutOfMemory` from an acquire in a store that has a context. An `Ambiguous` open → exit 3.
- **`main`:**
  - On Windows it is `int wmain(int argc, wchar_t** argv)`, which converts with `utf8Arguments` (an error → message, exit 1); on Linux it is `int main(int, char**)`. The console subsystem picks the `wmain` CRT entry by itself; no `/ENTRY` is needed.
  - It adds a `StderrSink` at level Warn and sets `LifetimeRegistry::setDetailed(true)` in Debug builds.
  - It parses. A failure prints `usageText()` and the message to stderr (with `--json` among the arguments: `{"error":{"code":"Arguments","message":…}}` on stdout instead) and exits 1.
  - Otherwise it runs `code = run(arguments)`, catches every exception (prints "internal error: <what>", code 2) and returns `dvv::finishWithLeakCheck(code)`. All objects are destroyed inside `run`.
  - All printing uses `std::print`/`std::println` (UTF-8; with `/utf-8`, MSVC writes Unicode to a console).
- **`run` common part** (info and dump):
  1. Create pools: `io = makeThreadPool(4)`, `cpu = makeThreadPool(0)`.
  2. `registry = ComponentRegistry::create(Capabilities{})`.
  3. `factory = SourceFactory::create(*registry, io)`.
  4. `parseUri(uri)` (`Format` → 2).
  5. `syncWait(factory->open({uri}))`. An error → `exitCodeFor(error, false)`. `Ambiguous` → text on stderr: `ambiguous source '<uri>'; candidates (pass ?impl=<id> or more parameters):`, then one line per candidate, `  <id>: confidence <n>, <why>`. With `--json`, print `ambiguousJson` on stdout: `{"error":{"code":"Ambiguous","message":…,"candidates":[{"provider","confidence","why"}]}}`. Exit 3.

  Every error is printed as text on stderr (`error: <describe()>`), or with `--json` as `errorJson(errcName(code), message)` on stdout.
- **`info`** prints `infoText` (stdout), or `infoJson` with `--json` (`dump(value, 2)`). Exit 0. No store and no GPU are involved.
  - `infoText` lines, in order:
    - `uri: <resolvedUri>`;
    - `provider: <id>`;
    - `format: <name>`;
    - `size: <W>x<H>`;
    - `fps: <num>/<den>`;
    - `frames: <n or unbounded>`;
    - `frame bytes: <geometry bytes>`;
    - `colour: matrix=<m> range=<r> transfer=<t> primaries=<p>`;
    - `access: random-cheap|random-costly|sequential`;
    - `reader: <reader>`, only when non-empty;
    - `warning: <text>`, one line per warning.
  - `infoJson` keys:
    - `uri`, `provider`;
    - `format` (= `formatToJson(desc.format)`, whose `name` member is the format name);
    - `width`, `height`;
    - `fps` (`{"num","den"}`);
    - `frames` (number or null);
    - `frameBytes`;
    - `colour` (`{"matrix","range","transfer","primaries"}`);
    - `access` (`randomCheap|randomCostly|sequential`), `deterministic`;
    - `reader` (string or null), `warnings` (array).
  - Colour names:
    - matrix: `unspecified bt601 bt709 bt2020 rgb`;
    - range: `unspecified limited full`;
    - transfer: `unspecified srgb bt1886 pq hlg linear`;
    - primaries: `unspecified bt601-525 bt601-625 bt709 bt2020`.
- **`dump`:**
  1. If `frameCount` is set and `frame + count > frameCount` → "frames <N>..<N+K−1> are outside the source (<F> frames)", exit 1.
  2. Resolve `--via`:
     - `cpu` → no Vulkan at all.
     - `gpu` → `GpuRegistry::create()` (options from the environment). An `Unsupported` error, or no `bestDevice()`, becomes `Unsupported` "no usable GPU device: <reason>", and other errors pass through. Then `createContext(*bestDevice())`. Any error → `exitCodeFor(e, true)`, so a missing device exits 4.
     - `auto` → as `gpu`, but "no usable GPU device" falls back to cpu (debug log).
  3. `store = FrameStore::create({.cpu, .io, .gpu = context or null})` (with a context, errors → `exitCodeFor(e, true)`).
  4. `origin = store->attach(std::shared_ptr<ISource>(std::move(source)))`.
  5. `domain = cpu ? store->hostDomain() : *store->stagingDomain()`.
  6. Open `--out` with `std::ofstream(pathFromUtf8(out), binary | trunc)`. A failure → `Io` "cannot write '<out>'", exit 2.
  7. For k in 0..K−1, sequentially: `read = syncWait(store->acquireOriginal({origin, N + k}, Api::HostPlanes, domain, *cpu, view == View::Original))`. An error → `exitCodeFor(e, context && code == OutOfMemory)`. Write the `HostPlanesB` planes in order: each plane's span, which is `rows × stride` bytes of a tight layout. Then drop the lease.
  8. Close the file and check the stream state (`Io` on failure).
  9. Print `wrote <K> frames (<bytes> bytes) of <format name> to <out> via <cpu|gpu>`. With `--json`, print instead:
     - `command`, `uri` (resolved), `first`, `frames`, `bytes`;
     - `view` (`original|planes`), `via` (`cpu|gpu`);
     - `format` (the name of the written format, e.g. `v210@planes`);
     - `out`;
     - `converters` (`convertersJson`: an array of `{"name","runs","bytes","nanoseconds"}`).
- **Shutdown order** (§5.7, also on error paths: write `run` so that scopes end in this order):
  1. `detach(origin)`;
  2. drop the leases;
  3. destroy the factory, then the store;
  4. `context->drain()`;
  5. reset the context, then the GPU registry and the component registry;
  6. reset the pools.

  `main` then calls `finishWithLeakCheck`.
- **`--version`** prints the lines `rawreel <v>`, `dvv-base <v>`, `dvv-registry <v>`, `dvv-platform <v>`, `dvv-gpu <v>` and `dvv-media <v>`. With `--json` it prints `{"rawreel":…,"dvv_base":…,…}`. The values come from compile definitions on `rawreel_cli_core` (`RAWREEL_VERSION="…"`, `RAWREEL_DVV_BASE_VERSION="…"`, …) set from the CMake helper below. `--help` prints `usageText()` to stdout, exit 0.

**Code.**

The UTF-8 argument helper (`src/cli/utf8_arguments.cpp`; platform call):

```cpp
#include "cli/utf8_arguments.h"

#if defined(_WIN32)
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define NOMINMAX
#include <windows.h>

#include <format>

namespace rawreel::cli {

dvv::Result<std::vector<std::string>> utf8Arguments(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    std::vector<std::string> arguments;
    arguments.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(argc));
    for (int i = 0; i < argc; ++i) {
        const wchar_t* wide = argv[i];
        const int length = static_cast<int>(std::wcslen(wide));
        if (length == 0) {
            arguments.emplace_back();
            continue;
        }
        const int bytes = WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, WC_ERR_INVALID_CHARS, wide, length,
                                              nullptr, 0, nullptr, nullptr);
        if (bytes <= 0) {
            return dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Format,
                                  std::format("argument {} is not valid Unicode", i));
        }
        std::string text(static_cast<std::size_t>(bytes), '\0');
        WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, WC_ERR_INVALID_CHARS, wide, length, text.data(), bytes,
                            nullptr, nullptr);
        arguments.push_back(std::move(text));
    }
    return arguments;
}

}  // namespace rawreel::cli
#endif
```

`main.cpp` (shape):

```cpp
namespace {
int runWith(const std::vector<std::string>& all) {
    std::vector<std::string_view> args(all.begin() + (all.empty() ? 0 : 1), all.end());
    auto parsed = rawreel::cli::parseArguments(args);
    if (!parsed) { /* usage + message (or JSON when "--json" is among args) */ return 1; }
    return rawreel::cli::run(*parsed);
}
int guarded(const std::vector<std::string>& all) {
    int code = 2;
    try { code = runWith(all); } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        std::println(stderr, "internal error: {}", e.what());
    }
    return dvv::finishWithLeakCheck(code);
}
}  // namespace
#if defined(_WIN32)
int wmain(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    setUpLogging();
    auto all = rawreel::cli::utf8Arguments(argc, argv);
    if (!all) { std::println(stderr, "error: {}", all.error().message); return 1; }
    return guarded(*all);
}
#else
int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    setUpLogging();
    return guarded(std::vector<std::string>(argv, argv + argc));
}
#endif
```

CMake version helper and targets (rawreel top-level CMake file):

```cmake
# rawreel_dvv_version(<package> <out>): the version of a dvv package, whether it was found
# installed (find_package sets <package>_VERSION) or added in-tree (read from its directory).
function(rawreel_dvv_version package out)
    set(version "")
    if(DEFINED ${package}_VERSION)
        set(version "${${package}_VERSION}")
    elseif(DEFINED ${package}_SOURCE_DIR)
        get_directory_property(version DIRECTORY "${${package}_SOURCE_DIR}"
                               DEFINITION ${package}_VERSION)
    endif()
    if(NOT version)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "rawreel: cannot find the version of ${package}")
    endif()
    set(${out} "${version}" PARENT_SCOPE)
endfunction()

add_library(rawreel_cli_core STATIC
    src/cli/arguments.cpp src/cli/commands.cpp src/cli/output.cpp src/cli/utf8_arguments.cpp)
target_include_directories(rawreel_cli_core PUBLIC ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src)
target_link_libraries(rawreel_cli_core PUBLIC dvv::media)
set(cli_versions "RAWREEL_VERSION=\"${PROJECT_VERSION}\"")
foreach(package IN ITEMS dvv_base dvv_registry dvv_platform dvv_gpu dvv_media)
    rawreel_dvv_version(${package} version)
    string(TOUPPER "${package}" upper)
    list(APPEND cli_versions "RAWREEL_${upper}_VERSION=\"${version}\"")
endforeach()
target_compile_definitions(rawreel_cli_core PRIVATE ${cli_versions})
dvv_set_compiler_options(rawreel_cli_core)

# rawreel-cli (spec §10.3): console, no wx; built under every preset including the sanitizers.
add_executable(rawreel_cli src/cli/main.cpp)
set_target_properties(rawreel_cli PROPERTIES OUTPUT_NAME rawreel-cli)
target_link_libraries(rawreel_cli PRIVATE rawreel_cli_core)
dvv_link_components(rawreel_cli dvv::media_sources)
dvv_set_compiler_options(rawreel_cli)
```

The shared e2e helper (`tests/cli/cli_common.cmake`):

```cmake
# Included by the run-cli-*.cmake scripts. Needs -DCLI=<rawreel-cli> and -DWORK_DIR=<dir>.
if(NOT CLI OR NOT WORK_DIR)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "run with -DCLI=<rawreel-cli> -DWORK_DIR=<scratch dir>")
endif()
file(REMOVE_RECURSE "${WORK_DIR}")
file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${WORK_DIR}")

# run_cli(<expected exit code> <stdout var> <stderr var> <arguments>...): runs the CLI in
# WORK_DIR and fails unless it exits with the expected code. A leak report fails every run.
function(run_cli expected out_var err_var)
    execute_process(COMMAND "${CLI}" ${ARGN} WORKING_DIRECTORY "${WORK_DIR}"
                    RESULT_VARIABLE code OUTPUT_VARIABLE out ERROR_VARIABLE err)
    if(err MATCHES "managed object\\(s\\) still alive at exit")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "rawreel-cli ${ARGN}: leak report\n${err}")
    endif()
    if(NOT code STREQUAL "${expected}")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "rawreel-cli ${ARGN}: exit ${code}, expected ${expected}\n"
                            "stdout:\n${out}\nstderr:\n${err}")
    endif()
    set(${out_var} "${out}" PARENT_SCOPE)
    set(${err_var} "${err}" PARENT_SCOPE)
endfunction()

# converter_runs(<json> <name> <out var>): the runs of one converter in dump --json (0 if absent).
function(converter_runs json name out_var)
    string(JSON count LENGTH "${json}" converters)
    set(runs 0)
    if(count GREATER 0)
        math(EXPR last "${count} - 1")
        foreach(i RANGE ${last})
            string(JSON entry_name GET "${json}" converters ${i} name)
            if(entry_name STREQUAL name)
                string(JSON runs GET "${json}" converters ${i} runs)
            endif()
        endforeach()
    endif()
    set(${out_var} ${runs} PARENT_SCOPE)
endfunction()

function(expect_match text regex what)
    if(NOT text MATCHES "${regex}")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "${what}: '${regex}' not found in:\n${text}")
    endif()
endfunction()
```

The GPU script (`tests/cli/run-cli-gpu.cmake`; the 77 mapping):

```cmake
include("${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/cli_common.cmake")

# Without a usable device the CLI exits 4 with "no usable GPU device"; that is a skip (77) unless
# DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1 (CI and local runs that must not skip).
execute_process(COMMAND "${CLI}" dump "synthetic:noise?fmt=gray&size=2x2" --via gpu
                        --out probe.raw WORKING_DIRECTORY "${WORK_DIR}"
                RESULT_VARIABLE code OUTPUT_VARIABLE out ERROR_VARIABLE err)
if(code EQUAL 4 AND err MATCHES "no usable GPU device")
    if("$ENV{DVV_REQUIRE_GPU}" STREQUAL "1")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1, but: ${err}")
    endif()
    message(STATUS "skipped: ${err}")
    cmake_language(EXIT 77)
endif()
if(NOT code EQUAL 0)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "probe dump failed with ${code}:\n${err}")
endif()

foreach(format IN ITEMS v210 p010le rgb48be nv12)
    set(uri "synthetic:noise?fmt=${format}&size=34x17&frames=3&seed=5")
    foreach(view IN ITEMS planes original)
        run_cli(0 cpu_json err dump "${uri}" --count 3 --view ${view} --via cpu
                --out ${format}-${view}-cpu.bin --json)
        run_cli(0 gpu_json err dump "${uri}" --count 3 --view ${view} --via gpu
                --out ${format}-${view}-gpu.bin --json)
        if(err MATCHES "VUID|Validation Error")
            message(FATAL_ERROR "validation message for ${format} ${view}:\n${err}")
        endif()
        execute_process(COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E compare_files
                                ${format}-${view}-cpu.bin ${format}-${view}-gpu.bin
                        WORKING_DIRECTORY "${WORK_DIR}" RESULT_VARIABLE differ)
        if(differ)
            message(FATAL_ERROR "${format} ${view}: --via gpu differs from --via cpu")
        endif()
        if(view STREQUAL "planes")
            converter_runs("${gpu_json}" GpuUnpack runs)
        else()
            converter_runs("${gpu_json}" Upload runs)
        endif()
        if(NOT runs GREATER 0)
            message(FATAL_ERROR "${format} ${view}: --via gpu ran no GPU converter")
        endif()
    endforeach()
endforeach()
```

**Tests first.**

Unit tests in `rawreel_cli_tests` (CTest `rawreel.cli_unit`; `tests/cli_main.cpp` is the doctest main with `finishWithLeakCheck`, as in dvv-base):
- `arguments: info` — `{"info", "x.yuv"}` → command Info, uri "x.yuv" and `json == false`; `{"info", "x.yuv", "--json"}` → `json == true`.
- `arguments: dump with every option` — `dump u --out o --frame 2 --count 3 --view planes --via gpu --json` → every field.
- `arguments: dump defaults` — frame 0, count 1, view Original, via Auto.
- `arguments: bench defaults` — `{"bench", "u"}` → frames 60, inflight 4.
- `arguments: version and help` — `{"--version"}` → Version and `{"--help"}` → Help; `{"--version", "x"}` is an error.
- `arguments: errors` — each of these is an error, and the message contains the quoted text:
  - `{}` → "missing command";
  - `{"play"}` → "unknown command";
  - `{"dump", "u"}` → "--out";
  - `{"info"}` → "missing";
  - `{"info", "a", "b"}` → "unexpected argument";
  - `{"info", "u", "--out", "o"}` → "not valid for info";
  - `{"dump", "u", "--out"}` → "needs a value";
  - `{"dump", "u", "--out", "o", "--frame", "-1"}`;
  - `{"dump", "u", "--out", "o", "--count", "0"}`;
  - `{"dump", "u", "--out", "o", "--view", "x"}`;
  - `{"dump", "u", "--out", "o", "--via", "x"}`;
  - `{"dump", "u", "--out", "o", "--out", "p"}` → "given twice";
  - `{"info", "u", "--bogus"}` → "unknown option";
  - `{"dump", "u", "--out", "o", "--frame", "x"}` → "bad number".
- `exit codes: table` — every row of the table above for both values of `gpu_stage` (16 checks).
- `output: infoJson keys` — a hand-made `SourceInfo` (v210 34×17, fps 25/1, frames 3, warnings `{"5 trailing bytes ignored"}`, reader "buffered"):
  - `j["format"]["name"] == "v210"`;
  - `j["frames"] == 3`;
  - `j["frameBytes"] == geometry(v210, 34, 17)->bytes`;
  - `j["colour"]["range"] == "unspecified"`;
  - `j["reader"] == "buffered"`;
  - `j["warnings"][0] == "5 trailing bytes ignored"`;
  - `frames: null` when `frameCount` is nullopt.
- `output: infoText lines` — contains `"format: v210\n"`, `"size: 34x17\n"` and `"warning: 5 trailing bytes ignored\n"`.
- `utf8 arguments: conversion` (Windows only, `#if defined(_WIN32)`):
  - `{L"rawreel-cli", L"ä日本🎞"}` → the second argument equals `reinterpret_cast<const char*>(u8"ä日本🎞")`;
  - an unpaired surrogate `L"\xD800"` → `Format`.

The e2e scripts below use `cli_common.cmake`; each uses a fresh `WORK_DIR`.
- `run-cli-info.cmake`:
  - `info "synthetic:gradient?fmt=v210&size=34x17"` → exit 0; stdout matches `format: v210` and `size: 34x17`.
  - The same with `--json` → `string(JSON … GET … provider)` == `synthetic`, `format name` == `v210`, `width` == 34 and `frames` == 10.
  - A raw file written by `dump "synthetic:noise?fmt=yuv420p&size=4x2" --count 2 --out a_4x2_yuv420p.yuv` → `info a_4x2_yuv420p.yuv --json` gives provider `raw` and frames 2.
  - A Y4M file written with `file(CONFIGURE OUTPUT y.y4m CONTENT "YUV4MPEG2 W4 H2 F25:1 Cmono\nFRAME\nABCDEFGHFRAME\nIJKLMNOP" NEWLINE_STYLE LF)` → `info y.y4m --json` gives provider `y4m`, format name `gray`, frames 2 **and** `warnings[0] == "1 trailing bytes ignored"`.
  - Then `dump y.y4m --frame 1 --view original --via cpu --out f1.raw`, after which `file(READ f1.raw c)` equals `IJKLMNOP`.
- `run-cli-dump.cmake`:
  1. `dump "synthetic:noise?fmt=v210&size=34x17&seed=2" --count 3 --view original --via cpu --out clip_34x17_v210.yuv` → exit 0.
  2. `info clip_34x17_v210.yuv --json` gives provider `raw`, format name `v210` and frames 3.
  3. `dump <synthetic uri> --count 3 --view planes --via cpu --out a.planes` and `dump clip_34x17_v210.yuv --count 3 --view planes --via cpu --out b.planes`: `compare_files a.planes b.planes` is equal.
  4. The `--json` of the cpu planes dump: `converter_runs` of `HostToStaging`, `Upload`, `GpuUnpack` and `Readback` are all 0, and `CpuUnpack` > 0.
  5. `file(SIZE a.planes)` equals 3 × 34 × 17 × 2 × 2 (Y plus two half-width chroma planes at 16 bits).
- `run-cli-errors.cmake`:
  - `info missing.yuv` → 2;
  - `info missing.yuv --json` → 2, and stdout JSON `error.code` == `Io`;
  - `file(WRITE clip_33x17.yuv "x")`, then `info clip_33x17.yuv` → 3, and stderr matches `raw: confidence 30`;
  - `dump x` → 1;
  - `play` → 1;
  - `dump "synthetic:noise?fmt=gray&size=2x2&frames=2" --frame 2 --out o` → 1;
  - `--help` → 0, and stdout matches `rawreel-cli dump`;
  - `--version` → 0, and stdout matches `dvv-media [0-9]`;
  - `info "synthetic:noise?fmt=nope&size=2x2"` → 2.
- `run-cli-unicode.cmake`:
  1. `file(WRITE "${WORK_DIR}/clip_ä日本_4x2_gray.yuv" "ABCDEFGH")` (the script is UTF-8).
  2. `info "${WORK_DIR}/clip_ä日本_4x2_gray.yuv"` → 0, and stdout matches `format: gray`.
  3. `dump <same> --view original --via cpu --out "${WORK_DIR}/out_ä.raw"` → 0, and the file content is `ABCDEFGH`.
- `run-cli-gpu.cmake`: the code above. On Windows NVIDIA it runs with `DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1`.

CTest registration in `tests/CMakeLists.txt`:

Its environment is `ENVIRONMENT "${gpu_test_environment};DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync;DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED=1;DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail"`, where `gpu_test_environment` is M1b's variable in `rawreel/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (validation-layer path, TSan suppressions).

**Notes:**
- Design references: §4.6, D19, §5.7, §10.3, §7, exit criteria 1 and 2, arbiter FIX 8 and FIX 21.
- Note (plan choice):
  - Argument errors bypass `exitCodeFor` (always 1).
  - `Internal`, `Cancelled` and host `OutOfMemory` map to 2.
  - `gpu_stage` is set for GPU setup errors and for `OutOfMemory` in a store with a context. Source-side `Unsupported` errors, e.g. Y4M frame parameters, therefore stay 3 under `--via gpu`.
- Note (plan choice): the Y4M fixture uses `file(CONFIGURE … NEWLINE_STYLE LF)` because `file(WRITE)` writes CR LF on Windows; it appends one LF, which exercises the trailing-bytes warning. The other fixtures contain no newline.
- Note (plan choice): the GPU script fails on any `VUID` or `Validation Error` text on stderr (the CLI logs validation messages there). This replaces a validation counter in the CLI.
- Note (plan choice): versions come from `get_directory_property(... DEFINITION <pkg>_VERSION)` for in-tree packages and from `<pkg>_VERSION` for installed ones.
- Risk: `cmake_language(EXIT 77)` needs CMake ≥ 3.29 (the pinned CMake 4.3 has it).

### Task 14: `bench` and the queue decision

**Goal:** `rawreel-cli bench <uri> [--frames N] [--inflight K] [--json]` runs frames through the real path (produce → staging → upload → unpack) with K reads in flight. It prints end-to-end frames/s and per-stage throughput. One measured run on local NVIDIA + NVMe applies the D18 decision rule, and the result is recorded in the plan and in spec §16.

**Files:**
- Modify `rawreel/src/cli/commands.cpp` (`bench`), `rawreel/src/cli/output.h` and `rawreel/src/cli/output.cpp` (`BenchReport`, `benchText`, `benchJson`).
- Modify `rawreel/tests/test_cli_output.cpp` (bench cases) and `rawreel/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (`rawreel.cli_e2e.bench`).
- Create `rawreel/tests/cli/run-cli-bench.cmake`.
- Modify the M2 plan file `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m2-media.md` (section "Changes during execution") and `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-rawreel-design.md` (§16).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 13: `Arguments::frames`/`inflight`, the GPU setup, the shutdown order, `exitCodeFor`.
  - Task 8: `acquireOriginal`, `deviceDomain()`, `stats().converters`.
  - Task 11: `SourceInfo::reader`.
  - dvv-base: `whenAll(std::vector<Task<T>>)`, `syncWait`.
- Produces (`output.h`):

```cpp
struct BenchStage { std::string name; std::uint64_t runs, bytes, nanoseconds; };
struct BenchReport {
    std::string uri, format, reader, device;   // reader: SourceInfo::reader ("" -> "none")
    std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    std::int64_t frames = 0, inflight = 0;
    std::uint64_t frameBytes = 0;              // geometry bytes of one original frame
    double seconds = 0;                        // wall time of the measured loop
    std::vector<BenchStage> stages;            // converters with runs > 0, Produce first
};
[[nodiscard]] std::string benchText(const BenchReport&);
[[nodiscard]] dvv::json::Value benchJson(const BenchReport&);
```

**Behaviour:**
- `bench` always uses the GPU. "No usable GPU device" → exit 4 (Task 13's setup with `gpu_stage = true`).
- With `frameCount` set and `frames > frameCount` → exit 1, "bench needs --frames <= <F> (the source has <F> frames)".
- The store is `FrameStore::create({.cpu, .io, .gpu = context})` with the default budgets.
- **Loop.** For `first = 0; first < N; first += K`:
  1. Build `std::vector<Task<Result<OriginalRead>>>` with `acquireOriginal({origin, i}, Api::VkImage, *store->deviceDomain(), *cpu)` for `i` in `[first, min(first + K, N))` (planes, Lossless).
  2. `syncWait(whenAll(std::move(tasks)))`.
  3. Fail on the first error (`exitCodeFor(e, code == OutOfMemory)`).
  4. Drop the leases.

  The wall time is `std::chrono::steady_clock` around the whole loop.
- **Report.**
  - `stages` = the `store->stats().converters` entries with `runs > 0`, in the order `Produce, HostToStaging, Upload, GpuUnpack`, then any others.
  - `fps = frames / seconds`.
  - End-to-end GB/s = `frames · frameBytes / seconds / 1e9`.
  - Per stage, GB/s = `bytes / nanoseconds` (bytes per nanosecond = GB/s; a per-run throughput, because the times of concurrent runs add up).
  - `device` = `context->deviceInfo().name`.
- **Text output:**

```
bench <uri>: <N> frames <W>x<H> <format>, inflight <K>, reader <reader>, device <device>
end-to-end: <fps, 1 decimal> frames/s, <GB/s, 2 decimals> GB/s in <seconds, 3 decimals> s
stage          runs        bytes   seconds   GB/s
Produce        <runs>  <bytes>  <s>  <GB/s>
...
```

- **JSON output:** `{"uri","device","format","width","height","frameBytes","frames","inflight","reader","seconds","fps","gbps","stages":[{"name","runs","bytes","nanoseconds","gbps"}]}`.
- **D18 decision rule** (applied by hand in Step 6 to the Step 5 numbers and written into spec §16): on local NVIDIA + NVMe with 4K yuv420p10le at K = 4:
  - end-to-end ≥ 60 fps → the universal queue stays (spec §17 default) and no M3 item;
  - otherwise the bottleneck stage's fallback becomes an M3 item:
    - Produce < 3 GB/s → F1 (IOCP on Windows / io_uring);
    - Upload < 3 GB/s → a transfer queue;
    - otherwise the R6 order: one submission for upload + unpack, then prefetch, then reads into staging (F3), then F1.

**Tests first:**
- `output: benchJson` (`test_cli_output.cpp`) — a report with `frames = 8`, `seconds = 0.5`, `frameBytes = 1000` and the stage `{"Upload", 8, 3'000'000'000, 1'000'000'000}`:
  - `j["fps"] == 16.0`;
  - `j["gbps"] == doctest::Approx(8 * 1000 / 0.5 / 1e9)`;
  - `j["stages"][0]["gbps"] == doctest::Approx(3.0)`;
  - `j["reader"] == "unbuffered"` for reader "unbuffered";
  - `j["reader"] == "none"` for an empty reader.
- `output: benchText` — the first line starts with `bench `, and a line starts with `Upload`.
- `run-cli-bench.cmake` (`rawreel.cli_e2e.bench`, `LABELS gpu`, `SKIP_RETURN_CODE 77`, the GPU environment of Task 13: `ENVIRONMENT "${gpu_test_environment};DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync;DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED=1;DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail"`). It uses the probe and 77 mapping of `run-cli-gpu.cmake`, with `bench` in place of `dump` as the probe. Then `bench "synthetic:noise?fmt=yuv420p10le&size=64x64&frames=8" --frames 8 --inflight 4 --json` → exit 0, with:
  - `fps` > 0;
  - `frames` == 8, `inflight` == 4;
  - stage runs `Produce` == 8, `HostToStaging` == 8, `Upload` == 8, `GpuUnpack` == 8 (read with `string(JSON …)` over `stages`);
  - `bench "synthetic:noise?fmt=gray&size=2x2&frames=2" --frames 3` → exit 1.

The unit cases fail first: `BenchReport` is undeclared.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 2: implement `bench`, `benchText` and `benchJson`.** Run `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 -E "\.package$" --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`. Expected: all pass, and `rawreel.cli_e2e.bench` runs (not skipped).
- [ ] **Step 3: Per-task checks.** In the background (one call): `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026 | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`; in parallel, `git -C rawreel add -A`, then clang-format on the changed files (`<base>` = the rawreel head before this task): `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd rawreel && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <base> -- "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r clang-format --dry-run --Werror && echo FORMAT-OK'`. Expected: `100% tests passed` in vs2026 Debug with no `Skipped` line for `rawreel.cli_e2e.gpu` or `rawreel.cli_e2e.bench`, then `FORMAT-OK`. Release, ASan and clang-tidy run at the Task 15 checkpoint.

```powershell
Push-Location rawreel; cmake --build --preset vs2026-release; Pop-Location
$cli = (Get-ChildItem -Recurse _build/rawreel/vs2026 -Filter rawreel-cli.exe | Where-Object FullName -Match '\\Release\\' | Select-Object -First 1).FullName
$benchDir = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'rawreel-bench'; New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $benchDir | Out-Null
$file = Join-Path $benchDir 'bench_3840x2160_yuv420p10le.yuv'
& $cli dump "synthetic:noise?fmt=yuv420p10le&size=3840x2160&frames=60" --view original --via cpu --count 60 --out $file
1..3 | ForEach-Object { & $cli bench $file --frames 60 --inflight 4 --json }
```

  The first line builds Release for the measurement only (no Release test run per task, decision U-PROC). Expected: a 1.49 GB file and three JSON reports. Take the median run by `fps`. The file is read unbuffered (reader `unbuffered`), so the OS cache does not flatter the numbers. If the reader shows `buffered`, record that.
  Then delete the bench file: `Remove-Item -Recurse $benchDir`. (The former lavapipe bench run for the record is not done; decision U-WIN.)

cmake -DREPO=rawreel-workspace -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake
```

  Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`. The records name no machine, user or path; a GPU model name is fine.

**Expected results:**
- `rawreel.cli_unit` gains 2 cases.
- `rawreel.cli_e2e.bench` passes on NVIDIA (vs2026 Debug); `FORMAT-OK`; privacy lints clean.
- The bench numbers and the rule's outcome are recorded.

**Notes:**
- Design references: D18, R6, F1, F3, F4, spec §16/§17; arbiter FIX 9.
- Note (plan choice): bench takes batches of K (`whenAll` of K reads, then the next K), as D18 states, not a sliding window. The target is VkImage planes in the device domain: exactly produce → staging → upload → unpack, with no readback.
- Note (plan choice): `--frames` larger than the source's frame count is an argument error (exit 1) rather than wrapping around, since wrapping would measure cache hits.
- Risk: a run below 60 fps creates an M3 item; that is the purpose of the measurement, not a failure of this task.

### Task 15: Spec, READMEs and final verification

**Goal:** Bring the spec and every README to the state M2 built, update the ledger, prove the M2 exit criteria with the `rawreel-workspace` Windows matrix, and run the milestone's one Linux sanity build (decision U-WIN).

**Files:**
- Modify `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-rawreel-design.md` (the sections listed below) and `rawreel-workspace/README.md` (layout and repository list, if Task 1 left anything out).
- Modify `dvv-media/README.md` (complete), `dvv-platform/README.md`, `dvv-gpu/README.md`, `dvv-registry/README.md` and `rawreel/README.md`.
- Update (not committed; inside `.git`) `rawreel-workspace/.git/sdd/progress.md`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the final code and names of Tasks 0-14 (documentation only), and the `## Changes during execution` section of `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m2-media.md`. If a task changed during execution, the docs follow the code, and the report says so.
- Produces: nothing for later tasks.

**Behaviour (documentation content).** In each spec section, replace the stale text with the M2 state. The text M1b Task 13b left may differ from the M0/M1a wording, so find each item's current sentence and replace it. Keep the spec's style: short sentences, no history.
- **§3.1:**
  - the dvv-platform row names `FileReader` (concrete handle; `IAsyncFileReader` is gone) next to the M1b items;
  - the dvv-gpu row says images reach shaders through push descriptors (no bindless heap);
  - the dvv-media row reads "pixel-format engine + catalogue + CPU reference, custom formats as JSON, `FrameStore` + `IFrame` implementations + converters, `ISource`/`ISourceProvider` + `SourceFactory`, core source providers (synthetic, raw, Y4M) in the component library `dvv_media_sources` (D1)", with dependencies base, registry, gpu, platform.

  Prefetch and media clock move out of the M2 text.
- **§4:**
  - file reads are blocking positional `FileReader::readAt` calls on an I/O thread pool (Windows: overlapped handle with a per-call event and a blocking wait; Linux: `O_DIRECT` + `pread`) — D8;
  - every requester resumes on its own executor;
  - prefetch with cancellation and the media clock arrive in M3.
- **§5.1:**
  - the required features gain `pushDescriptor`, `shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats` and `shaderStorageImageWriteWithoutFormat`;
  - R8_UINT/R16_UINT must support `STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT`;
  - the six descriptor-indexing features stay required, kept for a possible bindless heap (F12) and user shaders.
- **§5.3:** images are not pooled yet ("when measured", F10).
- **§5.4:**
  - push descriptors replace the bindless heap: `ComputeBindings{storageImages}`, set 0, `PUSH_DESCRIPTOR` layout, the `dispatch` overload moving images to `GENERAL`, the Slang `[format("unknown")] RWTexture2D<uint>` declaration (with the spelling Task 5 confirmed);
  - "no per-frame descriptor sets" still holds;
  - one generic unpack shader driven by push constants (absolute-byte two-dword loads, buffer size rule);
  - specialisation-constant fast paths only when measured;
  - a bindless heap is F12 (v1.x, if ever).
- **§6.2:** the M2 implementations `HostFrame`, `StagingFrame`, `VkBufferFrame` and `VkPlanesFrame`. A `HostUpload` `StagingFrame` offers only the `VkBuffer` view, a `HostReadback` one both. `MappedFrame` is v1.x and `AvFrameFrame` M4.
- **§6.3:** unpacked planes are one `R8_UINT`/`R16_UINT` image per component, LSB-aligned code values, `GENERAL` at rest; the host form is planar, tight and LE.
- **§6.4:** the M2 converters HostToStaging, Upload, GpuUnpack, Readback and CpuUnpack (the CPU fallback and the `--via cpu` path), with the cost model in one line. `ToDisplay` is M3; `Downscale`/`HostImageCopy` later.
- **§6.5:**
  - `attach`/`detach`;
  - `Task<Result<…>>` signatures;
  - `IExecutor& resume_on` on `acquire`, `acquireOriginal` and `pin`;
  - `acquire`, `acquireOriginal` and `pin` take the key (and view) by value;
  - one store mutex;
  - GPU conversions complete before publishing (no `retire()`);
  - the static converter graph with published and pending entries as plan sources, and the permanent no-path cache;
  - `StoreStats::converters`;
  - cancellation in M3 with the D10 hook.
- **§7.1–7.3:**
  - the provider interface (`schemes`, `match`, `open`), `OpenTicket` + `issueKey<T>`, and the confidence table and decision rule of §5.5;
  - Y4M limits (parameterless `FRAME`, CR LF unsupported, `C` mapping, missing `C` = 420jpeg);
  - the descriptor model D2 with `widthMultiple`, the 37-format catalogue (D3) and the layout goldens;
  - custom formats as JSON in M2 (`fmt=@<file.json>`), with the editor dialog in M3.
- **§9.4:** the new managed types and the handles (design §6 table, short form).
- **§10.3:** `info [--json]`, `dump` with its options, `bench [--frames N] [--inflight K]`, `--version`, `--help`; `wmain` with UTF-8 arguments on Windows; the exit codes, unchanged.
- **§10.4:** `FileReader` replaces `IAsyncFileReader`, with the open flags.
- **§12:** layout goldens per format and partial-group goldens; GPU unpack bit-exact against the CPU reference; the FFmpeg `extractplanes`/`framemd5` oracle is M4; CLI end-to-end scripts.
- **§15:**
  - the M2 row describes what was built; add a "**M2 demo**" paragraph with the three exit criteria and the tests that prove them, run on Windows (NVIDIA); Linux had the sanity build only (decision U-WIN);
  - "Deferred from M2" is the decided list of design §2 "Deferral decisions", each with its milestone, plus the U-WIN items for v1.x: Linux test runs (lavapipe GPU tests, the POSIX `FileReader` tests, ASan/TSan/UBSan), the lavapipe device check and the lavapipe bench;
  - in "Deferred from M1a", "images in ResourcePool: M2" becomes "when measured (F10)";
  - remove the kind-check item there (done in M2); conformance for dvv-components stays deferred;
  - add an "**Inherited by M3**" list: `ToDisplay` + colour engine, media clock, prefetch with cancellation, `VK_EXT_memory_budget` budgets, the custom-format editor dialog, and any item Task 14's bench rule produced.
- **§16:** keep Task 14's two items; add "mmap fallback: v1.x" if not already said.
- **§17:** "Decisions taken in M2" in the short form, one line each: D1–D20, plus the arbiter choices of `decisions.md` that are M2-design lines.

**READMEs:**
- **dvv-media:**
  - formats: the catalogue table (name, class, bits, planes) and the `widthMultiple` rule;
  - custom-format JSON: the schema from Task 3b, kept and checked against the code;
  - frames and the store: views, fidelity, `acquire`/`acquireOriginal`, `resume_on`, budgets and `trim`, converter stats;
  - sources: URIs of the three providers with their parameters, the confidence table, `impl=`, the resolved URI;
  - how to add a format: one catalogue line, one layout golden, the round-trip and GPU matrix pick it up;
  - linking: `dvv_link_components(<exe> dvv::media_sources)`.
- **dvv-platform:** a `FileReader` section with the open flags (Windows `FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED` + `FILE_FLAG_NO_BUFFERING`; Linux `O_DIRECT`), the probe at open and the buffered fallback, `alignment()`, thread safety, and `allocateAligned`. Check and complete it (Task 4 wrote the first version).
- **dvv-gpu:** an "Images in shaders" section: STORAGE images and `view()`, `ComputeBindings`, the dispatch overload, the `format("unknown")` declaration, the three new required features. Check and complete it (Task 5).
- **dvv-registry:** a "Kind check" section: `dvv::InterfaceKind<I>`, where to specialise it, and the unavailable reason text.
- **rawreel:** a "rawreel-cli" section: the commands and options of §4.6, the `--via` meaning, the exit-code table, the JSON forms (keys of `info`, `dump --json` and `bench --json`), and a worked example (`dump --count` then `info` on the result).

**Tests first:** this task has no new code. Its checks are Steps 4–7 below (the Windows workflows green with GPU tests run, the Linux sanity build, lint clean) and Step 8 (the exit criteria).

**Checkpoint task (U-PROC):** Task 15 is the milestone's final checkpoint and covers everything: Debug (its Per-task checks), Release and ASan for every repository through the workspace workflows, the ASan ×20 stress of Tasks 7a and 7b, clang-tidy on all sources of every C++ repository after its `ninja-clang` build, the workspace Linux sanity build, and the privacy lint on all seven repositories. Its docs commits change no C++ file, so its Per-task clang-format is covered by Step 6.

**Steps:**
- [ ] **Step 1: edit the spec as listed.** Then check that no stale term is left:

```powershell
Select-String -Path rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-rawreel-design.md -Pattern 'IAsyncFileReader|bindless descriptor heap|images in ResourcePool\): M2|\[DEFER'
```

  Expected: no line, except a bindless mention that is explicitly labelled "F12 / v1.x".
- [ ] **Step 2: write and complete the five READMEs.** Each command or URI in a README must run as written. Check the CLI examples by running them against the Release build of Task 14 Step 5, using a scratch folder under `$env:TEMP`. Define `$cli` in the same call with Task 14 Step 5's first line: `$cli = (Get-ChildItem -Recurse _build/rawreel/vs2026 -Filter rawreel-cli.exe | Where-Object FullName -Match '\\Release\\' | Select-Object -First 1).FullName`.
- [ ] **Step 3: commit the documentation per repository.** Use `docs: …` messages with the trailer: one commit each in rawreel-workspace (spec, README), dvv-media, dvv-platform, dvv-gpu, dvv-registry and rawreel. For example:

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

Run vs2026 (Debug and Release) and vs2026-asan as two separate background calls (`run_in_background`), each with the GPU env:

```powershell
$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel-workspace; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026; Pop-Location
```

$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel-workspace; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan; Pop-Location
```

  After the ASan workflow, the ASan ×20 stress of the risky Tasks 7a and 7b (final checkpoint: every risky task of the milestone), in the background:

```powershell
$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel-workspace; ctest --preset vs2026-asan -R "^dvv_media\.unit$" --repeat until-fail:20 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 5; Pop-Location
```

Check with `ctest --test-dir _build/rawreel-workspace/vs2026 -C Release -L gpu -N` (it lists them) and the test logs. dvv-base has no M2 change; the workspace build covers it.
- [ ] **Step 5: the milestone's Linux sanity build (`rawreel-workspace`, `ninja-clang`, no tests).** This is the milestone-end workspace Linux sanity build (decisions U-WIN, U-PROC), next to the per-repository checkpoint builds of Step 6. It configures and builds every repository, including the Linux-only code (POSIX `FileReader`, the GTK/X11 paths of M1b), as one background call in parallel with Step 4:

```powershell
wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc 'set -o pipefail && . _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd rawreel-workspace && cmake --preset ninja-clang > /dev/null && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 40 && echo LINUX-BUILD-OK'
```

  Expected: `LINUX-BUILD-OK`. No test runs (no ctest, no lavapipe, no ASan/TSan presets). A compile error in our code is a portability bug: fix it in its repository with a `fix:` commit (trailer, privacy lint), rerun that repository's Per-task checks and its Step 6 lint, then repeat this step. A failure of the toolchain or of a third-party package is not fixed: record it in the `## Changes during execution` section of `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m2-media.md` and tell the controller in the task report.
- [ ] **Step 6: checkpoint lint of every repository with code changes: full clang-format and clang-tidy on all sources, after the repository's `ninja-clang` configure + build with no tests** (the same file lists as the CI tidy step of `rawreel-workspace/.github/workflows/dvv-ci.yml`), for each `<repo>` of dvv-registry, dvv-platform, dvv-gpu, dvv-media and rawreel, in the background:

```powershell
wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc 'set -o pipefail && . _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd <repo> && cmake --preset ninja-clang >/dev/null && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 30 && git ls-files -z "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 8 clang-format --dry-run --Werror && git ls-files -z ":(glob)src/**/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/*.cpp" ":(glob)tools/*.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_win32.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_msw.cpp" | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 1 clang-tidy -p ../_build/<repo>/ninja-clang --quiet && echo LINT-OK'
```

  Expected: `LINT-OK` for each. A build error in our code is fixed (a `fix:` commit, then that repository's Per-task checks and this step again); a toolchain or third-party failure is recorded in `## Changes during execution` and reported.

foreach ($r in 'rawreel-workspace','dvv-base','dvv-registry','dvv-platform','dvv-gpu','dvv-media','rawreel') { cmake -DREPO=$r -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake }

## U-WIN changes

Decision U-WIN (2026-10-23, binding): v1.0 is Windows only; the code stays portable; nothing is tested
on Linux. The step numbers in "Arbiter fixes applied" above refer to the plan before these changes.

- **Global Constraints:** new items "Platform scope" (portable code, Linux paths compiled but not run),
  "Per-task full matrix" (vs2026 and vs2026-asan with GPU tests on NVIDIA, clang-format, clang-tidy,
  privacy lint), "Linux sanity build" (one WSL `ninja-clang` configure + build, no tests, only in Task 15
  and in a task that changes Linux-only code: Task 4) and "Risk accepted" (no TSan or UBSan on Windows).
  Toolchains: `ninja-clang` only, for clang-tidy and the sanity build. Shells: WSL only for lint and the
  sanity build; the WSLg/xvfb lines are gone. GPU tests: the WSL variable line is gone. Long runs:
  Windows workflows and the WSL lint build run in parallel.
- **Conventions:** WSL runs no tests. New "Full matrix for `<repo>`" line. The inner loop's full
  workflows are vs2026 and vs2026-asan. The Conventions lint now configures and builds `ninja-clang`
  first (clang-tidy needs the compile database and generated headers, as in the CI tidy job); that build
  runs no tests; our compile errors are fixed, toolchain or third-party failures are recorded.
- **Task 0:** the lavapipe device check (old Step 4) is cut (future.md); R1/R2 are decided on NVIDIA
  alone. Steps renumbered (old 5 -> 4, old 6 -> 5); Step 4 checks only the lint tools (no xvfb); the
  record block and the commit message drop lavapipe; the Ubuntu `vulkaninfo` note and the lavapipe facts
  are removed.
- **Task 1:** the WSL workflow call is removed from Step 4; Step 5's lint runs in the background (it
  builds the new `ninja-clang` tree). `ci.yml` adds no Linux job of its own. Expected results: vs2026
  and vs2026-asan.
- **Tasks 2, 3, 3b:** Step 4 is Windows only. TSan checks of Task 2 test 10 and Task 3b test 9 become
  ASan plus review; Task 3's UBSan shift check becomes a review check. Expected results name vs2026 and
  vs2026-asan.
- **Task 4:** Step 4 runs no Linux unit tests; it is the Linux sanity build of dvv-platform (Linux-only
  `file_reader_posix.cpp`), expecting `LINUX-BUILD-OK`. Step 6 drops the WSL presets. Case 13 is
  compiled, not run. A note says the POSIX path is reviewed and its tests are v1.x.
- **Task 5:** the lavapipe run (old Step 5) is removed; Steps renumbered (old 6 -> 5, 7 -> 6, 8 -> 7);
  the matrix step is Windows + lint; validation is checked on NVIDIA.
- **Task 6:** the goal and expected results name NVIDIA only; the lavapipe run (old Step 5) is removed;
  Steps renumbered (old 6 -> 5, 7 -> 6); `tests/tsan.supp` is kept as a portable build file; the
  lavapipe-mismatch note becomes a review rule for shader shifts.
- **Task 7a:** Step 4 (TSan x20 in WSL) is folded into the Windows path as an ASan stress x20
  (`ctest --preset vs2026-asan --repeat until-fail:20`) plus a reviewer check of the lock rules; Step 5
  drops the WSL presets; test 12 and the race note are reworded.
- **Task 7b:** Step 5 is the same ASan stress x20 (its own command); Step 6 spells out its matrix
  instead of pointing at Task 7a.
- **Task 8:** Step 5 is the Windows workflows only (it was the shared "Task 8 Steps 5 and 6" helper of
  Tasks 10-12); the lint may run in parallel; GPU cases run on NVIDIA; the lavapipe speed risk becomes
  a "keep sizes small" note.
- **Task 9:** Step 3 drops the three WSL presets of dvv-registry; Step 7 spells out its matrix instead of
  pointing at Task 8 Step 5.
- **Tasks 10, 11, 12:** "run ASan and the WSL presets" (Task 8 Steps 5 and 6) becomes a self-contained
  Windows matrix + lint step; Task 11's WSL temp-file rule becomes a portable note.
- **Task 13:** Step 6 is the Windows matrix only; Step 7's lint may run in parallel and compiles the
  Linux `main` entry; the e2e GPU test runs on NVIDIA; the `run-cli-gpu.cmake` comment no longer names
  lavapipe.
- **Task 14:** Step 3 spells out its matrix instead of pointing at Task 13 Step 6; the lavapipe bench
  (old Step 6) is cut (future.md) and the bench-file cleanup moves to Step 5; Steps renumbered (old 7 ->
  6, 8 -> 7); the record, the commit message and the expected results drop lavapipe; the spec §16 text
  says the Linux `O_DIRECT` path is not yet tested.
- **Task 15:** old Step 5 (WSL matrix on lavapipe) becomes the milestone's one Linux sanity build of
  `rawreel-workspace` (`ninja-clang` configure + build, no tests; our portability bugs fixed, toolchain
  or third-party failures recorded). Step 8 and the expected results take the exit criteria from the
  Windows logs only and quote `LINUX-BUILD-OK`; the spec §15 text names Windows-only testing and the
  U-WIN deferrals; the ledger gets the sanity build result.
- **Cut to v1.x (future.md):** the lavapipe device check (Task 0), the lavapipe GPU runs (Tasks 5, 6, 8
  and the e2e tests), the Linux FileReader test run (Task 4), the TSan x20 store stress (Tasks 7a, 7b)
  and the lavapipe bench (Task 14).

## U-PROC changes

User decisions U-PROC and U-PROC-tidy (decisions.md, 2026-10-23).
- **Global Constraints:** "Per-task full matrix" and the per-task Linux sanity build are replaced by "Per-task checks" (vs2026 Debug build + all tests with the GPU tests on NVIDIA, Debug concurrency repeats in a risky task, clang-format on the changed files, privacy lint), a "Checkpoints" list (Tasks 3, 5, 7b, 9, 13, 15) and "Checkpoint checks" (vs2026 Release, vs2026-asan with the ASan repeats of the risky tasks since the last checkpoint, clang-tidy on the files changed since the last checkpoint after one WSL `ninja-clang` configure + build that is also the Linux sanity build). Toolchains, Shells (read-only `git diff --name-only` in WSL) and Long runs follow; the workspace workflow groups now end at checkpoints (Tasks 9, 13, 15).
- **Conventions:** the "Full matrix for `<repo>`" line and the "Conventions lint" recipe are replaced by short "Per-task checks" and "Checkpoint checks" lines pointing at Global Constraints.
- **Every task:** the full-matrix step becomes the Per-task Windows Debug run (package test included), and the lint step becomes clang-format on the changed files (all files in the new dvv-media in Task 1). Expected results name vs2026 Debug and `FORMAT-OK`, and say which checkpoint runs Release, ASan and clang-tidy.
- **Task 4:** the per-task Linux sanity build of `file_reader_posix.cpp` is removed; the Task 5 checkpoint's dvv-platform `ninja-clang` build compiles it and case 13.
- **Tasks 7a, 7b (risky):** the ASan stress ×20 becomes a Debug stress ×20 (`ctest --preset vs2026 --repeat until-fail:20`); the ASan ×20 runs once at the Task 7b checkpoint and again at Task 15. The count stays 20, as these tasks had it.
- **Checkpoint tasks 3, 5, 7b, 9, 13:** a new last step "Checkpoint checks" with the full command lines and the repositories and `<cp-base>` it covers. Tasks 9 and 13 keep their workspace workflow (G1, G2) before it.
- **Task 14:** the bench builds Release for the measurement only (`cmake --build --preset vs2026-release`).
- **Task 15 (final checkpoint):** the workspace vs2026 (Debug and Release) and vs2026-asan workflows, the ASan ×20 stress, the full clang-format and clang-tidy of every C++ repository after its `ninja-clang` build, the workspace Linux sanity build and the privacy lint on all seven repositories.

## Changes during execution

Task 0 (device check, 2026-10-23):
- NVIDIA: an NVIDIA desktop GPU, apiVersion 1.4.x, maxPushDescriptors 32, pushDescriptor true,
  shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats true, shaderStorageImageWriteWithoutFormat true;
  R8_UINT/R16_UINT optimal: STORAGE_IMAGE, STORAGE_WRITE_WITHOUT_FORMAT (checked with the
  Show-StorageFormatBits parser on `vulkaninfo --show-formats`; R8_UINT and R16_UINT are in separate groups).
- R1: not needed. R2: not needed.
- Baseline workspace test count: 41 (`ctest -N -C Release`; `-C Debug` lists 42, because
  `workspace.wx_consumer` is registered for Debug only). Task 1 expects 43 with `-C Release`.
- WSL tools: cmake 4.3.2, clang-format 19.1.7, clang-tidy (LLVM) 19.1.0.

Task 5 (images in shaders, 2026-10-23):
- Slang spelling confirmed (R3): `[format("unknown")]` on `RWTexture2D<uint>` gives OpTypeImage
  Sampled 2, ImageFormat Unknown and the `StorageImageWriteWithoutFormat` capability with slangc
  2026.20; no `SLANGC_OPTIONS` fallback needed. Task 6 uses the same spelling.
- `gpu_test_images.cpp` case 1: `copyImageToBuffer` keeps an image that is in GENERAL in GENERAL
  (M1a behaviour), so the test moves the image to TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL with an explicit
  `transition` before the copy; the recorder is unchanged.

Task 14 (bench, 2026-10-24):
- Setup: an NVIDIA desktop GPU (the NVIDIA driver of the time), Windows 11, `vs2026-release`
  build, local NVMe SSD, K = 4, 60 frames of 3840x2160 yuv420p10le (24883200 bytes each, a
  1.49 GB file written by `dump --via cpu`), no validation layer. Reader: `unbuffered`.
- Three runs: 43.6, 46.4 and 48.2 frames/s. Median run (by fps): 46.4 frames/s, 1.16 GB/s end to
  end in 1.292 s. Its stages (GB/s per run, bytes / nanoseconds): Produce 0.46, HostToStaging 3.79,
  Upload 2.24, GpuUnpack 4.56. The other two runs agree in shape (Produce 0.47 and 0.60, Upload
  1.76 and 1.38).
- D18 outcome: M3 item: F1 (IOCP on Windows / io_uring) (bottleneck Produce at 0.46 GB/s). Upload
  (2.24 GB/s) is also below 3 GB/s; the rule names one fallback, the one of the bottleneck stage,
  so the transfer queue is not an M3 item. Task 15 adds F1 to the "Inherited by M3" list.
- `--help` now describes bench (Task 13's "not available yet" text is gone).

Task 15 (final verification, 2026-10-24):
- Carried fix (Task 13 review, Important): rawreel 3caef01 `test: catch every validation message in
  the CLI GPU e2e test`: the GPU and bench e2e scripts use dvv-gpu's pattern
  `Vulkan [a-z|-]+ (warning|error)` through `expect_no_validation` in `cli_common.cmake`, also on the
  probe runs. A sync-hazard line (`Vulkan validation error [SYNC-HAZARD-WRITE-AFTER-READ]: …`) now
  fails the check; the old `VUID|Validation Error` regex missed it.
- Workspace matrix (Windows, NVIDIA, `DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1`, sync validation required): `vs2026` Debug
  59/59 and Release 58/58, `vs2026-asan` 52/52, no `***Skipped`, no AddressSanitizer report; ASan ×20
  stress of `dvv_media.unit` passed 20 of 20. `dvv_media.gpu`: 13 cases, 9444 assertions, 0 skipped.
- Linux sanity build (workspace `ninja-clang`, configure + build, no tests): `LINUX-BUILD-OK`; no
  toolchain or third-party failure to record.
- Lint after each repository's `ninja-clang` build (full clang-format, clang-tidy on all sources):
  `LINT-OK` for dvv-registry, dvv-platform, dvv-gpu, dvv-media and rawreel.
- Spec: the M2 sections (§3.1, §4, §5.1–5.4, §6.2–6.5, §7.1–7.3, §9.4, §10.3, §10.4, §12, §15, §16,
  §17) describe what was built. The docs follow the code where it differs from the design: the
  planner is a bounded relaxation, not Dijkstra; eviction takes Derived, Lossless, then Exact; the
  confidence rule has no "explicit hint wins" (a Y4M file with explicit `fmt` and `size` is
  ambiguous). §16 also says that the stage rates are not link rates and which run is the median.
- Inherited by M3 (controller decisions from the Task 14 review): F1 with the caveat that the reads
  already overlap (M3 Task B measures `bench` at K = 1 and K = 8 first and builds IOCP only if K = 8
  beats K = 1); the E7 risk that Upload + GpuUnpack already take about 16.6 ms per 4K frame against a
  16.7 ms budget (checked by the M3 4K60 run, Task 11b, and its fallback Task P).
- Minor items logged during M2, for the final M2 review to triage (none blocks the exit criteria;
  "fixed" marks the ones already closed):
  - Task 1: `FrameKeyHash` spread test too weak (a degenerate hash passes; strengthened in Task 3,
    still weak per the Task 3 review); raw U+FFFD literal in `pathFromUtf8` (fixed, d21af02);
    `utf8FromPath` fallback untested (fixed, d21af02).
  - Task 2: `unpacked formats` and `geometry matches hand-computed sizes` test gaps.
  - Task 3: test 6 mismatch detail; no odd-width golden; the d21af02 commit message carries a raw
    replacement character.
  - Task 3b: weak substring checks in "malformed JSON values"; "formatToJson writes every key" uses
    spelling tables; README rules wording; `@planes` constant defined twice (`format_json.cpp`,
    `pixel_format.cpp`).
  - Task 4: the probe-refusal reopen path has no test (the seam replaces only the opener); `open()`
    can return `Internal` for a non-power-of-two sector size; glibc's fallback `STATX_DIOALIGN`
    definition (POSIX path); test case 3 depends on the volume's alignment.
  - Task 5: `createImage` makes a view for depth/stencil or multi-planar SAMPLED images (invalid
    view); the reflection binding check compares only `{space, index}`, not type or count; view and
    image retire in two calls (ordering not strictly guaranteed); two barrier paths untested
    (GENERAL kept by `copyImageToBuffer`, a mixed GENERAL/non-GENERAL dispatch); compile-fail case 5
    is weak.
  - Task 6: GPU test inputs always come from `pack()` (zero padding; `media::unpack` never called);
    the 16 GiB limit assumes an unsigned dword index; `recordUnpack` comment on another device's
    buffer; unparsable reflection JSON gives `Format`, not the documented `Internal`.
  - Task 7a: `HostFrame::setDataOffset` unguarded after publish (fixed, 81571d5); `REQUIRE` inside
    opener `jthread`s terminates the test process on a timeout; compile-fail cases do not prove that
    a `CreationKey<FrameStore>` cannot be forged; `frameKey<T>` records frame_store.h as the creation
    site in detailed lifetime mode.
  - Task 7b: stress `wrong_bytes` check; reader/pin asserts; eviction helpers that can throw inside
    fragile windows; a cached "no path" can hide a path from an existing entry when `maxPlanDepth`
    is small (order-dependent); "adopted root disables caching" untested (mutation survives);
    compile-fail case 3 patterns too broad; the brief's "original-format request never returns
    Derived" intent untested after the deviation; stress coverage of the 7b paths.
  - Task 8: HostToStaging's zeroed padding never reaches the GPU (Upload copies `layout.bytes`);
    `VkPlanesFrame` device-tier accounting undercounts image memory; no eviction test concurrent
    with in-flight GPU steps; the second CpuUnpack edge, the GPU-store stats contract, the
    unpadded-buffer error text and the `VkPlanesFrame` native view are untested; `require_exact`
    naming (spec: `requireExact`).
  - Task 9: `openWithImpl` test uses a hand-built `Uri`; some §5.5 branches untested (impl with
    confidence 0, …); an `impl` naming a disabled provider, or one that does not serve the scheme,
    is reported as "unknown source provider"; the conformance loop body was vacuous until Task 10.
  - Task 10: the in-range code-value check cannot fail; "index outside the source" checks only the
    code; fractional fps in the resolved URI (`fps=30000%2F1001`, later fixed by the dvv-base
    `formatUri` fix 2058db0); `match()` guesses with plain `findFormat`.
  - Task 11: `parseFilename` tries the whole name first; the raw resolved URI drops `impl=raw`
    (y4m keeps `impl=y4m`); the custom-JSON reopen test.
  - Task 12: duplicate URI parameter names are not rejected (`checkParameters`); the 4096-byte
    header limit is not really tested; an empty Y4M tag is a hard `Format` error; the unaligned-offset
    test does not assert the unbuffered path.
  - Task 13: the GPU e2e check only matched "VUID|Validation Error" (Important; fixed in Task 15,
    3caef01); the GPU leg asserts "> 0" GPU runs, not 3 of 3 (`Readback == 3`, `CpuUnpack == 0`);
    CPU-vs-GPU e2e coverage is 4 formats at one size (no 8-bit planar, packed 8-bit 4:2:2 or gray);
    a value-taking option swallows a following option (`dump u --out --json`).
  - Task 14: §16 wording (stage rates are not link rates, "median run" — both fixed in Task 15);
    "local NVMe" sits behind a RAID-mode storage driver; no warm-up batch (run 1 slowest);
    `frameBytesOf` duplicates `output.cpp`'s `frameBytes`.
  - Task 15 (found while writing the READMEs):
    - `bench` on a synthetic URI without `frames=` exits 1, because the default `--frames 60` exceeds
      the synthetic default of 10 frames;
    - a frame-range error prints as `error: Format: …` in text but `Arguments` in JSON;
    - text errors carry a source location such as `[commands.cpp:208]` (`describe()`);
    - `--help` says exit 4 is "no usable GPU device for --via gpu", but `bench` exits 4 too, and it
      does not mention `bench --frames` above the frame count (exit 1);
    - a Y4M file opened with explicit `fmt` and `size` is ambiguous (y4m 100, raw 95), so an
      explicit hint does not win as the old spec §7.2 said (spec now states the real rule);
    - a raw file with only a size never opens without `fmt=` or `impl=raw` (30 < 60);
    - a relative `fmt=@<path>` is relative to the working directory (1 MiB limit);
    - adding a catalogue format also needs `CATALOGUE_SIZE` and several test tables (documented in
      the dvv-media README);
    - the M2 design is not committed with the docs (the M1b final review made the same point for
      the M1b design); spec §17 cites the decisions in short form only.
